AutoDex: An Automated Real-World System for Dexterous Grasping Data Collection
Authors: Mingi Choi, Gunhee Kim, Jisoo Kim, Taeksoo Kim, Taeyun Ha, Jongbin Lim, Hanbyul Joo
First: 2026-06-22T17:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:59:55+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 9 figures. Includes supplementary material
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning robust dexterous grasping requires real-world data that records the physical outcomes of grasp attempts. Such data is hard to obtain at scale: teleoperation yields valid physical outcomes but is slow and operator-biased, while simulation-based generation is cheap and scalable but cannot certify contact validity. A natural solution is to generate candidate grasps and verify them on real hardware, but this scales only if the entire collection loop (perception, execution, labeling, and reset) runs without human intervention. We present AutoDex, an automated real-world data-collection system that closes this loop: for each candidate from a replaceable generator, it localizes the object under severe hand-object occlusion with dense 20-camera perception, executes collision-monitored robot motions, labels lift-and-hold success or failure, and actively resets the object between trials to expose additional candidates across stable poses. The result is a reusable database of physically labeled grasp trials that downstream systems can query by retrieval and feasibility filtering. Using AutoDex, we collect 3,593 grasp trials across Allegro and Inspire hands on 100 diverse objects, with synchronized multi-view observations and robot-state logs. For a matched 500-trajectory collection, AutoDex requires 10.3 h versus 49.4 h for teleoperation, yielding a 4.8x throughput improvement, and grasps retrieved from the AutoDex-validated database succeed 76% versus 34% for simulation-only validation. Code and data will be publicly released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AutoDex：一种用于灵巧抓取数据收集的自动化真实世界系统
学习鲁棒的灵巧抓取需要记录抓取尝试物理结果的真实世界数据。此类数据大规模获取困难：遥操作虽能产生有效物理结果，但速度慢且受操作员偏见影响；而基于模拟的生成虽廉价可扩展，却无法保证接触有效性。自然的解决方案是生成候选抓取方案并在真实硬件上验证，但该方案仅在感知、执行、标注和重置的整个收集循环无需人工干预时才能规模化。我们提出AutoDex，一种自动化的真实世界数据收集系统，闭合该循环：对每个可替换生成器产生的候选方案，它在严重手-物体遮挡下使用密集20摄像头感知进行物体定位，执行碰撞监控的机器人运动，标注抓取成功或失败，并在试验间主动重置物体以在稳定姿态下暴露更多候选方案。结果是一个可复用的物理标注抓取试验数据库，下游系统可通过检索和可行性过滤查询。使用AutoDex，我们在100个多样化物体上针对Allegro和Inspire双手收集了3,593次抓取试验，包含同步多视角观测和机器人状态日志。与匹配的500轨迹遥操作相比，AutoDex仅需10.3小时，而遥操作需49.4小时，吞吐量提升4.8倍；从AutoDex验证数据库检索的抓取方案成功率76%，而仅模拟验证仅为34%。代码和数据将公开发布。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of acquiring scalable, physically validated dexterous grasping data, AutoDex automates the entire data collection pipeline from perception to reset, enabling real-world validation without human intervention. It uses a replaceable grasp generator, dense multi-camera perception to localize objects under occlusion, collision-monitored robot execution, automated success/failure labeling, and active object resetting to explore diverse poses. Experimental results show AutoDex collects 3,593 grasp trials across 100 objects using Allegro and Inspire hands in 10.3 hours — 4.8x faster than teleoperation — and grasps retrieved from its validated database succeed at 76% compared to 34% for simulation-only methods.
为解决高精度抓取数据难以规模化获取的问题，AutoDex 自动化了从候选抓取生成、多视角视觉定位、碰撞监控执行、成功/失败标注到主动重置物体的全流程。实验表明，AutoDex 在100个物体上收集了3593次抓取试验，比人工遥操作效率提升4.8倍（10.3小时 vs 49.4小时），且其验证数据库中抓取成功率高达76%，远超仅靠仿真验证的34%。
Lift4D: Harmonizing Single-View 3D Estimation for 4D Reconstruction In-the-Wild
Authors: Yehonathan Litman, Xiaoxuan Ma, Manan Shah, Nicolas Ugrinovic, Kris Kitani, Fernando De la Torre, Shubham Tulsiani
First: 2026-06-22T17:59:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:59:54+00:00
Comments: Webpage, Demos: https://lift4d.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Reconstructing dynamic non-rigid objects from monocular video requires integrating visual cues from direct observations with data-driven priors over geometry and appearance. Prior approaches either learn to directly predict 4D representations from visual input or initialize a 3D representation that is subsequently deformed and refined based on video evidence. However, the former are constrained by the scarcity of 4D training data, while the latter leverage priors only for the initial reconstruction and rely solely on video supervision thereafter; neither handles complex in-the-wild scenarios with large deformations and occlusions well. We present Lift4D, a test-time optimization framework that addresses both limitations. First, we adapt an existing single-view 3D reconstruction model to yield temporally consistent per-frame predictions via causal latent conditioning, providing a coherent initialization for a deformable 3D Gaussian Splatting representation. We then ``sculpt'' this representation to match the input video through an occlusion-aware optimization that faithfully recovers visible surface details while completing unobserved regions using a view-conditioned diffusion prior. We demonstrate that Lift4D clearly improves over prior 4D reconstruction methods, particularly on challenging in-the-wild sequences with severe occlusions and non-rigid motion.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Lift4D：统一单目3D估计以实现野外4D重建
从单目视频重建动态非刚性物体，需要将直接观测的视觉线索与几何和外观的数据驱动先验相结合。先前方法要么直接从视觉输入预测4D表示，要么初始化3D表示并基于视频证据进行变形和优化。前者受限于4D训练数据稀缺，后者仅在初始化阶段利用先验，之后完全依赖视频监督；两者均难以处理野外场景中大变形和遮挡的复杂情况。我们提出Lift4D，一种测试时优化框架，解决了上述两个限制。首先，我们调整现有单目3D重建模型，通过因果潜在条件化生成时序一致的逐帧预测，为可变形3D高斯点云表示提供连贯初始化。然后，我们通过遮挡感知优化“雕刻”该表示，使其匹配输入视频，忠实恢复可见表面细节，同时利用视图条件扩散先验完成未观测区域。实验证明，Lift4D显著优于现有4D重建方法，尤其在遮挡严重和非刚性运动的挑战性野外序列上表现突出。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing 4D reconstruction methods—either data-starved or insufficiently adaptive to complex real-world deformations and occlusions—the authors propose Lift4D, a test-time optimization framework. It first adapts a single-view 3D model using causal latent conditioning to generate temporally consistent per-frame predictions, initializing a deformable 3D Gaussian Splatting representation. Then, it refines this representation via an occlusion-aware optimization that leverages a view-conditioned diffusion prior to recover visible surface details and complete occluded regions. Experiments show Lift4D significantly outperforms prior methods, especially on challenging in-the-wild sequences with severe occlusions and non-rigid motion.
为解决现有4D重建方法在数据稀缺或处理复杂真实场景（如严重遮挡与非刚性形变）时的不足，Lift4D提出一种测试时优化框架。首先，通过因果潜在条件化使单视角3D模型生成时序一致的逐帧预测，初始化可变形的3D高斯点云表示；随后，通过遮挡感知优化结合视图条件扩散先验，精确恢复可见表面细节并填补未观测区域。实验表明，Lift4D在具有严重遮挡与非刚性运动的野外挑战序列上显著优于现有方法。
Randomized YaRN Improves Length Generalization for Long-Context Reasoning
Authors: Manas Mehta, Fangcong Yin, Greg Durrett
First: 2026-06-22T17:59:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:59:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are typically pretrained on short sequences and then extended to work on longer sequences with additional training. However, such LLMs still struggle to further generalize to very long sequences. We propose Randomized YaRN, a training method that improves length generalization by combining YaRN-based positional extrapolation with randomized positional encoding and a length curriculum. During training on short context data, tokens are assigned YaRN positional encodings sampled from a larger position range, exposing the model to out-of-distribution positional representations even on short-context inputs. We evaluate Randomized YaRN on two challenging long-context reasoning benchmarks, BABILong and Multi-Round Coreference Resolution (MRCR). When training on data with <8K context, Randomized YaRN consistently improves reasoning performance on context lengths from 16K to 128K and outperforms standard fine-tuning, with the largest gains appearing at far out-of-distribution lengths. Our results suggest that progressively exposing models to OOD positional distributions provides an effective recipe for generalizable long-context reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：随机化YaRN提升长上下文推理的长度泛化能力
大型语言模型（LLMs）通常在短序列上预训练，再通过额外训练扩展至长序列。然而，此类模型仍难以进一步泛化至极长序列。我们提出随机化YaRN训练方法，通过结合基于YaRN的位置外推、随机位置编码及长度课程学习，提升长度泛化能力。在短上下文数据训练时，令牌被分配从更大位置范围采样的YaRN位置编码，即使在短上下文输入中也能使模型接触分布外的位置表示。我们在两个具有挑战性的长上下文推理基准（BABILong和多轮指代消解MRCR）上评估随机化YaRN。当训练数据上下文长度小于8K时，随机化YaRN在16K至128K上下文长度上持续提升推理性能，优于标准微调，尤其在远分布外长度上收益最大。结果表明，逐步使模型接触分布外位置分布是实现可泛化长上下文推理的有效方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limited generalization of large language models to very long sequences despite fine-tuning, the authors propose Randomized YaRN, a method that combines YaRN positional extrapolation with randomized positional encoding and a length curriculum. During training on short-context data, tokens receive positional encodings sampled from a broader range, exposing the model to out-of-distribution positional representations. Evaluated on BABILong and MRCR, Randomized YaRN consistently improves reasoning performance on long contexts (16K–128K tokens) when trained on <8K context data, outperforming standard fine-tuning, with the most significant gains observed at extreme lengths.
为了解决大语言模型在长序列推理中泛化能力不足的问题，作者提出了Randomized YaRN方法，结合YaRN位置外推、随机位置编码和长度课程训练。训练时，短上下文数据中的token被赋予从更广范围采样的位置编码，使模型在短上下文阶段即接触分布外的位置表示。在BABILong和MRCR两个长上下文推理基准上，该方法在训练于<8K上下文数据时，持续提升16K至128K长上下文的推理性能，优于标准微调，尤其在极端长度外推时效果最显著。
LIBERO-Safety: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Physical and Semantic Safety in Vision-Language-Action Models
Authors: Rongxu Cui, Zongzheng Zhang, Jingrui Pang, Haohan Chi, Jinbang Guo, Saining Zhang, Shaoxuan Xie, Xin Jin, Yao Mu, Jiaolong Yang, Guocai Yao, Xianyuan Zhan, Ya-Qin Zhang, Hao Zhao
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-22T17:59:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:59:53+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ECCV 2026, Project Page: https://libero-safety.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Despite the impressive manipulation capabilities of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, their operational safety under strict constraints remains largely unverified. To address this, we introduce a parametric safety benchmark to procedurally generate safety-critical scenarios with comprehensive stochasticity. To overcome the scalability bottlenecks of human teleoperation, we develop a novel keypose-driven data generation pipeline. Leveraging this infrastructure, we curate a large-scale dataset of 19,664 strictly collision-free demonstrations with extensive domain randomization. We then conduct a systematic cross-paradigm evaluation of eight VLA and two embodied foundation models. Our analysis reveals a critical generalization-safety tension: although high-diversity training fosters safer trajectories, task success remains fundamentally bottlenecked by sub-optimal trajectory synthesis and semantic misalignment. By providing a scalable pipeline, a robust dataset, and profound failure-mode insights, LIBERO-Safety establishes a crucial foundation for developing safe and reliable VLA models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LIBERO-Safety：视觉-语言-动作模型物理与语义安全的综合基准
尽管视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型具备令人印象深刻的操控能力，但其在严格约束下的运行安全性仍缺乏充分验证。为此，我们提出一个参数化安全基准，可程序化生成具有全面随机性的安全关键场景。为克服人类遥操作的可扩展性瓶颈，我们开发了一种新颖的关键姿态驱动数据生成流程。基于此基础设施，我们构建了一个包含19,664个严格无碰撞演示的大规模数据集，并进行了广泛的领域随机化。随后，我们对八种VLA模型和两种具身基础模型进行了系统性的跨范式评估。分析表明，存在关键的泛化-安全性张力：虽然高多样性训练有助于生成更安全的轨迹，但任务成功率仍受次优轨迹合成和语义错位的根本性制约。通过提供可扩展的流程、稳健的数据集和深刻的失败模式洞察，LIBERO-Safety为开发安全可靠的VLA模型奠定了关键基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of systematic safety verification for Vision-Language-Action models under strict operational constraints, the authors introduce LIBERO-Safety, a parametric benchmark that procedurally generates safety-critical scenarios with high stochasticity. To scale data collection beyond human teleoperation, they develop a keypose-driven data generation pipeline, resulting in a large-scale dataset of 19,664 collision-free demonstrations with extensive domain randomization. Evaluating eight VLA models and two embodied foundation models, they find a generalization-safety trade-off: while diverse training improves safety, task success is limited by sub-optimal trajectory synthesis and semantic misalignment, highlighting critical failure modes for future model development.
为解决视觉-语言-动作模型在严格约束下安全性能缺乏系统验证的问题，作者提出了LIBERO-Safety基准，通过参数化方法生成具有随机性的安全关键场景。为突破人工远程操作的可扩展性瓶颈，他们设计了基于关键姿态的数据生成管道，构建了包含19,664个无碰撞演示的大型数据集，并进行领域随机化。对八种VLA模型和两种具身基础模型的系统评估显示，高多样性训练虽提升安全性，但任务成功率受限于轨迹合成不佳与语义对齐错误，揭示了安全与性能之间的根本性张力。
LaST-HD: Learning Latent Physical Reasoning from Scalable Human Data for Robot Manipulation
Authors: Jiaming Liu, Yinxi Wang, Chenyang Gu, Siyuan Qian, Xiangju Mi, Hao Chen, Jiawei Chen, Qingpo Wuwu, Xiaoqi Li, Nuowei Han, Yiming Zhang, Xuheng Zhang, Yang Yue, Yeqing Yang, Lei Wang, Peng Jia, Hao Tang, Shanghang Zhang
First: 2026-06-22T17:59:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:59:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human-hand demonstrations provide a direct and scalable source of physical interaction data for robot learning. While manual retargeting is indispensable for establishing kinematic action correspondence across different morphologies, robust transfer requires going beyond geometry to address the underlying alignment of physical dynamics between human and robot manipulation. To address this, we introduce LaST-HD, a novel human-to-robot action learning paradigm that extends reasoning-before-acting VLA by aligning human-hand and robot demonstrations in a shared latent reasoning space. Rather than mimicking human kinematics, LaST-HD trains an auxiliary action-conditioned world model on unpaired human-hand and robot trajectories to synthesize unified latent targets. After aligning cross-embodiment representations in this shared forward-dynamics space, these targets supervise LaST-HD's latent reasoning process, enabling it to internalize shared physical dynamics and drive efficient human-hand action learning. Moreover, we develop Out-of-Lab (OOL) Glove, a low-cost motion-capture glove tailored to LaST-HD for human-hand data collection. The captured human data provide precise keypoints and serve as universal action supervision across grippers and dexterous hands. Armed with the aligned latent space and high-fidelity human-hand data, we develop a progressive mixed-to-human training recipe comprising mixed human-robot co-training and human-hand online correction post-training. Through mixed co-training, LaST-HD improves generalization to novel objects, scenes, and positions using only human-hand demonstrations. With online correction, LaST-HD further adapts to novel environments and achieves over 90\% accuracy using only 20 minutes of OOL glove data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LaST-HD：从可扩展的人类数据中学习潜在物理推理以实现机器人操作
人类手部演示为机器人学习提供了直接且可扩展的物理交互数据来源。虽然手动重定向对于在不同形态间建立运动学动作对应关系至关重要，但稳健的迁移需要超越几何结构，解决人类与机器人操作间底层物理动力学的对齐问题。为此，我们提出了LaST-HD，一种新颖的人类到机器人动作学习范式，它通过在共享的潜在推理空间中对齐人类手部与机器人演示，扩展了“先推理后行动”的VLA方法。LaST-HD不模仿人类运动学，而是训练一个辅助的动作条件世界模型，利用未配对的人类手部与机器人轨迹合成统一的潜在目标。在该共享前向动力学空间中对齐跨形态表示后，这些目标监督LaST-HD的潜在推理过程，使其内化共享的物理动力学并驱动高效的双手动作学习。此外，我们开发了低成本的“实验室外（OOL）手套”，专为LaST-HD设计用于采集人类手部数据。所采集的人类数据提供精确关键点，并作为跨夹爪与灵巧手的通用动作监督。借助对齐的潜在空间和高保真人类手部数据，我们设计了一种渐进式混合到人类训练方案，包括混合人类-机器人协同训练和训练后的人类手部在线修正。通过混合协同训练，LaST-HD仅使用人类手部演示即可提升对新物体、场景和位置的泛化能力。通过在线修正，LaST-HD进一步适应新环境，并仅使用20分钟的OOL手套数据即实现超过90%的准确率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to transfer human-hand manipulation skills to robots while preserving physical dynamics, LaST-HD introduces a latent-space alignment method that bridges human and robot demonstrations via a shared forward-dynamics world model. Instead of copying kinematics, it trains an auxiliary model on unpaired trajectories to generate unified latent targets, which guide the robot’s reasoning process to internalize shared physical behaviors. Experimental results show that this approach enables efficient learning from human demonstrations, achieving over 90% accuracy on novel tasks after just 20 minutes of low-cost motion-capture data using the OOL Glove, and significantly improves generalization across objects, scenes, and positions through mixed co-training and online correction.
为实现人类手部操作技能向机器人有效迁移，LaST-HD提出一种在共享动力学空间中对齐人类与机器人演示的隐式推理框架。该方法通过训练辅助世界模型生成统一的隐式目标，引导机器人学习共享物理行为。实验表明，仅用20分钟采集的人类数据，LaST-HD即可在新任务中达到90%以上准确率，并通过混合协同训练和在线修正显著提升对新物体、场景和位置的泛化能力。
Keep The Essentials: Efficient Reference Conditioned Generation via Token Dropping
Authors: Rishubh Parihar, Ayush Raina, R. Venkatesh Babu, Or Patashnik
First: 2026-06-22T17:59:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:59:28+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://sparsecontext.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Reference-based diffusion models enable highly controllable image generation by leveraging elements from input images to guide prompt-driven synthesis. However, these models are computationally expensive in runtime, and their cost scales severely with the number of input references. While the efficiency of diffusion models has been extensively studied in the context of prompt-driven generation, it remains largely under-explored in the realm of reference-based models. This setting presents unique challenges not addressed by methods focusing solely on generation. In particular, the wasteful representation of references as dense token grids offers significant opportunities for improvement. In this work, we present Sparse Context, a method for constructing sparse reference representations by retaining only a reduced subset of reference tokens. We observe that even without modifying the model, dropping a significant portion of reference tokens at inference time largely preserves its generation capabilities. To fully realize this potential, we fine-tune the model with random token dropping at varying ratios, encouraging robustness to partial reference representations. Crucially, this training strategy decouples the model from any specific token selection rule, allowing flexible control at inference time. At inference time, instead of random dropping, we apply task-aware token selection strategies that prioritize the most informative regions of the reference images, adapting the token budget to the input and task requirements. Extensive experiments show our method achieves a 4x increase in inference speed for multi-reference generation and an 2x for single reference generation. Importantly, this efficiency is achieved without compromising visual quality across both spatially-aligned editing and subject-driven generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：保留核心要素：通过令牌删除实现高效的参考条件生成
基于参考的扩散模型通过利用输入图像中的元素引导提示驱动合成，实现了高度可控的图像生成。然而，这些模型在运行时计算成本高昂，且成本随输入参考数量急剧增加。尽管扩散模型在提示驱动生成中的效率已被广泛研究，但在基于参考的模型领域仍鲜有探索。该场景提出了仅关注生成的方法无法解决的独特挑战。特别是，将参考表示为密集令牌网格的方式存在显著改进空间。本文提出Sparse Context方法，通过仅保留参考令牌的缩减子集来构建稀疏参考表示。我们观察到，即使不修改模型，在推理时删除大量参考令牌仍能基本保持其生成能力。为充分释放这一潜力，我们通过在不同比例下随机删除令牌对模型进行微调，以增强其对部分参考表示的鲁棒性。关键的是，这种训练策略使模型与任何特定令牌选择规则解耦，从而在推理时提供灵活控制。在推理时，我们不再采用随机删除，而是应用任务感知的令牌选择策略，优先选择参考图像中最信息丰富的区域，并根据输入和任务需求调整令牌预算。大量实验表明，我们的方法在多参考生成中实现4倍推理速度提升，在单参考生成中实现2倍提升。重要的是，这种效率提升在空间对齐编辑和主体驱动生成中均未牺牲视觉质量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of reference-based diffusion models, especially as reference count increases, this work introduces Sparse Context, a method that selectively drops tokens from reference images to construct sparse representations without altering the model. By fine-tuning the model with random token dropping during training, it learns to generalize across partial reference inputs, enabling flexible, task-aware token selection at inference time. Experiments demonstrate a 4x speedup for multi-reference and 2x for single-reference generation, with no degradation in visual quality for both spatially-aligned editing and subject-driven synthesis.
为解决参考图像驱动扩散模型在多参考输入时计算成本急剧上升的问题，作者提出Sparse Context方法：在推理阶段通过丢弃参考图像中非关键的token，同时保留生成能力。通过在训练中引入随机token丢弃策略，使模型具备对任意token选择规则的泛化能力。推理时采用任务感知的token选择策略，优先保留信息量大的区域，动态适配输入与任务需求。实验表明，该方法在多参考生成中提速4倍、单参考生成提速2倍，且在空间对齐编辑与主体驱动生成任务中均未降低视觉质量。
CoorDex: Coordinating Body and Hand Priors for Continuous Dexterous Humanoid Loco-Manipulation
Authors: Sikai Li, Shuning Li, Zhenyu Wei, Yunchao Yao, Chenran Li, Mingyu Ding
First: 2026-06-22T17:59:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:59:20+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://skevinci.github.io/coordex/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Humanoid loco-manipulation is often simplified into a stop-and-go process: walking to an object, stopping to manipulate it, and then resuming locomotion. It also commonly relies on low degree-of-freedom (DoF) end effectors that behave like an open-close grasp primitive. We introduce CoorDex, a learning pipeline that converts high-dimensional body and dexterous hand control into coordinated latent residual control, enabling high-DoF dexterous loco-manipulation on the move. Starting from simulated whole-body and hand demonstrations, CoorDex trains privileged motion tracking teachers for the humanoid body and dexterous hand, distills them into proprioception-conditioned latent priors, and uses the frozen priors as the action space for downstream residual reinforcement learning. A coordinated latent residual policy composes these priors through shared task context and separate body-hand residual heads, preserving natural whole-body motion while improving finger-level contact reliability. CoorDex enables a Unitree G1 humanoid with a 20-DoF WUJI hand to execute dexterous manipulation while in motion, including non-stop bottle grasping and carrying, fridge door opening on the move, and cube pick-and-turn. Ablations on the walk-grasp-carry task show that joint-space PPO, joint-space hand control, and monolithic latent prediction all fail under the same reward budget, while the latent-prior interface and coordinated residual structure make high-dimensional contact-rich loco-manipulation trainable. Project Page: https://skevinci.github.io/coordex/
中文标题/摘要
标题：CoorDex：用于连续灵巧人形机器人运动-操作的躯干与手先验协调
人形机器人运动-操作常被简化为停顿式过程：行走至物体、停止操作、再恢复运动。其通常依赖低自由度（DoF）末端执行器，行为类似开合抓取。我们提出CoorDex，一种学习管道，将高维身体与灵巧手控制转化为协调的潜在残差控制，实现移动中高自由度灵巧运动-操作。从模拟的全身与手部演示出发，CoorDex训练特权运动跟踪教师，将其蒸馏为本体感觉条件化潜在先验，并将冻结先验作为下游残差强化学习的动作空间。协调潜在残差策略通过共享任务上下文与独立身体-手残差头组合这些先验，在保持自然全身运动的同时提升指级接触可靠性。CoorDex使配备20-DoF WUJI手的Unitree G1人形机器人能在移动中执行灵巧操作，包括不间断瓶抓取与搬运、移动中冰箱门开启、立方体抓取与旋转。在行走-抓取-搬运任务的消融实验表明，关节空间PPO、关节空间手控制及整体潜在预测均在相同奖励预算下失败，而潜在先验接口与协调残差结构使高维接触密集型运动-操作可训练。项目页面：https://skevinci.github.io/coordex/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of stop-and-go humanoid manipulation and the limitations of low-DoF end effectors, CoorDex introduces a learning pipeline that coordinates high-dimensional body and dexterous hand control via latent residual policies. It trains proprioception-conditioned latent priors from simulated demonstrations and uses them as a frozen action space for residual reinforcement learning, with separate body and hand residual heads sharing task context. Experiments on a Unitree G1 humanoid with a 20-DoF hand demonstrate successful continuous dexterous manipulation—such as bottle grasping, fridge door opening, and cube pick-and-turn—while moving. Ablations confirm that joint-space PPO, monolithic latent prediction, and joint-space hand control fail under the same reward budget, whereas the latent-prior interface and coordinated residual structure enable trainable, contact-rich loco-manipulation.
为解决人形机器人在移动中执行精细操作时频繁停顿和低自由度末端执行器的局限性，CoorDex提出一种通过潜在残差策略协调身体与灵巧手控制的学习框架。该方法从仿真演示中训练基于本体感觉的潜在先验，将其冻结为下游残差强化学习的动作空间，并通过共享任务上下文与独立的身体-手残差头保持自然运动同时提升指尖接触可靠性。实验表明，Unitree G1人形机器人搭载20自由度手在移动中可完成连续精细操作，如持续握瓶、开门、立方体抓取与旋转，消融实验显示关节空间方法与单体潜在预测在相同奖励预算下失败，验证了潜在先验与协调残差结构的有效性。
Semantic Browsing: Controllable Diversity for Image Generation
Authors: Sara Dorfman, Maya Vishnevsky, Omer Dahary, Or Patashnik, Daniel Cohen-Or
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-22T17:59:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:59:17+00:00
Comments: ECCV 2026. Project page: https://saradorfman1.github.io/SemanticBrowsing-webpage/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Modern text-to-image models excel in visual fidelity and prompt adherence. However, this strict adherence comes at the cost of diversity: generated samples tend to collapse into a single visual interpretation. Existing methods to improve diversity produce outputs driven by incidental variations rather than meaningful design choices. This motivates a new variant of the diversity task where structure is enforced on the generated samples. We introduce a method for controlled diversity that enables Semantic Browsing, where users can navigate structured image galleries and experience creative exploration through a systematic traversal of meaningful, interpretable axes of variation. Achieving this level of semantic control requires a deep understanding of the scene. We exploit the fact that recent text-to-image models are trained on elaborated captions, effectively decoupling semantic decision-making from pixel generation. This enables a paradigm shift: instead of relying on stochastic variation within the text-to-image model, we induce diversity directly at the text level. By leveraging rich textual representations, we allow a Vision Language Model (VLM) to operate on the full scene context. To overcome the generic outputs typical of standard VLMs, we employ an agentic workflow that explicitly enforces structured variation attuned to the original prompt. We demonstrate that our method produces diverse and navigable design spaces where every variation corresponds to a specific, user-understandable semantic decision.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语义浏览：图像生成中的可控多样性
现代文本到图像模型在视觉保真度和提示遵循方面表现优异，但这种严格遵循的代价是多样性不足：生成样本往往坍缩为单一视觉解释。现有提升多样性的方法产生的是偶然变化驱动的输出，而非有意义的设计选择。为此，我们提出一种新的多样性任务变体，通过结构化约束生成样本。我们引入一种可控多样性方法，实现语义浏览：用户可导航结构化图像画廊，通过系统性遍历有意义且可解释的变异轴，体验创意探索。实现这一语义控制水平需要对场景有深入理解。我们利用近期文本到图像模型在详尽描述语料上训练的特点，有效将语义决策与像素生成解耦。这带来范式转变：不再依赖文本到图像模型内部的随机变化，而是直接在文本层面诱导多样性。通过利用丰富的文本表示，我们使视觉语言模型（VLM）能操作完整场景上下文。为克服标准VLM通常产生的泛化输出，我们采用一种代理工作流，明确强制执行与原始提示相适应的结构化变异。实验证明，我们的方法生成多样且可导航的设计空间，每个变异都对应一个用户可理解的特定语义决策。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between visual fidelity and diversity in text-to-image models, this work introduces Semantic Browsing—a method for generating diverse, controllable image variations by structuring diversity at the text level rather than relying on stochastic generation. It leverages the semantic richness of elaborated captions and a Vision-Language Model (VLM) to interpret and manipulate scene context, enabling users to navigate structured, interpretable axes of variation. Experimental results show that this agentic workflow produces coherent, semantically meaningful, and navigable design spaces where each variation corresponds to a specific, user-understandable design choice, significantly improving diversity over prior methods that rely on incidental variation.
为解决文本到图像模型在视觉保真度与多样性之间的权衡问题，本文提出“语义浏览”方法，允许用户在结构化、语义明确的图像变化空间中导航。该方法不依赖随机噪声，而是在文本层面诱导多样性，利用丰富的文本表示和视觉语言模型（VLM）对完整场景上下文进行操作。通过一个主动工作流，强制执行与原始提示对齐的结构化变化，确保每种变化都对应一个清晰、可理解的语义决策。实验表明，该方法生成了多样且可导航的设计空间，用户可系统性探索有意义的美学与构图维度，同时不牺牲提示遵循性。
AIR: Adaptive Interleaved Reasoning with Code in MLLMs
Authors: Cong Han, Xiaohan Lan, Haibo Qiu, Yujie Zhong
First: 2026-06-22T17:58:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:58:54+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Following the paradigm shift initiated by OpenAI o3, interleaved reasoning with code to enhance multimodal large language models (MLLMs) has become a pivotal research frontier. The existing literature focuses primarily on tool-use within vision-perception tasks. However, such approaches typically rely on predefined heuristics for visual manipulation and are inherently incapable of addressing numerical computation problems due to their exclusive focus on visual operations. This paper empowers MLLMs with adaptive interleaved reasoning capabilities through extended reinforcement learning training on code-augmented complex numerical computation tasks. To this end, we propose a comprehensive three-component solution consisting of: a two-stage cold-start data construction pipeline, data filtering strategies for RL dataset curation, and an adaptive tool-invocation strategy leveraging a group-constrained reward function for interleaved reasoning trajectories. Extensive experiments demonstrate that after Reinforcement Learning training with the group-constrained reward function, performance improves by an average of 6.1 percentage points (pp) on evaluation benchmarks. Specifically, the accuracy for interleaved reasoning samples increases by 9.9 pp, and the overall success rate of tool-use exceeds 95%. Our data and code are available at: https://github.com/CongHan0808/AIR.git.
