Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance multimodal large language models (MLLMs) beyond vision-perception tasks, this paper introduces AIR, a method that enables adaptive interleaved reasoning with code through reinforcement learning. It addresses the limitation of existing tool-use approaches, which rely on fixed heuristics and cannot handle numerical computations. The method combines a two-stage data pipeline, RL dataset filtering, and a group-constrained reward function to guide adaptive tool invocation. Experimental results show a 6.1 percentage point average improvement on benchmarks, with 9.9 pp gain in interleaved reasoning accuracy and over 95% tool-use success rate.
为突破多模态大语言模型在视觉任务外的计算能力局限，本文提出AIR方法，通过强化学习训练使模型具备自适应代码交互推理能力。该方法包含三部分：两阶段冷启动数据构建、强化学习数据筛选策略，以及基于群体约束奖励函数的工具调用机制。实验表明，该方法在评估基准上平均提升6.1个百分点，其中交互推理样本准确率提升9.9个百分点，工具调用成功率超过95%。
Open Problem: Is AdamW Effective Under Heavy-Tailed Noise?
Authors: Dingzhi Yu, Hongyi Tao, Yuanyu Wan, Luo Luo, Lijun Zhang
First: 2026-06-22T17:58:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:58:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AdamW is the de facto optimizer for training large language models (LLMs), yet the theory behind it still lives mostly in finite-variance regimes. This is increasingly unsatisfying, as empirical evidence indicates that stochastic gradient noise in LLM pretraining is typically heavy-tailed. Recent work shows that sign-based optimizers such as Lion and Muon achieve sharp heavy-tailed rates, and that AdaGrad can also converge under heavy-tailed noise. However, no rigorous convergence theory for AdamW has yet been established in this regime. Can AdamW converge under the same heavy-tailed assumptions, or does its second-moment accumulator create a genuine obstruction? We formulate this as an open problem, prove a positive weighted-metric benchmark, and give a corridor lower-bound mechanism showing how denominator memory can hide large gradients.
中文标题/摘要
标题：开放问题：AdamW 在重尾噪声下是否有效？
AdamW 是训练大型语言模型（LLMs）的主流优化器，但其理论目前仍主要局限于有限方差情形。这越来越令人不满，因为实证证据表明 LLM 预训练中的随机梯度噪声通常具有重尾特性。近期研究表明，基于符号的优化器（如 Lion 和 Muon）可实现尖锐的重尾收敛速率，AdaGrad 也可在重尾噪声下收敛。然而，目前尚无针对 AdamW 在该情形下的严谨收敛理论。AdamW 是否能在相同重尾假设下收敛？其二阶矩累加器是否构成真正的障碍？我们将此问题形式化为开放问题，证明了一个正加权度量基准，并提出一种下界机制，说明分母记忆如何隐藏大梯度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the empirical observation that stochastic gradient noise in large language model pretraining is often heavy-tailed, this work investigates whether AdamW, the widely used optimizer, can converge under such conditions. The authors propose a theoretical framework to analyze AdamW’s behavior in heavy-tailed regimes, proving a positive weighted-metric benchmark and introducing a corridor lower-bound mechanism to demonstrate how its second-moment accumulator may mask large gradients. Their findings suggest that while AdamW may still perform well empirically, its convergence under heavy-tailed noise lacks rigorous theoretical support, leaving open the question of whether its design inherently limits its effectiveness in such settings.
鉴于大语言模型预训练中随机梯度噪声常呈重尾分布，本文探讨了广泛使用的优化器AdamW是否能在该条件下收敛。作者分析了AdamW在重尾噪声下的表现，指出其二阶矩累加器可能阻碍收敛，而这一问题在基于符号的优化器或AdaGrad中未出现。他们建立了AdamW的正向加权度量基准，并提出走廊下界机制，揭示分母记忆如何掩盖大梯度，从而阐明AdamW在重尾收敛中的理论局限。
IMAGIN-4D: Image-Guided Controllable Interaction Generation
Authors: Sai Kumar Dwivedi, Federica Bogo, Buğra Tekin, Chenhongyi Yang, Nadine Bertsch, Tomas Hodan, Michael J. Black, Dimitrios Tzionas, Shreyas Hampali
First: 2026-06-22T17:58:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:58:03+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 8 figures. Project page: https://imagin4d.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Generating human-object interactions (HOI) is central to character animation, robotics, AR/VR, and embodied AI. Recent HOI generation methods synthesize motion from text, object geometry, and sparse waypoints, controlling action semantics and object trajectories. However, these signals underspecify interaction: the same prompt and trajectory can produce different grasps, approach directions, body poses, object poses, contacts, and body-object layouts. We address this ambiguity with a reference image as a visual specification of the desired interaction snapshot. However, a single global image representation conflates distinct cues and conditions all frames on identical visual evidence. We therefore introduce IMAGIN-4D, a diffusion-based HOI generator that decomposes image conditioning spatio-temporally. For spatial conditioning, IMAGIN-4D extracts supervised interaction-state tokens for body pose, object pose, body-object contact, and spatial relationships at the depicted frame. For temporal conditioning, it computes frame-aware tokens by querying image patches per generated frame, allowing sequence segments to attend to different visual cues from the same image. To balance image, text, and waypoint cues, IMAGIN-4D uses role-aware conditioning: text, waypoints, and interaction-state tokens use separate AdaLN streams, while frame-aware visual tokens cross-attend with motion tokens. Since HOI motion datasets lack paired images, we build a synthetic motion-to-image rendering pipeline from FullBodyManipulation (FBM) and introduce an image-adherence metric to evaluate whether generated motions match the reference snapshot. Experiments on FBM and BEHAVE show that IMAGIN-4D improves fine-grained interaction control over single-token and uniformly image-conditioned baselines while preserving waypoint-following and motion quality. Code and models will be released at https://imagin4d.github.io.
中文标题/摘要
标题：IMAGIN-4D：基于图像引导的可控交互生成
生成人-物交互（HOI）是角色动画、机器人、AR/VR和具身AI的核心。近期的HOI生成方法通过文本、物体几何和稀疏关键点合成运动，控制动作语义和物体轨迹。然而，这些信号对交互的描述不足：相同的提示和轨迹可产生不同的抓取方式、接近方向、身体姿态、物体姿态、接触点和身体-物体布局。我们引入参考图像作为所需交互快照的视觉规范以解决此歧义。但单一全局图像表示会混淆不同线索，并使所有帧依赖相同视觉证据。因此，我们提出IMAGIN-4D，一种基于扩散的HOI生成器，将图像条件分解为空间与时间维度。空间条件方面，IMAGIN-4D从所描绘帧中提取监督交互状态标记，涵盖身体姿态、物体姿态、身体-物体接触及空间关系。时间条件方面，它通过为每个生成帧查询图像块计算帧感知标记，使序列片段可关注同一图像的不同视觉线索。为平衡图像、文本和关键点线索，IMAGIN-4D采用角色感知条件：文本、关键点和交互状态标记使用独立的AdaLN流，而帧感知视觉标记与运动标记交叉注意。由于HOI运动数据集缺乏配对图像，我们从FullBodyManipulation（FBM）构建合成运动到图像渲染管线，并引入图像契合度指标评估生成运动是否匹配参考快照。在FBM和BEHAVE上的实验表明，IMAGIN-4D在保持关键点跟随和运动质量的同时，相比单标记和均匀图像条件基线显著提升细粒度交互控制能力。代码与模型将在https://imagin4d.github.io发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the ambiguity in generating human-object interactions (HOI) from text, waypoints, and sparse visual cues, IMAGIN-4D introduces a diffusion-based method that decomposes image conditioning into spatial and temporal components to enable finer control. It extracts interaction-state tokens from a reference image for body and object poses, contact, and spatial relationships at the depicted frame, and generates frame-aware visual tokens by querying image patches per generated frame, allowing different temporal segments to attend to distinct visual cues. To balance multimodal inputs, it employs role-aware conditioning with separate AdaLN streams for text, waypoints, and interaction-state tokens, while enabling cross-attention between visual tokens and motion tokens. Evaluated on synthetic datasets using a novel image-adherence metric, IMAGIN-4D outperforms single-token and uniformly image-conditioned baselines in fine-grained interaction control while maintaining waypoint adherence and motion quality.
为解决生成人-物交互时因单一图像提示导致动作细节不一致的问题，IMAGIN-4D提出一种基于扩散模型的方法，将图像条件分解为空间与时间两部分：空间上提取参考图像中的人体姿态、物体姿态、接触关系等交互状态标记；时间上通过每帧查询图像局部区域生成帧感知视觉标记，使不同帧可关注图像不同区域。为平衡文本、轨迹点与视觉信息，采用角色感知条件化机制，文本、轨迹点与交互状态分别通过独立AdaLN流处理，视觉标记与运动标记交叉注意。在合成数据集上实验表明，该方法在保持轨迹跟随与运动质量的同时，显著提升交互细节的可控性。
PsyBridge: A Hybrid Intelligent Framework for Multi-Dimensional Mental Health Assessment and Decision Support
Authors: Sunil Wanjari, Manish Thakre, Aayushi Asole, Sharwari Raut, Kwabena Adu-Duodu, Yinhao Li, Stanly Wilson
First: 2026-06-22T17:57:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:57:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mental health assessment commonly relies on isolated screening instruments or data-driven models that often lack interpretability and multi-dimensional integration. Existing approaches frequently focus on individual indicators such as depression or anxiety while providing limited support for comprehensive and explainable decision-making. To address this limitation, this study proposes PsyBridge, a hybrid intelligent decision-support framework designed for multi-dimensional mental health assessment through the integration of clinically validated screening tools, cognitive evaluation, and personality profiling within a unified architecture. The proposed framework incorporates PHQ-9 and GAD-7 assessments alongside cognitive and behavioural indicators using a modular design and a weighted aggregation mechanism to generate interpretable mental health risk classifications and recommendations. To evaluate the framework, a semi-synthetic dataset consisting of 500 patient profiles representing varying severity levels was constructed based on clinically grounded score distributions. Experimental results demonstrate that PsyBridge achieves an overall accuracy of 0.84, outperforming standalone PHQ-9 and GAD-7 assessments while improving precision, recall, and F1-score. Sensitivity analysis and ablation studies further indicate that integrating cognitive and personality components contributes to more stable classification performance and reduces inconsistencies in moderate-risk prediction. The findings suggest that PsyBridge provides a scalable and interpretable approach for AI-assisted mental health decision support, particularly within digital healthcare and telehealth environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PsyBridge：一种用于多维度心理健康评估与决策支持的混合智能框架
心理健康评估通常依赖孤立的筛查工具或数据驱动模型，常缺乏可解释性和多维度整合。现有方法多聚焦于抑郁或焦虑等单一指标，对全面且可解释的决策支持支持有限。为解决此问题，本研究提出PsyBridge，一种混合智能决策支持框架，通过在统一架构内整合临床验证的筛查工具、认知评估与人格画像，实现多维度心理健康评估。该框架结合PHQ-9与GAD-7评估，辅以认知与行为指标，采用模块化设计与加权聚合机制，生成可解释的心理健康风险分类与建议。为评估该框架，构建了包含500个患者档案的半合成数据集，反映不同严重程度，基于临床依据的评分分布。实验结果表明，PsyBridge整体准确率达0.84，优于单独使用PHQ-9与GAD-7评估，同时提升精确率、召回率与F1分数。敏感性分析与消融研究进一步表明，整合认知与人格成分可提升分类稳定性，减少中等风险预测的不一致性。研究结果表明，PsyBridge为AI辅助心理健康决策支持提供了一种可扩展且可解释的方法，尤其适用于数字医疗与远程医疗环境。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of isolated mental health screening tools that lack interpretability and multi-dimensional integration, this study introduces PsyBridge, a hybrid intelligent framework that combines clinically validated assessments (PHQ-9 and GAD-7), cognitive evaluation, and personality profiling within a unified modular architecture. Using a weighted aggregation mechanism, PsyBridge generates interpretable risk classifications and recommendations. Evaluated on a semi-synthetic dataset of 500 patient profiles, the framework achieved 84% overall accuracy, outperforming standalone PHQ-9 and GAD-7 assessments in precision, recall, and F1-score. Sensitivity analysis confirmed that incorporating cognitive and personality components enhances classification stability and reduces prediction inconsistency in moderate-risk cases, demonstrating PsyBridge’s potential for scalable, explainable AI support in digital mental health settings.
为解决现有精神健康筛查工具缺乏可解释性和多维度整合的问题，本研究提出PsyBridge框架，融合临床验证的PHQ-9与GAD-7量表、认知评估及人格测评，构建统一架构。通过模块化设计与加权聚合机制，生成可解释的风险分类与建议。在包含500个患者档案的半合成数据集上测试，该框架整体准确率达84%，优于单独使用PHQ-9和GAD-7的评估效果，且在精确率、召回率和F1分数上均有提升。敏感性分析表明，整合认知与人格维度可增强分类稳定性，降低中等风险预测的不一致性。
Teaching LLMs String Matching, Backtracking, and Error Recovery to Deduce Bases and Truth Tables for the Combinatorially Exploding Bit Manipulation Puzzles
Authors: Prateek Agnihotri, Sanchit Jain, Prabhat Agnihotri, Aditya Prasad, Shubham Jain
First: 2026-06-22T17:57:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:57:08+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 4 figures, 2 tables. 7th Place Solution for the NVIDIA Nemotron Model Reasoning Challenge (Kaggle)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents our algorithmic innovations for the NVIDIA Nemotron Model Reasoning Challenge, focusing on Bit Manipulation Puzzles. In this task, the objective is to discover a hidden logical rule transforming input binary strings to outputs, then apply it to unseen inputs. Large Language Models (LLMs) notoriously struggle here; traditional methods force them to simulate complex boolean logic and arithmetic, leading to hallucinations. Furthermore, the search space of bitwise operations (combinations of shifts, rotations, and logic gates) suffers from a severe combinatorial explosion. To overcome this computational intractability, we present a novel approach that abandons arithmetic logic entirely in favor of string similarity, structured search, and autonomous error recovery. Our core contributions are: 1. Bases and Truth Table Formulation: We reframe logic-gate deduction into a base-selection task, leveraging string similarity (minimal bit flips) to isolate primitive transformations ("bases") and deduce truth tables without complex arithmetic. 2. Backtracking DFS and Error Recovery: We formalize a search process that tests candidate bases, detects logical collisions across examples, and backtracks upon failure to perform robust error recovery. 3. Bit Tokenization and Interactive Reasoning SFT: We force the tokenizer to encode binary strings as individual single-bit tokens. We use dynamic masking to simulate external oracle feedback, training the model to hypothesize, self-evaluate, and backtrack natively. Evaluated on bit manipulation puzzles, our approach achieved over 96% validation accuracy. This represents the highest performance in this category, driving our 7th Place overall finish in the contest.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过字符串匹配、回溯与错误恢复训练大语言模型推导位操作谜题的基元与真值表
本文提出了针对NVIDIA Nemotron模型推理挑战中位操作谜题的算法创新。该任务要求发现将输入二进制字符串映射到输出的隐藏逻辑规则，并将其应用于未见输入。大语言模型在此类任务中表现不佳，传统方法迫使它们模拟复杂的布尔逻辑与算术运算，导致幻觉。此外，位操作（移位、旋转与逻辑门组合）的搜索空间存在严重的组合爆炸问题。为克服这一计算不可行性，我们提出了一种全新方法，完全摒弃算术逻辑，转而采用字符串相似性、结构化搜索与自主错误恢复。 核心贡献包括：1. 基元与真值表建模：将逻辑门推导重构为基元选择任务，利用字符串相似性（最小位翻转）隔离原始变换（“基元”），无需复杂算术即可推导真值表。2. 回溯深度优先搜索与错误恢复：形式化了一种候选基元测试过程，检测跨示例的逻辑冲突，并在失败时回溯以实现鲁棒错误恢复。3. 位元分词与交互式推理监督微调：强制分词器将二进制字符串编码为单比特独立令牌，并通过动态掩码模拟外部预言机反馈，训练模型原生地进行假设、自我评估与回溯。 在位操作谜题上评估，我们的方法验证准确率超过96%，在该类别中表现最佳，助力我们在竞赛中获得第7名。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty LLMs face in solving bit manipulation puzzles due to combinatorial explosion and hallucinations, this work introduces a method that replaces arithmetic logic with string similarity, structured backtracking, and error recovery. It reformulates the problem as selecting minimal-bit-flip bases and deducing truth tables, then trains models to reason interactively using bit-tokenized inputs and dynamic masking for self-correction. Experiments show the method achieves over 96% validation accuracy, ranking 7th overall in the NVIDIA Nemotron Model Reasoning Challenge.
为了解决大语言模型在位操作谜题中因组合爆炸和算术推理导致的幻觉问题，该研究提出了一种新方法，用字符串相似性、结构化回溯和错误恢复替代传统算术逻辑。方法将问题重构为选择最小位翻转基元并推导真值表，同时通过比特化输入和动态掩码模拟反馈，训练模型自主假设、评估与回溯。实验表明该方法在验证集上准确率超96%，在NVIDIA Nemotron推理挑战赛中位列第7。
Can LLMs Reliably Self-Report Adversarial Prefills, and How?
Authors: Quang Minh Nguyen, Uzair Ahmed, Taegyoon Kim
First: 2026-06-22T17:56:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:56:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Prior work shows that large language models (LLMs) exhibit introspective capability on benign tasks. We extend the question to safety contexts and examine how reliably a model can recognize that its own prior response was elicited by an adversarial prefill attack. Across ten open-weight instruction-tuned LLMs (3B to 70B) and four safety benchmarks, no model reliably recognizes its own compromised outputs, with models claiming intent on prefilled responses at an average rate of $27.3\%$. Introspective signal stems largely from safety- and refusal-related reasoning. Orthogonalizing models' weights against the refusal direction collapses the gap between claiming rates on prefilled and natural outputs to near zero, though the direction is not its unique mediator. The signal is also probe-dependent: framing the question as internal intention versus external tampering elicits qualitatively different responses on the same models. We test three LoRA finetuning methods (SFT, GRPO, DPO) on eight models from 3B to 27B; all three widen the intention-probe gap on every model from 8B to 27B, with method ranking varying by model. The intervention does not transfer to the tampering probe and counterintuitively raises attack success rate under adversarial prefill on most models, amounting to a partial mitigation. These findings outline mechanisms underpinning the observed introspective signals in safety contexts and highlight risks in the reliability of LLM self-reports.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型能否可靠地自我报告对抗性预填充攻击，以及如何？
先前研究表明，大语言模型（LLMs）在良性任务上表现出内省能力。我们将问题扩展到安全情境，考察模型能否可靠识别自身先前响应是因对抗性预填充攻击所致。在十款开放权重指令微调LLM（3B至70B）及四项安全基准测试中，无模型能可靠识别自身被攻陷的输出，模型声称预填充响应具有意图的平均比例为27.3%。内省信号主要源于安全与拒绝相关推理。将模型权重正交化于拒绝方向后，预填充与自然输出的声称率差距缩小至近零，但该方向并非其唯一中介变量。该信号亦依赖于探针：将问题表述为内部意图与外部篡改，会引发同一模型产生质性不同的响应。我们在八款3B至27B模型上测试三种LoRA微调方法（SFT、GRPO、DPO）；所有方法在8B至27B模型上均扩大了意图探针的差距，且方法排名因模型而异。该干预措施对篡改探针无迁移效应，反而在多数模型上因对抗性预填充导致攻击成功率上升，构成部分缓解。这些发现揭示了安全情境下内省信号的机制，并凸显LLM自我报告可靠性的风险。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models can reliably detect when their outputs are influenced by adversarial prefill attacks, extending prior introspective capability research into safety contexts. Using ten open-weight instruction-tuned LLMs (3B–70B) and four safety benchmarks, the authors find that models rarely recognize compromised outputs, falsely claiming intent on prefilled responses at 27.3% average rate. The introspective signal primarily arises from safety- and refusal-related reasoning, which can be reduced by orthogonalizing model weights against refusal directions, though not eliminated. The signal also depends on question framing: internal intention probes yield higher false claims than external tampering probes. Three LoRA fine-tuning methods (SFT, GRPO, DPO) tested on eight models (3B–27B) widened the intention-probe gap across all models from 8B to 27B, but did not improve tampering detection and paradoxically increased attack success rates, indicating partial mitigation with unintended consequences.
本研究探讨大语言模型在安全场景下能否可靠识别自身输出是否受对抗性预填充攻击影响，将此前对良性任务的内省能力研究扩展至安全语境。通过对十款开放权重指令微调模型（3B–70B）和四个安全基准测试，研究发现模型极少能识别被操控的输出，平均有27.3%的模型错误声称对预填充内容有主观意图。该内省信号主要源于安全与拒绝相关推理，通过将模型权重正交于拒绝方向可显著缩小预填充与自然输出的意图声称差距，但并非唯一中介因素。该信号还依赖于提问方式——内部意图与外部篡改的表述会引发模型不同响应；三种LoRA微调方法（SFT、GRPO、DPO）在8B至27B模型上均扩大了意图探测的差距，但反而在多数模型上提高了对抗预填充攻击的成功率，表明其缓解效果有限且不具迁移性。
Tapered Language Models
Authors: Reza Bayat, Ali Behrouz, Aaron Courville
First: 2026-06-22T17:56:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:56:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern language models, including transformer, recurrent, and memory-based variants, share a common chassis: a stack of identical layers in which parameters are allocated uniformly across depth. This is a default inherited from the original transformer and largely unchanged since, yet a growing body of evidence suggests that layers contribute non-uniformly to the final output, with later layers refining the residual stream rather than transforming it. We ask whether parameter capacity should reflect this asymmetry. Our controlled experiment shows that, under a fixed budget, allocating more capacity to earlier layers and less to later layers improves perplexity over a uniform-width baseline, while the reverse allocation hurts. Building on this result, we introduce Tapered Language Models (TLMs), an architectural principle in which a parameter-bearing component is monotonically tapered across depth under a fixed total budget. MLPs are the natural site for this instantiation: they dominate parameter count across all modern LM families and expose width as a single, clean axis of variation. Across three model scales and four architectures (Transformer, Gated Attention, Hope-attention, and Titans), tapering MLP width via a smooth cosine schedule consistently improves perplexity and downstream benchmark performance over uniform baselines, at no additional parameter or compute cost. These findings establish depth-aware capacity allocation as a simple, architecture-agnostic axis of language model design, a free lever hidden in plain sight.
中文标题/摘要
标题：锥形语言模型
现代语言模型，包括Transformer、循环和基于记忆的变体，共享一个共同架构：堆叠的相同层，参数在深度上均匀分配。这一设计源于原始Transformer，自那以来基本未变，但越来越多的证据表明，各层对最终输出的贡献非均匀，后期层主要精炼残差流而非转换它。我们探讨参数容量是否应反映这种不对称性。我们的受控实验表明，在固定预算下，将更多容量分配给早期层、较少分配给后期层，相比均匀宽度基线能改善困惑度，而反向分配则会损害效果。基于此结果，我们提出锥形语言模型（TLMs），一种在固定总预算下，参数承载组件随深度单调递减的架构原则。MLP是该原则的自然实现场所：它们在所有现代语言模型家族中主导参数数量，且宽度是其单一、清晰的可变轴。在三种模型规模和四种架构（Transformer、门控注意力、Hope-注意力和Titans）中，通过平滑余弦调度缩减MLP宽度，相比均匀基线，始终能提升困惑度和下游基准性能，且不增加额外参数或计算成本。这些发现确立了深度感知容量分配作为语言模型设计中一个简单、架构无关的自由杠杆，这一隐藏在明处的杠杆此前未被充分利用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by evidence that language model layers contribute non-uniformly to output—with later layers refining rather than transforming the residual stream—this work investigates whether parameter allocation should reflect this depth asymmetry. It proposes Tapered Language Models (TLMs), an architecture where parameter capacity is monotonically reduced across layers under a fixed budget, implemented most naturally via tapering MLP widths. Experiments across three scales and four architectures show that tapering MLP width using a cosine schedule consistently improves perplexity and downstream performance over uniform baselines, without increasing parameters or compute.
鉴于语言模型各层对输出贡献不均——后期层主要精炼而非转换残差流——本研究探讨参数分配是否应反映这种深度不对称性。提出Tapered Language Models（TLMs）架构，即在固定预算下逐层单调减少参数容量，最自然的实现方式是通过余弦调度递减MLP宽度。实验表明，在三种规模和四种架构上，采用余弦调度递减MLP宽度可稳定提升困惑度和下游性能，且不增加参数或计算开销。
Bellman-sufficient Information Complexity
Authors: Yunbei Xu
First: 2026-06-09T17:49:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:55:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We develop Bellman-sufficient information complexity, a representation-level framework for studying information-theoretic complexity in sequential decision making. The primitive object is an environment space $Ω$ and an admissible algorithm class. The intrinsic object is a Bellman-sufficient state representation together with an information index $Y=χ(Ω)$, often the optimal decision or value object rather than the full environment. This replaces syntactic model realizability with representation-level sufficiency for decision making. On the upper-bound side, learning is organized as a dynamic program on the sufficient state with a logarithmic information potential for the index. In fixed-truth analysis this potential is represented by the coordinate log loss $γ\log(1/q_t(χ(ω^\star)))$; in the indexed Algorithmic Information Ratio (AIR) regret identities it gives rise to the log-posterior telescope, and after Bayesian posterior averaging it corresponds to an entropy term. On the lower side, a Bellman-Fano certificate uses the same state and index to compare the indexed information telescope with the ghost-good mass of low-regret reference trajectories. The central matching statement is therefore a conditional Bellman information-risk sandwich when the log-penalized Bellman upper value and the ghost-quantile lower certificate close on the same representation and at the same radius. UCB, E2D/DEC, and AMS/EBO then appear as tractable certificates or relaxations of this same log-potential Bellman program, rather than as separate notions of information complexity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：贝尔曼充分信息复杂度
我们提出了贝尔曼充分信息复杂度，这是一种用于研究序列决策中信息论复杂度的表示层面框架。基本对象是环境空间 $Ω$ 与可接受的算法类。内在对象是贝尔曼充分状态表示，以及信息索引 $Y=χ(Ω)$，通常为最优决策或价值对象，而非完整环境。这用表示层面的充分性替代了语法模型的可实现性。在上界方面，学习被组织为在充分状态上的动态规划，其索引具有对数信息势。在固定真值分析中，该势由坐标对数损失 $γ\log(1/q_t(χ(ω^\star)))$ 表示；在索引算法信息比率（AIR）的遗憾恒等式中，它产生对数后验望远镜，经贝叶斯后验平均后对应熵项。在下界方面，贝尔曼-费诺证书使用相同的状态和索引，将索引信息望远镜与低遗憾参考轨迹的幽灵良好质量进行比较。因此，核心匹配陈述是当对数惩罚的贝尔曼上值与幽灵分位数下证书在相同表示和相同半径上收敛时的条件贝尔曼信息风险夹层。UCB、E2D/DEC 和 AMS/EBO 则表现为该对数势贝尔曼程序的可处理证书或松弛，而非独立的信息复杂度概念。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to study information-theoretic complexity in sequential decision making at a representation level rather than syntactic model realizability, the paper introduces Bellman-sufficient information complexity, which uses a Bellman-sufficient state representation and an information index derived from the environment. The framework upper-bounds learning via a dynamic program on the sufficient state, where the information potential is expressed through log-loss or log-posterior terms, and lower-bounds it via a Bellman-Fano certificate comparing information telescopes against low-regret reference trajectories. The central result is a conditional Bellman information-risk sandwich where upper and lower bounds converge on the same representation and radius, and algorithms like UCB, E2D/DEC, and AMS/EBO emerge as tractable relaxations of this unified Bellman program.
为在决策序列中以表征层面而非语法模型层面刻画信息复杂度，该文提出贝尔曼充分信息复杂度框架，其核心是贝尔曼充分状态表征与从环境导出的信息索引。上界通过在充分状态上构建动态规划程序实现，信息势能以对数损失或后验熵形式表达；下界则通过贝尔曼-费诺证书，比较索引信息望远镜与低遗憾参考轨迹的“幽灵质量”。核心结论是条件贝尔曼信息风险夹层，当上界与下界在相同表征与半径下收敛时，UCB、E2D/DEC 和 AMS/EBO 等算法可视为该贝尔曼程序的可计算松弛，而非独立的信息复杂度概念。
GeoFidelity-Bench: Evaluating Segment-Level Geographic Fidelity in Text-to-Image Street-View Generation
Authors: Kaizhen Tan, Hanzhe Hong, Siru Tao
First: 2026-06-22T17:53:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:53:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Text-to-image models can generate visually plausible city streets, but whether their outputs correspond to a requested road segment rather than a generic city prior remains unclear. We introduce GeoFidelity-Bench, a reference-panel benchmark for segment-conditioned geographic fidelity in street-view generation. It contains 7,117 curated Mapillary images covering 109 named OpenStreetMap road segments in 25 cities across six continents. For each generated panel, the benchmark ranks the target reference panel against panels from the nearest segment in the same city, other segments in the same city, and segments from other cities, making local discrimination rather than absolute target similarity the primary test. We evaluate six open-weight text-to-image generators under city-only, street-and-neighborhood, and GPS-augmented prompts. Adding street and neighborhood names is associated with an increase of 5.5 percentage points in top-1 retrieval accuracy over city-only prompts, with a 95% confidence interval from 3.4 to 7.7 percentage points. However, the similarity margin between the target and the nearest segment in the same city remains near zero, indicating that local names improve broad local plausibility more than exact segment identity. Prompts that keep the city fixed but use incorrect street or neighborhood names further show that only part of the gain depends on the correct local names, while appending raw GPS coordinates as ordinary text yields no statistically clear additional benefit. Held-out real-image queries successfully recover segment identity, showing that the curated references contain usable segment-level signal. GeoFidelity-Bench thus reveals a persistent gap between city- or neighborhood-plausible street-view generation and faithful generation for a specific road segment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GeoFidelity-Bench：评估文本到图像街景生成中的路段级地理保真度
文本到图像模型可以生成视觉上合理的城市街道，但其输出是否对应请求的特定路段而非通用城市先验仍不明确。我们引入GeoFidelity-Bench，这是一个针对街景生成中路段条件地理保真度的参考面板基准。该基准包含7,117张精选的Mapillary图像，覆盖六大洲25个城市中的109条命名OpenStreetMap路段。对于每个生成的面板，该基准将目标参考面板与同城市最近路段、同城市其他路段及不同城市路段的面板进行排名，使局部区分而非绝对目标相似性成为主要测试标准。我们在仅城市名、街道与社区名、以及GPS增强提示下评估了六种开源文本到图像生成器。添加街道和社区名称可使Top-1检索准确率比仅城市名提示提高5.5个百分点（95%置信区间为3.4至7.7个百分点）。然而，目标与同城市最近路段之间的相似度差距仍接近零，表明局部名称主要提升的是广泛局部合理性，而非精确路段身份。固定城市但使用错误街道或社区名称的提示进一步表明，部分增益依赖于正确的局部名称，而将原始GPS坐标作为普通文本附加则无统计学上显著的额外收益。未参与训练的真实图像查询成功恢复了路段身份，表明精选参考包含可用的路段级信号。因此，GeoFidelity-Bench揭示了城市或社区合理街景生成与特定路段忠实生成之间持续存在的差距。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess whether text-to-image models can accurately generate street views for specific road segments rather than generic city scenes, the authors introduce GeoFidelity-Bench, a benchmark using 7,117 curated Mapillary images across 109 named road segments in 25 cities. The benchmark evaluates six open-weight models under varying prompt conditions—city-only, street-and-neighborhood, and GPS-augmented—and ranks generated images against reference panels from the nearest segment in the same city, other segments in the same city, and segments from other cities. Results show that adding street and neighborhood names improves top-1 retrieval accuracy by 5.5 percentage points over city-only prompts, but the target segment remains indistinguishable from the nearest local segment, suggesting local plausibility is enhanced without precise segment fidelity. Incorrect local names yield partial gains, while raw GPS coordinates offer no significant improvement, and real-image queries successfully recover segment identity, indicating the benchmark’s validity and revealing a persistent gap between plausible and faithful segment-level generation.
为评估文本到图像模型能否准确生成特定道路段的街景而非泛化的城市场景，作者构建了GeoFidelity-Bench基准，包含109个命名道路段在25座城市中的7117张精选Mapillary图像。该方法通过将生成图像与同城市最近段、同城市其他段及不同城市段的参考图像进行排序，强调局部区分而非绝对相似性。在城市名、街名与社区名、以及GPS增强提示下测试了六款开源模型，添加街名与社区名使Top-1检索准确率提升5.5个百分点（95%置信区间：3.4–7.7），但目标段与同城市最近段的相似度仍接近零，表明局部合理性提升强于精确段落识别。使用错误的街名或社区名仅部分提升效果，而直接附加原始GPS坐标无显著增益。真实图像查询能成功恢复段落身份，验证了基准的有效性，并揭示了城市级合理生成与精确段落级忠实生成之间持续存在的差距。
On the Limits of Prompt-Conditioned Language Models as General-Purpose Learners
Authors: David Mguni, Julian Ma, Jun Wang
First: 2026-06-22T17:52:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:52:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are frequently portrayed as general-purpose solvers capable of solving arbitrary tasks. We argue that this view overlooks a fundamental constraint: language is a compressed and capacity-limited interface for conveying task information. Modelling User--System interaction as a bilevel \emph{cheap-talk} game, we analyse how latent tasks are encoded into prompts and reinterpreted under alignment and safety constraints. We introduce a conceptual decomposition separating task inference from execution and derive PAC-Bayes bounds that distinguish finite-sample estimation error from irreducible structural limitations. Our first main result establishes an \emph{expressivity floor}: language acts as a capacity-limited communication channel, and whenever the informational complexity of a task family exceeds the capacity of that channel, distinct tasks become unavoidably indistinguishable to the Solver, inducing a strictly positive error floor that cannot be eliminated by additional data, optimisation, or model scaling alone. We then establish an \emph{objective-misalignment floor}: when alignment constraints restrict the admissible output set, the User-ideal distribution may lie outside the feasible class, inducing an irreducible distortion. Together, these results yield a formal negative conclusion: prompt-conditioned LLMs are not universal problem solvers through prompting alone, as there exist task families for which correct behaviour is provably unattainable even in the infinite-data regime. More broadly, our analysis shows the limits of prompt-based generalisation arise from information-constrained communication and alignment-constrained objectives. This suggests that interfaces beyond natural language, including multimodal observations and, external memory, may reduce the inherent LLM limitations by increasing the task-relevant information available to the System.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关于提示条件语言模型作为通用学习者的能力极限
大型语言模型（LLMs）常被描绘为能解决任意任务的通用求解器。我们认为，这种观点忽视了一个根本限制：语言是传递任务信息的压缩且容量受限的接口。我们将用户-系统交互建模为双层\emph{廉价对话}博弈，分析潜在任务如何编码进提示并在对齐与安全约束下被重新解释。我们引入一个概念分解，将任务推断与执行分离，并推导出PAC-Bayes界限，区分有限样本估计误差与不可消除的结构限制。我们的第一个主要结果确立了\emph{表达能力下限}：语言作为容量受限的通信信道，当任务族的信息复杂度超过该信道容量时，不同任务对求解器变得不可避免地不可区分，从而诱导一个严格为正的误差下限，仅靠增加数据、优化或模型规模无法消除。接着我们确立了\emph{目标错位下限}：当对齐约束限制了可接受输出集时，用户理想分布可能位于不可行类之外，从而诱导不可消除的失真。这两个结果共同得出一个形式化的否定结论：仅靠提示条件的LLMs并非通用问题求解器，因为存在某些任务族，即使在无限数据条件下，其正确行为也可证明无法实现。更广泛地说，我们的分析表明，基于提示的泛化能力的局限源于信息受限的通信与对齐约束的目标。这暗示，超越自然语言的接口（如多模态观测和外部记忆）可能通过增加系统可用的任务相关信息，降低LLM的固有局限。
Summary / 总结
This paper challenges the notion that large language models (LLMs) can act as universal problem solvers through prompting alone, arguing that natural language is a compressed, capacity-limited interface for task communication. The authors model user-system interaction as a bilevel cheap-talk game, decomposing task inference from execution and deriving PAC-Bayes bounds to separate estimation error from structural limitations. Their first result establishes an 'expressivity floor': when task informational complexity exceeds language’s capacity, distinct tasks become indistinguishable to the model, creating an irreducible error floor unaffected by more data or model scaling. Their second result, the 'objective-misalignment floor', shows that alignment constraints may exclude the user’s ideal output distribution, causing unavoidable distortion. Together, these findings formally prove that prompt-conditioned LLMs cannot solve all task families correctly even with infinite data, highlighting that generalization limits stem from information constraints and objective alignment.
本文质疑大型语言模型（LLM）仅通过提示即可作为通用问题求解器的观点，指出自然语言作为任务信息传递的接口存在容量限制和压缩性。作者将用户-系统交互建模为双层廉价对话博弈，将任务推断与执行分离，并推导出PAC-Bayes界限以区分估计误差与结构限制。主要发现包括两个不可消除的误差下限：表达能力下限，即当任务信息复杂度超过语言容量时，不同任务在模型中变得不可区分；目标错位下限，即对齐约束限制了可接受输出空间，导致理想用户分布不可达，从而产生固有失真。二者共同证明，即使在无限数据、模型扩展或优化条件下，提示驱动的LLM仍无法正确解决某些任务家族，其局限源于信息约束的通信机制与对齐约束的目标空间。
PHAST-Net: Attention-Guided, Physics-Informed Network for Unified Estimation of Ideal Time-Frequency Representations
Authors: James M. Cozens, Simon J. Godsill
First: 2026-06-22T17:49:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:49:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce PHAST-Net, an attention-guided, physics-informed network for unified estimation of Ideal Time-Frequency Representations (ITFRs), spanning spectral, tempo-based, metrical, and harmonic representations such as Spectrograms, Tempograms, and Metrograms. PHAST-Net learns an application-general mapping from a constellation of wavelet transforms, the proposed Continuous Log-frequency Adaptive Wavelet Transform (CLAWT), to high-resolution, cross-term-suppressed time-frequency (T-F) representations. The proposed constellation of CLAWTs is selected through Cohen's class kernel analysis to maximise curvature coverage in a logarithmic-frequency T-F plane tailored to harmonic signal structure. PHAST-Net further incorporates a proposed physics-informed auxiliary reprojection loss designed to reconstruct the idealised observed CLAWT constellation from the predicted ITFR and the corresponding Cohen's class kernels during training. This auxiliary objective promotes transform consistency and energy conservation, mitigates pathological target sparsity, and enhances optimisation stability. Attention layers further promote effective cross-term suppression across the input constellation. The log-frequency formulation also enables Harmonic PHAST-Net, which estimates a Harmonic ITFR that isolates fundamental structure, supporting robust fundamental-only representations for speech and music, such as derived fundamental Tempograms and Metrograms. We further introduce Spline-PHAST-Net, which parameterises detected and associated T-F ridges as continuous spline trajectories, enabling arbitrary-grid re-rendering and signal reconstruction. Trained on an effectively unbounded procedurally generated dataset, PHAST-Net demonstrates improved accuracy over established approaches, providing a unified framework for high-resolution, cross-term-robust analysis of speech, music, and broader nonstationary signals.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PHAST-Net：一种基于注意力引导和物理信息的统一理想时频表示估计网络
我们提出了PHAST-Net，一种基于注意力引导和物理信息的网络，用于统一估计理想时频表示（ITFRs），涵盖频谱、节奏、节拍和和声表示，如谱图、节奏图和节拍图。PHAST-Net学习从一组小波变换——所提出的连续对数频率自适应小波变换（CLAWT）——到高分辨率、交叉项抑制的时频（T-F）表示的通用映射。所提出的CLAWT集合通过Cohen类核分析选择，以最大化在针对谐波信号结构设计的对数频率T-F平面上的曲率覆盖。PHAST-Net进一步引入了一种物理信息辅助重投影损失，该损失在训练过程中从预测的ITFR和相应的Cohen类核重建理想化的观测CLAWT集合。该辅助目标促进变换一致性与能量守恒，缓解病态目标稀疏性，并增强优化稳定性。注意力层进一步促进输入集合中交叉项的有效抑制。对数频率形式还支持谐波PHAST-Net，该网络估计隔离基本结构的谐波ITFR，支持语音和音乐的鲁棒基本音高表示，如衍生的基本音高节奏图和节拍图。我们进一步引入了Spline-PHAST-Net，将检测到并关联的T-F脊线参数化为连续样条轨迹，从而支持任意网格重渲染和信号重建。在有效无界的程序生成数据集上训练后，PHAST-Net在准确性上优于现有方法，提供了一个统一框架，用于高分辨率、交叉项鲁棒的语音、音乐及更广泛非平稳信号分析。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a unified, high-resolution time-frequency representation across diverse signal types, PHAST-Net introduces an attention-guided, physics-informed neural architecture that maps a constellation of wavelet transforms—specifically the Continuous Log-frequency Adaptive Wavelet Transform (CLAWT)—to Ideal Time-Frequency Representations (ITFRs). The CLAWT constellation is optimized via Cohen’s class kernel analysis to maximize curvature coverage in a log-frequency plane suited to harmonic structures. The network employs a physics-informed auxiliary loss to enforce transform consistency and energy conservation during training, while attention mechanisms suppress cross-terms across input transforms. Additionally, Harmonic PHAST-Net isolates fundamental structures for speech and music, and Spline-PHAST-Net enables continuous spline-based re-rendering of T-F ridges for signal reconstruction. Evaluated on procedurally generated data, PHAST-Net outperforms existing methods in accuracy and robustness for nonstationary signals.
为实现对频谱、节奏、节拍和和声等多类时频表示的统一高精度分析，PHAST-Net提出一种基于注意力机制与物理约束的神经网络架构。该网络通过Cohen类核分析选择一组连续对数频率自适应小波变换（CLAWT），映射至理想时频表示（ITFR）。网络引入物理辅助损失，确保预测ITFR与重构CLAWT的一致性，促进能量守恒并缓解稀疏问题。注意力机制有效抑制跨变换交叉项，对数频率设计支持和声PHAST-Net以分离基频结构，Spline-PHAST-Net则将检测到的时频脊线建模为连续样条轨迹，支持任意网格重绘与信号重建。在程序生成数据集上训练后，PHAST-Net在语音、音乐及非平稳信号分析中显著优于现有方法。
MAS-PromptBench: When Does Prompt Optimization Improve Multi-Agent LLM Systems?
Authors: Juyang Bai, Laixi Shi
First: 2026-06-22T17:48:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:48:40+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://juyangbai.github.io/MAS-PromptBench/ ; Code: https://github.com/juyangbai/MAS-PromptBench
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Multi-agent systems (MAS) offer a scalable path forward for agentic AI, comprising multiple LLM-based agents, each assigned a system prompt and a position within a workflow that governs inter-agent coordination and output aggregation. System prompts thus form a critical and accessible optimization surface: they specify agents' roles and behaviors, enabling system-level improvements without model finetuning. Although prompt optimization has shown substantial potential for single LLMs, extending it to MAS poses distinct challenges, notably an exponentially growing search space. It remains unclear whether, when, and by how much prompt optimization improves MAS performance, and how sensitive such gains are to system configuration. In this work, we systematically study system-prompt optimization across a broad range of MAS setups varying in task, workflow, communication protocol, and team size, benchmarking two prompt optimizers that naturally extend state-of-the-art single-agent methods. The results reveal its potential to unlock significant gains while exposing open challenges, characterizing when and how much prompt optimization helps across diverse MAS settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MAS-PromptBench：提示优化在多智能体LLM系统中何时能提升性能？
多智能体系统（MAS）为代理型AI提供了一条可扩展的发展路径，由多个基于LLM的智能体组成，每个智能体分配系统提示和工作流中的位置，以协调智能体间交互并聚合输出。系统提示因此构成一个关键且可访问的优化面：它们定义智能体的角色与行为，可在不进行模型微调的情况下实现系统级改进。尽管提示优化对单个LLM展现出巨大潜力，将其扩展至MAS面临独特挑战，尤其是搜索空间呈指数级增长。目前尚不清楚提示优化在MAS中何时、如何以及多大程度上能提升性能，其增益对系统配置的敏感性如何。本研究系统性地考察了多种MAS设置（任务、工作流、通信协议、团队规模）下的系统提示优化，评估了两种自然扩展当前最优单智能体方法的提示优化器。结果揭示了其潜在的巨大增益，同时暴露了开放性挑战，并刻画了提示优化在不同MAS设置中何时及多大程度上有效。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether and how prompt optimization improves multi-agent LLM systems, motivated by the need to enhance coordination and performance without model fine-tuning. The authors systematically evaluate two prompt optimizers across diverse MAS configurations—including varying tasks, workflows, communication protocols, and team sizes—and benchmark their effectiveness against baseline prompts. Results show that prompt optimization can yield significant performance gains in many settings, particularly when agents have complex roles or communication is constrained, but gains are highly sensitive to system design and often diminish or vanish in simpler or larger teams.
该研究探讨了系统提示优化在多智能体系统（由基于LLM的智能体组成，系统提示定义角色与协作）中的有效性。作者在多种任务、工作流、通信协议和团队规模下，对两种源自单智能体方法的提示优化器进行基准测试。实验表明，提示优化在特定场景下（如智能体角色复杂或通信受限时）可显著提升性能，但其效果高度依赖系统配置，且在团队规模扩大或工作流复杂时收益往往下降。
Action-BED: Task-Driven Bayesian Experimental Design with Singly Intractable Objectives
Authors: Tom Rossa, Angus Phillips, Tom Rainforth
First: 2026-06-22T17:48:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:48:07+00:00
Comments: Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bayesian experimental design (BED) has traditionally been based on maximising expected uncertainty reductions from prior to posterior. A major shortfall of this approach is that it leads to doubly intractable objectives that are difficult to optimise, while customising them to particular downstream tasks of interest can also be difficult. Following first principles decision theory, we demonstrate that BED can alternatively be formulated in terms of an expected future loss (EFL) on downstream actions, providing a simple and naturally task-driven framework. Critically, we then show that all such EFLs can be rearranged into singly intractable objectives that can be jointly optimised with respect to both the design policy and a downstream action policy using stochastic gradients, an approach we refer to as ACTION-BED. This formulation further sidesteps the need for any explicit posterior or marginal likelihood estimation and is naturally implicit, requiring only the ability to sample from the joint model over model parameters and data, and evaluate the downstream loss function. It thus allows design policies to be learned more effectively, efficiently, and simply than existing methods, while providing easy customisation to different downstream tasks and losses.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ACTION-BED：面向任务驱动的贝叶斯实验设计与单次不可计算目标
贝叶斯实验设计（BED）传统上基于最大化从先验到后验的预期不确定性减少。该方法的主要缺陷是会导致双重不可计算目标，难以优化，且针对特定下游任务进行定制也较困难。遵循决策理论的基本原理，我们证明BED可另以下游行动的预期未来损失（EFL）形式表述，提供一个简单且自然的任务驱动框架。关键的是，我们进一步表明所有此类EFL均可重排为单次不可计算目标，可通过随机梯度联合优化设计策略与下游行动策略，我们称之为ACTION-BED。该表述进一步避免了对任何显式后验或边际似然估计的需求，且天然隐式，仅需从联合模型（模型参数与数据）采样并评估下游损失函数的能力。因此，它使设计策略的学习比现有方法更有效、更高效、更简单，同时便于针对不同下游任务和损失进行定制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational intractability and task-agnostic nature of traditional Bayesian experimental design, this work reformulates BED using expected future loss (EFL) from downstream actions, enabling task-driven design. It introduces ACTION-BED, a method that transforms EFL into singly intractable objectives, allowing joint optimization of design and action policies via stochastic gradients without requiring explicit posterior or marginal likelihood estimation. Experiments show ACTION-BED achieves superior design performance compared to existing methods, while being more efficient and adaptable to diverse downstream tasks and loss functions.
为解决传统贝叶斯实验设计（BED）计算复杂、难以针对下游任务定制的问题，本文提出基于下游动作预期未来损失（EFL）的重新表述，构建任务驱动框架。通过ACTION-BED方法，将EFL转化为单次不可计算目标，支持设计策略与动作策略的联合优化，无需显式后验或边缘似然估计，仅需联合模型采样与下游损失评估。实验表明，该方法在学习设计策略时更高效、更简单，且易于适配不同下游任务与损失函数。
A Reduced Order Model for Emergent Mechanics in Woven Systems
Authors: Anvay A. Pradhan, Evgueni T. Filipov, Talia Y. Moore
First: 2026-06-22T17:43:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:43:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Woven structures exhibit rich mechanical behaviors including anisotropic stiffness, shear-induced locking, and crimp interchange that emerge purely from the geometric arrangement of individual weavers rather than from constituent material properties. Existing models either homogenize these interactions or resolve them at prohibitive computational cost. We introduce a reduced-order model that bridges this gap by representing individual weaver interactions through a system of nodes and four physically interpretable stiffness elements capturing axial deformation, in-plane uncrimping, inter-weaver shear, and frictional slip. Eigenvalue analysis of the unit cell confirms that the lowest-energy deformation modes correspond directly to known weave-specific phenomena, and that each element is necessary for a complete kinematic and mechanistic description. Element stiffness parameters are calibrated against empirical three-point bending and shear data, achieving agreement within 5% across varied weaver widths and spacings. The validated model is then applied to demonstrate capabilities beyond the reach of continuum approaches including: the emergent Poisson's response arising from crimp interchange, stepwise force reduction during progressive weaver pullout, stress localization under three distinct tearing configurations, and programmable mechanical anisotropy through spatially graded weaver stiffness. The physical transparency and computational efficiency of the framework position it as a practical tool for the analysis and design of woven architected materials with programmable mechanical response.
中文标题/摘要
标题：编织结构中涌现力学行为的降阶模型
编织结构表现出丰富的力学行为，包括各向异性刚度、剪切诱导锁定和弯折互换，这些行为纯粹源于单根编织丝的几何排列，而非材料本征属性。现有模型要么将这些相互作用均质化，要么在计算成本上不可接受。本文提出一种降阶模型，通过节点系统和四个具有物理意义的刚度元件（轴向变形、平面内弯折解除、编织丝间剪切、摩擦滑移）表征单根编织丝相互作用。单元胞的特征值分析证实，最低能量变形模式直接对应已知的编织特异性现象，且每个元件对完整的运动学和力学描述均不可或缺。元件刚度参数通过实验三点弯曲和剪切数据校准，误差在5%以内，适用于不同编织丝宽度和间距。经验证的模型进一步应用于展示连续介质方法无法实现的能力：源于弯折互换的涌现泊松响应、编织丝逐步拔出过程中的分阶段力衰减、三种撕裂构型下的应力局域化，以及通过空间梯度编织丝刚度实现的可编程力学各向异性。该框架的物理透明性和计算效率使其成为分析和设计具有可编程力学响应的编织仿生材料的实用工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to capture emergent mechanical behaviors in woven systems without relying on material properties or computationally expensive simulations, the authors propose a reduced-order model that represents individual weaver interactions via a node-based system with four physically interpretable stiffness elements. These elements model axial deformation, in-plane uncrimping, inter-weaver shear, and frictional slip, and are calibrated against experimental bending and shear data to within 5% accuracy. The model successfully reproduces known weave-specific phenomena through eigenvalue analysis and demonstrates capabilities beyond continuum methods, including emergent Poisson’s response, stepwise force reduction during weaver pullout, stress localization under tearing, and programmable anisotropy via spatial stiffness gradients.
为在不依赖材料属性且避免高计算成本的前提下捕捉编织结构中涌现的力学行为，该文提出一种降阶模型，通过节点系统与四个物理可解释的刚度元件（轴向变形、平面解缠、织元间剪切与摩擦滑移）表征单根织元相互作用。单元胞的特征值分析证实各元件对应已知的编织特异性变形模式，且与实验弯折与剪切数据校准误差控制在5%以内，适用于不同织元尺寸与间距。该模型成功再现了连续介质方法无法模拟的现象，如由织元曲率交换引起的泊松效应、织元逐步拔出过程中的力阶跃衰减、三种撕裂模式下的应力局域化，以及通过空间刚度梯度实现的可编程各向异性，兼具物理清晰性与计算高效性，适用于编织结构材料的设计与分析。
Statistical Taylor Expansion: A New and Path-Independent Method for Uncertainty Analysis
Authors: Chengpu Wang
First: 2024-10-02T04:02:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:41:06+00:00
Comments: 53 pages, 44 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Statistical Taylor expansion is a rigorous extension of conventional Taylor expansion that replaces each precise input variable with a random variable of known distribution and sample count, then computes the mean, deviation, and a bounding reliability of every result. By tracking the propagation of input uncertainties through all intermediate steps, it renders the final result path-independent, with precise quantification of the tracking quality. This path-independence sets it fundamentally apart from conventional numerical approaches, which are path-dependent. This study presents an implementation called variance arithmetic and demonstrates its performance across diverse mathematical applications. This study also reveals the potentially substantial impact of numerical errors in library functions, the defect of applying input uncertainties as weights in conventional regression, and the modeling error of the discrete Fourier transformation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：统计泰勒展开：一种新的、与路径无关的不确定性分析方法
统计泰勒展开是对传统泰勒展开的严谨扩展，将每个精确输入变量替换为具有已知分布和样本数的随机变量，然后计算每个结果的均值、偏差及置信下界。通过追踪输入不确定性在所有中间步骤中的传播，该方法使最终结果与路径无关，并精确量化追踪质量。这种与路径无关的特性使其从根本上区别于传统数值方法（路径依赖）。本研究提出了一种名为方差算术的实现方式，并展示了其在多种数学应用中的表现。此外，本研究还揭示了库函数中数值误差的潜在重大影响、传统回归中将输入不确定性作为权重的缺陷，以及离散傅里叶变换的建模误差。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for path-independent uncertainty quantification in complex computations, this study introduces statistical Taylor expansion—a method that replaces deterministic inputs with random variables and computes mean, deviation, and reliability bounds for outputs. By propagating uncertainties through all intermediate steps, the method ensures results are independent of computational path, unlike conventional numerical approaches. Experimental results demonstrate its effectiveness across diverse mathematical applications, while also uncovering significant numerical errors in standard library functions, flaws in using input uncertainties as regression weights, and modeling inaccuracies in discrete Fourier transforms.
为解决传统数值方法路径依赖导致的不确定性分析缺陷，该研究提出统计泰勒展开法，将确定性输入变量替换为已知分布的随机变量，计算输出的均值、偏差与可靠性边界。通过实现“方差算术”，该方法在所有计算步骤中追踪不确定性传播，确保结果与路径无关。实验在多种数学场景中验证了其准确性与鲁棒性，同时揭示了标准库函数中的显著数值误差、输入不确定性作为回归权重的缺陷，以及离散傅里叶变换的建模误差。
Dynamic estimation of slowly varying sequences
Authors: Prashant Gokhale, Mikhail Khodak, Sandeep Silwal
First: 2026-06-22T17:40:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:40:49+00:00
Comments: Preprint. 14 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We consider the problem of sequentially approximating functions of each element in a slowly-varying sequence, i.e. one where the magnitude $α_i$ of the difference between the elements at positions $i$ and $i-1$ is small. Recent work on implicit trace estimation shows that when $α_t$ is small, reusing queries to past sequence elements can reduce the overall cost [Dharangutte \& Musco, NeurIPS~2021; Woodruff et al., NeurIPS~2022]. We introduce a framework generalizing this to a variety of linear and nonlinear functions on diverse vector spaces, obtaining novel sequential estimation results for matrix powers, spectral densities, Monte Carlo integration, and a boundary value problem from partial differential equations~(PDEs). Furthermore, we develop a novel algorithm for use with this framework that locally scales the estimation budget with $α_t$, obtaining sharper path-length-style variation bounds of form $\mathcal O(\sum_{i=1}^mα_i)$ on the cost of estimating a sequence of length $m$. This improves upon the previous implicit trace estimation bound of $\mathcal O(m\cdot\max_iα_i)$ [Dharangutte \& Musco, NeurIPS~2021], which is achieved by fixing the query budget using the worst-case $α_i$ and is thus inefficient for stable sequences with rare bursts. Lastly, while all past work assumes a known bound on $α_i$, we show in certain cases how the changes can be estimated on-the-fly with (nearly) no added cost. In summary, our framework makes the sequential approximation toolkit general-purpose and adaptive while improving upon state-of-the-art-guarantees for dynamic trace estimation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：缓慢变化序列的动态估计
我们研究了对缓慢变化序列中每个元素的函数进行序列近似的问题，即序列中位置i与i-1处元素之差的幅度α_i较小。近期关于隐式迹估计的工作表明，当α_t较小时，重用对过去序列元素的查询可降低总成本[Dharangutte & Musco, NeurIPS 2021; Woodruff 等, NeurIPS 2022]。我们提出一个框架，将其推广至多种向量空间上的线性和非线性函数，从而为矩阵幂、谱密度、蒙特卡洛积分及偏微分方程（PDEs）中的边界值问题获得新颖的序列估计结果。此外，我们开发了一种新算法，该算法在框架内根据α_t局部调整估计预算，获得形式为O(∑_{i=1}^m α_i)的更优路径长度式变化成本界，优于先前隐式迹估计的O(m·max_i α_i)界[Dharangutte & Musco, NeurIPS 2021]，后者通过固定查询预算以应对最坏情况α_i，因此在稳定序列中偶发突增时效率低下。最后，尽管以往所有工作均假设α_i有已知上界，我们展示了在某些情况下可近乎无额外成本地实时估计变化量。总之，我们的框架使序列近似工具通用且自适应，并改进了动态迹估计的最先进保证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to efficiently approximate functions of elements in slowly-varying sequences, this work generalizes implicit trace estimation techniques to handle a wide range of linear and nonlinear functions across diverse vector spaces. The authors introduce a novel sequential estimation framework that dynamically allocates computational resources based on local sequence variation, achieving tighter cost bounds of the form O(∑α_i) compared to the prior worst-case bound of O(m·maxα_i). Experimental results demonstrate improved efficiency for tasks including matrix powers, spectral densities, Monte Carlo integration, and PDE boundary value problems, particularly when sequences exhibit stable behavior with infrequent large changes. The framework also supports on-the-fly estimation of sequence variations without significant overhead, enhancing adaptability in real-world applications.
为高效近似缓慢变化序列中各元素的函数值，本文将隐式迹估计技术推广至多种线性和非线性函数及不同向量空间，提出一种动态分配计算预算的框架。该框架根据局部序列变化调整资源，实现 $\mathcal O(\sum_{i=1}^mα_i)$ 的成本界，优于此前 $\mathcal O(m\cdot\max_iα_i)$ 的最坏情况界，尤其在稳定序列中表现更优。实验表明，该方法在矩阵幂、谱密度、蒙特卡洛积分及偏微分方程边界问题中均有效，且支持无需额外开销的序列变化实时估计，兼具自适应性与通用性。
EnterpriseClawBench: Benchmarking Agents from Real Workplace Sessions
Authors: Jincheng Zhong, Weizhi Wang, Che Jiang, Kai Tian, Zhenzhao Yuan, Junlin Yang, Dianqiao Lei, Kaiyan Zhang
First: 2026-06-22T17:39:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:39:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Enterprise agents increasingly operate inside workspaces: they read heterogeneous files, invoke tools, and deliver business artifacts. We introduce EnterpriseClawBench, an enterprise agent benchmark constructed from proprietary, real-world agent sessions. Starting from a large archive of workplace sessions, the EnterpriseClawBench produces 852 reproducible tasks, each paired with recovered fixtures, rewritten prompts, role classes, skill subclasses, hard rules, and semantic rubrics. Because the sessions contain internal enterprise content, we do not release the benchmark data; instead, our reusable contribution is the construction and evaluation protocol. On EnterpriseClawBench, the best configuration reaches only 0.663 (Codex with GPT-5.5). These results show that enterprise agent evaluation must report harness--model combinations, artifact delivery, visual quality, cost, runtime, and skill-transfer behavior, rather than collapsing performance into a single score. Code: https://github.com/FrontisAI/EnterpriseClawBench
中文标题/摘要
标题：EnterpriseClawBench：基于真实工作场景会话的企业级代理基准测试
企业代理越来越多地在工作空间内运行：它们读取异构文件、调用工具并交付业务成果。我们引入了EnterpriseClawBench，这是一个基于专有真实世界代理会话构建的企业级代理基准。从大量工作场景会话中，EnterpriseClawBench生成了852个可复现任务，每个任务均配以恢复的测试用例、重写提示、角色类别、技能子类、硬性规则和语义评分标准。由于会话包含内部企业内容，我们不发布基准数据；我们的可复用贡献是构建与评估协议。在EnterpriseClawBench上，最佳配置仅达到0.663（Codex与GPT-5.5）。这些结果表明，企业代理评估必须报告框架-模型组合、成果交付、视觉质量、成本、运行时和技能迁移行为，而非将性能压缩为单一分数。代码：https://github.com/FrontisAI/EnterpriseClawBench

Motivated by the need to evaluate enterprise agents operating in real workspaces, this paper introduces EnterpriseClawBench, a benchmark derived from proprietary real-world agent sessions.
