Experimental results show that even the best configuration—Codex with GPT-5.5—achieves only 0.663 performance, highlighting the complexity of enterprise tasks. The study emphasizes that evaluation must report multiple dimensions including harness–model combinations, artifact delivery, visual quality, cost, runtime, and skill transfer, rather than relying on a single score.
为评估在真实工作环境中运行的企业代理，本文提出了EnterpriseClawBench，该基准由专有真实工作会话构建。方法从大量工作会话档案中提取852个可复现任务，并配以相关测试数据、提示重写、角色类别、技能子类、硬性规则和语义评分标准。实验结果显示，最佳配置（Codex + GPT-5.5）仅达到0.663的性能，表明企业任务复杂性高。研究强调评估必须报告多个维度，如代理组合、成果交付、视觉质量、成本、运行时和技能迁移行为，而非仅用单一分数衡量表现。
Lightweight Neural Framework for Robust 3D Volume and Surface Estimation from Multi-View Images
Authors: Diego E. Farchione, Ramzi Idoughi, Peter Wonka
First: 2026-06-22T17:39:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:39:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate volume and surface area estimation is critical for diverse applications, from marine ecology to medical diagnostics. However, existing methods often suffer from high computational costs and poor performance with sparse and noisy data. We propose a fully feed-forward framework that regresses scale-normalized volume and surface area and their associated uncertainties directly from multi-view images. By fusing 3D point cloud reconstructions with view-aligned 2D features through a graph-based decoder, our model bypasses iterative optimization, ensuring exceptional scalability and rapid inference. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art methods, particularly when operating with a low number of input images. Validated across coral monitoring, dietary analysis, and anthropometry, our proposed framework provides a robust, adaptable solution for quantitative shape analysis. This architecture provides a high-speed, scalable alternative for precise geometric estimation from visual data, maintaining high performance even in resource-constrained or sparse-view scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向多视角图像的轻量级神经框架，用于鲁棒三维体积与表面积估计
准确的体积与表面积估计对海洋生态、医学诊断等广泛应用至关重要。然而现有方法常因计算成本高且在稀疏、噪声数据下表现不佳。我们提出一种全前馈框架，直接从多视角图像回归尺度归一化的体积、表面积及其不确定性。通过图结构解码器融合三维点云重建与视图对齐的二维特征，模型绕过迭代优化，确保卓越的可扩展性与快速推理。实验表明，该方法优于现有最先进方法，尤其在输入图像数量较少时表现突出。经珊瑚监测、饮食分析与人体测量验证，本框架为定量形状分析提供鲁棒、可适应的解决方案，为从视觉数据中实现高精度几何估计提供高速、可扩展的替代方案，即使在资源受限或稀疏视角场景下仍保持高性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate and efficient 3D volume and surface estimation in applications like marine ecology and medical diagnostics, this work introduces a lightweight, fully feed-forward neural framework that directly regresses scale-normalized volume, surface area, and their uncertainties from multi-view images. The method fuses 3D point cloud reconstructions with view-aligned 2D features via a graph-based decoder, eliminating iterative optimization for faster inference and scalability. Experiments show it outperforms existing methods—especially under sparse or noisy input conditions—and achieves high accuracy across coral monitoring, dietary analysis, and anthropometry tasks, demonstrating robustness and adaptability in resource-constrained settings.
为满足海洋生态与医学诊断等领域对高效准确三维体积与表面积估计的需求，本文提出一种轻量级全前馈神经框架，直接从多视角图像回归尺度归一化的体积、表面积及其不确定性。该方法通过图结构解码器融合三维点云重建与视角对齐的二维特征，避免迭代优化，实现快速推理与高可扩展性。实验表明，该方法在输入图像稀疏时表现优于现有方法，并在珊瑚监测、饮食分析与人体测量等任务中保持稳定高性能。
Can AI Detect Life? Lessons from Artificial Life
Authors: Ankit Gupta, Christoph Adami
First: 2026-04-13T18:05:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:37:37+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 7 figures. Proceedings of Alife 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern machine learning methods have been proposed to detect life in extraterrestrial samples, drawing on their ability to distinguish biotic from abiotic samples based on training models using natural and synthetic organic molecular mixtures. Here we show using Artificial Life that such methods are easily fooled into detecting life with near 100% confidence even if the analyzed sample is not capable of life. This is due to modern machine learning methods' propensity to be easily fooled by out-of-distribution samples. Because extra-terrestrial samples are very likely out of the distribution provided by terrestrial biotic and abiotic samples, using AI methods for life detection is likely to yield significant false positives.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能能检测生命吗？来自人工生命的启示
现代机器学习方法被提出用于检测地外样本中的生命，其依据是通过训练模型区分生物与非生物样本，使用天然和合成有机分子混合物。本文通过人工生命实验表明，此类方法极易被欺骗，即使分析样本不具备生命能力，仍能以近100%置信度误判为生命。这是由于现代机器学习方法易受分布外样本误导。鉴于地外样本极可能超出地球生物与非生物样本的分布范围，使用AI方法进行生命检测很可能产生大量假阳性结果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing use of machine learning to detect life in extraterrestrial samples, this study investigates whether such AI methods can reliably distinguish biotic from abiotic materials. Using Artificial Life simulations, the researchers demonstrate that modern ML models can confidently classify synthetic, non-living samples as life-like with near 100% accuracy, due to their susceptibility to being misled by out-of-distribution data. Since extraterrestrial samples are likely to fall outside the training distribution of Earth-based biological and abiotic data, the study concludes that AI-based life detection is prone to generating substantial false positives.
鉴于机器学习在检测地外生命中的应用日益增多，本研究探讨此类AI方法能否可靠区分生物与非生物样本。通过人工生命模拟，研究人员发现，训练于地球有机混合物的现代机器学习模型，能以近100%置信度将合成的非生命样本误判为生命体。这是因为模型易受分布外样本误导，而地外样本很可能超出训练数据分布，导致实际应用中产生大量假阳性结果。
PISCES: Annotation-free Text-to-Video Post-Training via Optimal Transport-Aligned Rewards
Authors: Minh-Quan Le, Gaurav Mittal, Cheng Zhao, David Gu, Dimitris Samaras, Mei Chen
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-02T04:37:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:32:38+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026. Project page and code: https://roar-ai.github.io/pisces/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1 · Project2
Abstract
Text-to-video (T2V) generation aims to synthesize videos with high visual quality and temporal consistency that are semantically aligned with input text. Reward-based post-training has emerged as a promising direction to improve the quality and semantic alignment of generated videos. However, recent methods either rely on large-scale human preference annotations or operate on misaligned embeddings from pre-trained vision-language models, leading to limited scalability or suboptimal supervision. We present $\texttt{PISCES}$, an annotation-free post-training algorithm that addresses these limitations via a novel Dual Optimal Transport (OT)-aligned Rewards module. To align reward signals with human judgment, $\texttt{PISCES}$ uses OT to bridge text and video embeddings at both distributional and discrete token levels, enabling reward supervision to fulfill two objectives: (i) a Distributional OT-aligned Quality Reward that captures overall visual quality and temporal coherence; and (ii) a Discrete Token-level OT-aligned Semantic Reward that enforces semantic, spatio-temporal correspondence between text and video tokens. To our knowledge, $\texttt{PISCES}$ is the first to improve annotation-free reward supervision in generative post-training through the lens of OT. Experiments on both short- and long-video generation show that $\texttt{PISCES}$ outperforms both annotation-based and annotation-free methods on VBench across Quality and Semantic scores, with human preference studies further validating its effectiveness. We show that the Dual OT-aligned Rewards module is compatible with multiple optimization paradigms, including direct backpropagation and reinforcement learning fine-tuning. Project page: https://roar-ai.github.io/pisces
中文标题/摘要
标题：PISCES：通过最优传输对齐奖励实现无标注文本到视频的后训练
文本到视频（T2V）生成旨在合成视觉质量高、时间一致且与输入文本语义对齐的视频。基于奖励的后训练已成为提升生成视频质量和语义对齐度的有前景方向。然而，近期方法要么依赖大规模人工偏好标注，要么基于预训练视觉-语言模型中对齐不佳的嵌入，导致可扩展性受限或监督效果不佳。我们提出PISCES，一种无标注的后训练算法，通过新颖的双最优传输（OT）对齐奖励模块解决上述局限。为使奖励信号与人类判断对齐，PISCES利用OT在分布级和离散token级上桥接文本与视频嵌入，使奖励监督实现两个目标：(i) 分布级OT对齐质量奖励，捕捉整体视觉质量和时间连贯性；(ii) 离散token级OT对齐语义奖励，强制文本与视频token间语义和时空对应。据我们所知，PISCES是首个从最优传输视角提升生成式后训练中无标注奖励监督的方法。在短视频和长视频生成实验中，PISCES在VBench的Quality和Semantic评分上均优于有标注和无标注方法，人类偏好研究进一步验证其有效性。我们证明双OT对齐奖励模块兼容多种优化范式，包括直接反向传播和强化学习微调。项目页面：https://roar-ai.github.io/pisces
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing reward-based text-to-video post-training methods that rely on human annotations or misaligned embeddings, PISCES introduces an annotation-free approach using a Dual Optimal Transport (OT)-aligned Rewards module. It employs OT to align text and video embeddings at both distributional and discrete token levels, generating two reward signals: one for overall visual quality and temporal coherence, and another for semantic and spatio-temporal correspondence between text and video tokens. Experimental results on short- and long-video generation demonstrate that PISCES outperforms both annotation-based and annotation-free baselines on VBench metrics and human preference studies, while being compatible with multiple optimization frameworks including direct backpropagation and reinforcement learning.
为解决文本到视频生成中对大规模人工标注依赖、且现有无标注方法监督不足的问题，PISCES提出一种双最优传输（OT）对齐奖励模块，通过OT在分布级与离散token级对齐文本与视频嵌入，从而实现无标注的高质量监督。该模块包含两个奖励：分布级OT对齐质量奖励用于捕捉视觉与时间一致性，离散token级OT对齐语义奖励用于强化文本与视频在时空维度的语义对应。实验表明，PISCES在短视频与长视频生成任务上均优于有标注与无标注基线方法，在VBench质量与语义评分上表现最佳，且人类偏好研究进一步验证其有效性，同时该模块兼容直接反向传播与强化学习等多种优化范式。
TailorMind: Towards Preference-Aligned Multimodal Content Generation
Authors: Hengji Zhou, Ye Liu, Yufeng Liu, Si Wu, Lianghao Xia, Liqiang Nie
First: 2026-06-22T17:31:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:31:21+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 13 figures, 6 tables. Code available at https://github.com/iLearn-Lab/TailorMind
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Personalized content systems depend on available UGC and struggle when suitable content is absent, delayed, or costly to create. Although multimodal generators can synthesize content on demand, how to translate behavioral traces into generation-ready preferences remains underexplored. We study personalized multimodal content generation: creating user-tailored multimodal content without existing item pools or waiting for matching UGC. We propose TailorMind, linking collaborative preference modeling with controllable multimodal generation. TailorMind enriches sparse user histories via hypergraph collaborative filtering and optimizes textual profiles with ranking-error feedback and textual gradient descent. Retrieval-augmented style control grounds outputs in authentic UGC patterns, while cross-modal cohesion reflection reduces semantic drift. We construct TailorBench, a benchmark from three mainstream platforms evaluated along five dimensions: coherence, novelty, aesthetic, hallucination, profiling. Experiments show that TailorMind achieves competitive or stronger coherence, improves novelty and aesthetic quality over representative generation baselines and ground-truth UGC, demonstrating advantages over retrieving available content or comparable UGC, while achieving up to 29% Recall gains in reranking. Our code is released at: https://github.com/iLearn-Lab/TailorMind.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TailorMind：面向偏好对齐的多模态内容生成
个性化内容系统依赖现有用户生成内容（UGC），当合适内容缺失、延迟或成本高昂时表现不佳。尽管多模态生成器可按需合成内容，但如何将行为轨迹转化为生成所需的偏好仍属探索领域。本文研究个性化多模态内容生成：在无现有内容池或无需等待匹配UGC的情况下，生成用户定制化多模态内容。我们提出TailorMind，将协同偏好建模与可控多模态生成相结合。TailorMind通过超图协同过滤丰富稀疏用户历史，并结合排序误差反馈与文本梯度下降优化文本画像。检索增强的风格控制使输出贴近真实UGC模式，跨模态一致性反思则减少语义漂移。我们构建了TailorBench基准，涵盖三大主流平台，从连贯性、新颖性、美学、幻觉、画像五个维度评估。实验表明，TailorMind在连贯性上表现竞争力或更强，新颖性与美学质量优于代表性生成基线及真实UGC，相较检索现有内容或同类UGC更具优势，重排序召回率最高提升29%。代码已开源：https://github.com/iLearn-Lab/TailorMind。
Summary / 总结
TailorMind addresses the challenge of generating personalized multimodal content when existing user-generated content is scarce or unavailable, by translating user behavioral traces into generation-ready preferences. It combines collaborative preference modeling with controllable multimodal generation, using hypergraph filtering to enrich sparse histories and textual gradient descent to optimize user profiles. The system employs retrieval-augmented style control to ground outputs in authentic UGC patterns and cross-modal cohesion reflection to reduce semantic drift. Evaluated on TailorBench across five dimensions, TailorMind outperforms baselines in coherence, novelty, and aesthetic quality, and achieves up to 29% recall gains in reranking compared to retrieval-based methods.
TailorMind旨在解决在缺乏现有用户生成内容时生成个性化多模态内容的问题，通过将用户行为轨迹转化为可控生成偏好。其方法结合超图协同过滤增强稀疏用户历史，并通过文本梯度下降优化用户画像，同时采用检索增强风格控制与跨模态一致性反思机制，确保输出符合真实UGC模式并保持语义连贯。在TailorBench基准测试中，TailorMind在连贯性、新颖性和美学质量上优于主流基线模型，且相比检索方法在重排序中最高提升29%的召回率。
Flatness Preserves Instruction Following in Vision-Language-Action Models
Authors: Haochen Zhang, Yonatan Bisk
First: 2026-06-22T17:30:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:30:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-language-action (VLA) models have the potential for open-world generalization by leveraging pretrained vision-language representations, yet downstream finetuning on limited robot data often degrades these representations, leading to brittle policies that ignore language instructions in favor of visual shortcuts, a failure mode we term instruction blindness. We hypothesize that standard finetuning with limited data applies gradients to a sparse set of points, which manifests as a sharp loss landscape with high-curvature minima. We propose to address this directly through flatness-preserving optimization while finetuning on the exact same data, where learning a flatter landscape results in a model more robust to perturbations in the weight space. Specifically, we demonstrate that simply applying sharpness-aware minimization during VLA finetuning significantly improves instruction following by over 60% across multiple simulation and real-world benchmarks without additional data, architectural modification, or retraining. We further analyze the effect of selective sharpness, quantify its effects, and show that our approach is complementary to existing guidance techniques. Project page can be found at https://haochenz11.github.io/papers/flatness-vla/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：平坦性可保持视觉-语言-动作模型对指令的遵循
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型可通过利用预训练的视觉-语言表示实现开放世界泛化，但下游微调通常在有限的机器人数据上进行，这会损害这些表示，导致模型倾向于忽略语言指令而采用视觉捷径，我们称之为指令盲。我们假设标准微调在有限数据下仅对稀疏点施加梯度，表现为具有高曲率极小值的尖锐损失景观。我们提出在微调过程中直接通过保持平坦性的优化方法解决此问题，学习更平坦的损失景观可使模型对权重空间中的扰动更具鲁棒性。具体而言，我们证明仅在VLA微调中应用尖锐感知最小化，即可在多个仿真与真实世界基准测试中无需额外数据、架构修改或重新训练，显著提升指令遵循能力60%以上。我们进一步分析选择性尖锐度的影响，量化其效果，并表明该方法与现有引导技术互补。项目页面见 https://haochenz11.github.io/papers/flatness-vla/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the degradation of instruction-following in vision-language-action models due to brittle policies that favor visual shortcuts over language, the authors propose flatness-preserving optimization during finetuning to mitigate this issue. They hypothesize that standard finetuning with limited data leads to sharp loss landscapes and high-curvature minima, causing instruction blindness. Their method applies sharpness-aware minimization during VLA finetuning without altering data, architecture, or retraining, resulting in models that follow instructions more robustly. Experiments show over 60% improvement in instruction following across multiple simulation and real-world benchmarks, with additional analysis confirming the complementary nature of selective sharpness to existing methods.
为解决视觉-语言-动作模型在有限数据微调后因偏好视觉捷径而忽视语言指令的问题，作者提出在微调过程中采用平坦性保持优化方法。他们假设标准微调会导致损失函数景观尖锐、极值曲率高，从而引发指令盲。该方法在微调中引入尖锐感知最小化，无需修改架构或增加数据，使损失函数更平坦。实验表明，该方法在多个仿真与真实世界基准上使指令遵循能力提升超60%，且可与现有引导方法协同使用。
Learning Process Rewards via Success Visitation Matching for Efficient RL
Authors: Raymond Tsao, Andrew Wagenmaker, Sergey Levine
First: 2026-06-22T17:30:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:30:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In many modern applications of reinforcement learning (RL), the natural reward for a task of interest is inherently sparse: a reward of 0 is given everywhere except when the task is completed, when a reward of +1 is given. Training a policy to maximize such a sparse reward requires solving a challenging credit assignment problem, leading to slow or ineffective RL improvement. We propose a simple approach to transform a sparse outcome reward into a dense process reward. Our approach relies on training a discriminator to distinguish between previous successful and unsuccessful episodes, and using this discriminator to incentivize the RL-learned policy to match the state-action visitations of successful episodes, while avoiding those of unsuccessful episodes. By incentivizing the policy to match the visitations over all states, not just those that correspond to task success, this reward provides dense feedback on whether progress is being made towards task completion, and, we show, provably achieves this without changing the optimal policy. Focusing on finetuning of robotic control policies, we demonstrate that our approach leads to significantly faster RL finetuning performance on both simulated and real-world manipulation tasks, as compared to simply maximizing the sparse outcome reward.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过成功访问匹配实现学习过程奖励以提高强化学习效率
在许多现代强化学习（RL）应用中，目标任务的自然奖励固然是稀疏的：除任务完成时给予+1奖励外，其余情况均为0奖励。训练策略以最大化此类稀疏奖励需解决复杂的信用分配问题，导致RL改进缓慢或无效。我们提出一种简单方法，将稀疏结果奖励转化为密集过程奖励。该方法依赖于训练一个判别器以区分先前成功与失败的回合，并利用该判别器激励RL学习的策略模仿成功回合的状态-动作访问模式，同时避免失败回合的访问模式。通过激励策略匹配所有状态的访问模式，而不仅限于任务成功对应的状态，该奖励可提供密集反馈，判断是否正朝着任务完成推进，且我们证明其在不改变最优策略的前提下可实现此目标。聚焦于机器人控制策略的微调，我们表明，与仅最大化稀疏结果奖励相比，本方法在模拟和真实世界操作任务中均显著提升了RL微调性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of training RL agents under sparse reward signals, this work proposes a method to convert sparse outcome rewards into dense process rewards by training a discriminator to distinguish successful from unsuccessful episodes. The RL policy is then guided to mimic the state-action visitations of successful episodes while avoiding those of unsuccessful ones, enabling continuous feedback on progress toward task completion without altering the optimal policy. Experiments on robotic control tasks show that this approach significantly accelerates RL finetuning in both simulation and real-world settings compared to direct sparse reward maximization.
为了解决稀疏奖励下强化学习训练效率低的问题，该文提出通过训练一个判别器区分成功与失败的轨迹，再引导策略模仿成功轨迹的状态-动作访问模式、避开失败轨迹，从而将稀疏奖励转化为密集过程奖励。该方法在不改变最优策略的前提下，提供持续的任务进展反馈。实验表明，该方法在仿真与真实机器人控制任务中，显著加速了强化学习微调过程。
Muown Implicitly Performs Angular Step-size Decay
Authors: Florian Hübler, Kai Lion, Antonio Orvieto, Niao He
First: 2026-06-22T17:28:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:28:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Matrix-aware optimizers such as Muon and Muown have recently shown strong empirical performance for pre-training Transformers. In particular, Muown separates each weight matrix into row magnitudes and an un-normalized direction variable, updating the former with Adam and the latter with Muon. We show that the directional update of Muown is equivalent to a Riemannian step on the normalized directions, while the magnitude of the un-normalized parameterization only modulates the angular step size. This explains the step-size stability of Muown and suggests making the angular step size explicit. The resulting method, AngularMuown, optimizes directly over the normalized directions and uses a schedulable angular multiplier decoupled from the radial magnitude update. AngularMuown improves over Muown and, at the time of writing, a preliminary version is leading the per-optimizer category of the modded nanoGPT speedrunning competition. Further experiments on Qwen2-0.5B, and 1.1B parameter mixture-of-experts models confirm the algorithm scales beyond small models. An implementation of the algorithm is available at https://github.com/fhueb/angular-muown
中文标题/摘要
标题：Muown隐式执行角步长衰减
近期，矩阵感知优化器如Muon和Muown在Transformer预训练中展现出强大的实证性能。特别是，Muown将每个权重矩阵拆分为行幅值和未归一化方向变量，前者用Adam更新，后者用Muon更新。我们证明，Muown的方向更新等价于归一化方向上的黎曼步长，而未归一化参数化中的幅值仅调节角步长。这解释了Muown的步长稳定性，并建议显式设定角步长。由此产生的方法AngularMuown直接优化归一化方向，并使用与径向幅值更新解耦的可调度角乘数。AngularMuown优于Muown，截至本文撰写时，其初步版本在修改版nanoGPT速度竞赛中领先于所有优化器类别。在Qwen2-0.5B和1.1B参数的专家混合模型上的进一步实验验证了该算法可扩展至大型模型。算法实现见https://github.com/fhueb/angular-muown
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the empirical success of matrix-aware optimizers like Muown, this work investigates why Muown’s directional update stabilizes step sizes by showing it implicitly performs angular step-size decay on normalized directions. The authors derive that the un-normalized parameterization in Muown modulates angular step size without affecting direction, leading to a decoupled update strategy. They propose AngularMuown, which explicitly optimizes over normalized directions using a schedulable angular multiplier, separating angular and radial updates. Experiments show AngularMuown outperforms Muown on small models and scales to larger architectures like Qwen2-0.5B and 1.1B MoE models, with a preliminary version leading the modded nanoGPT speedrunning competition.
为了解释矩阵感知优化器如Muown在Transformer预训练中表现优异的原因，本文研究其方向更新为何具有稳定步长。作者发现Muown的方向更新等价于在归一化方向上的黎曼步，而其幅度更新隐式调节角步长。为此，他们提出AngularMuown，直接在归一化方向上优化，并使用可调度的角乘数与径向幅度更新解耦。实验表明，AngularMuown优于Muown，并在modded nanoGPT速度赛中领先，且在Qwen2-0.5B和1.1B MoE等更大模型上同样有效。
Meta-learning ecological priors from large language models explains human learning and decision making
Authors: Akshay K. Jagadish, Mirko Thalmann, Julian Coda-Forno, Marcel Binz, Eric Schulz
First: 2025-08-28T16:07:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:28:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human cognition is profoundly shaped by the environments in which it unfolds. Yet, it remains an open question whether learning and decision making can be explained as a principled adaptation to the statistical structure of real-world tasks. We introduce ecologically rational analysis, a computational framework that unifies the normative foundations of rational analysis with ecological grounding. Leveraging large language models to generate ecologically valid cognitive tasks at scale, and using meta-learning to derive rational models optimized for these environments, we develop a new class of learning algorithms: Ecologically Rational Meta-learned Inference (ERMI). ERMI internalizes the statistical regularities of naturalistic problem spaces and adapts flexibly to novel situations, without requiring hand-crafted heuristics or explicit parameter updates. We show that ERMI captures human behavior across 15 experiments spanning function learning, category learning, and decision making, outperforming several established cognitive models in trial-by-trial prediction. Our results suggest that much of human cognition may reflect adaptive alignment to the ecological structure of the problems we encounter in everyday life.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从大型语言模型中元学习生态先验可解释人类学习与决策
人类认知深受其发生环境的影响。然而，学习与决策是否可被解释为对现实任务统计结构的原理性适应，仍是一个开放问题。我们引入生态理性分析，这是一种将理性分析的规范基础与生态学基础统一的计算框架。通过利用大型语言模型大规模生成生态有效的认知任务，并借助元学习推导出针对这些环境优化的理性模型，我们开发了一类新型学习算法：生态理性元学习推理（ERMI）。ERMI 内化了自然问题空间的统计规律性，并能灵活适应新情境，无需人工设计启发式规则或显式参数更新。我们在涵盖函数学习、类别学习和决策的15项实验中展示了ERMI对人类行为的捕捉能力，其逐试预测性能优于多个现有认知模型。我们的结果表明，人类认知的许多方面可能反映了对日常生活中所遇问题生态结构的适应性调整。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether human learning and decision-making can be understood as adaptive responses to the statistical structure of real-world environments. It proposes Ecologically Rational Meta-learned Inference (ERMI), a novel algorithm derived via meta-learning from large language models that generate ecologically valid cognitive tasks. ERMI learns to internalize the statistical regularities of naturalistic problem spaces and adapts flexibly to new situations without manual heuristics or parameter tuning. Experimental results across 15 tasks in function, category, and decision learning show ERMI outperforms several established cognitive models in trial-by-trial prediction, suggesting human cognition may largely reflect principled alignment with ecological task structures.
该研究探讨人类学习与决策是否可视为对现实世界任务统计结构的适应性响应。研究提出了一种名为“生态理性元学习推理”（ERMI）的新学习框架，该框架通过元学习从大语言模型生成的生态有效认知任务中推导得出，能内化自然问题空间的统计规律，并灵活适应新情境，无需人工设计启发式规则或显式参数调整。在涵盖函数学习、类别学习和决策的15项实验中，ERMI在逐试预测上持续优于多个经典认知模型，表明人类认知可能很大程度上反映了对日常任务生态结构的适应性调整。
GeoTransolver: Learning Physics on Irregular Domains Using Multi-scale Geometry Aware Physics Attention Transformer
Authors: Corey Adams, Rishikesh Ranade, Ram Cherukuri, Sanjay Choudhry
First: 2025-12-23T14:40:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:26:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present GeoTransolver, a multiscale geometry-aware physics attention transformer for Computer Aided Engineering (CAE). GeoTransolver extends the Transolver backbone with GALE (Geometry-Aware Latent Embeddings) attention, which pairs physics-aware self-attention on learned state slices with cross-attention to a shared geometry and global context computed via multi-scale ball queries (inspired by Domino) and reused in every block. Implemented and released in NVIDIA PhysicsNeMo, GeoTransolver persistently projects geometry and global parameters, into physical state spaces to anchor computations to domain structure and operating regimes. We benchmark on DrivAerML, SHIFT-SUV, and SHIFT-Wing against Domino, Transolver (PhysicsNeMo implementation), and literature-reported AB-UPT, evaluating drag/lift R2 and relative L1 errors on field variables. As an additional nonlinear structural mechanics application, we also report Transolver and GeoTransolver results on bumper-beam and full-vehicle Body-in-White (BIW) crash-dynamics benchmarks, evaluating relative L2 trajectory error and probe-level kinematic MSE. GeoTransolver delivers improved accuracy, robustness to geometry and regime shifts, and favorable data efficiency; we include DrivAerML ablations and qualitative contour and design-trend results, advancing operator learning for high-fidelity surrogates on complex, irregular, non-linear domains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GeoTransolver：使用多尺度几何感知物理注意力变换器在不规则域上学习物理
我们提出了GeoTransolver，一种用于计算机辅助工程（CAE）的多尺度几何感知物理注意力变换器。GeoTransolver在Transolver主干架构上扩展了GALE（几何感知潜在嵌入）注意力机制，该机制将物理感知的自注意力应用于学习到的状态切片，并与通过多尺度球查询（受Domino启发）计算并每块重复使用的共享几何结构和全局上下文进行交叉注意力。GeoTransolver在NVIDIA PhysicsNeMo中实现并发布，持续将几何和全局参数投影到物理状态空间，以锚定计算至域结构和运行工况。我们在DrivAerML、SHIFT-SUV和SHIFT-Wing上与Domino、Transolver（PhysicsNeMo实现）及文献报道的AB-UPT进行基准测试，评估阻力/升力R2和场变量相对L1误差。作为额外的非线性结构力学应用，我们还在保险杠梁和整车白车身（BIW）碰撞动力学基准上报告了Transolver和GeoTransolver的结果，评估相对L2轨迹误差和探针级运动学MSE。GeoTransolver实现了更高的精度、对几何和工况变化的鲁棒性以及良好的数据效率；我们包含DrivAerML消融实验和定性等值线与设计趋势结果，推动在复杂、不规则、非线性域上高保真代理模型的操作者学习。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for high-fidelity physics modeling on irregular and nonlinear engineering domains, GeoTransolver introduces a multi-scale geometry-aware attention mechanism into a transformer architecture. It combines physics-aware self-attention with cross-attention to shared geometry and global context, computed via multi-scale ball queries, to anchor computations to domain structure. Evaluated on aerodynamic and structural benchmarks including DrivAerML, SHIFT-SUV, SHIFT-Wing, bumper-beam, and BIW crash dynamics, GeoTransolver outperforms baseline models like Domino and Transolver in drag/lift R2 and relative L1/L2 errors, demonstrating improved accuracy, robustness to geometry and regime shifts, and better data efficiency.
为解决计算机辅助工程中不规则复杂域上的物理建模需求，GeoTransolver在Transolver架构基础上引入了多尺度几何感知注意力机制。其GALE注意力结合物理感知自注意力与跨注意力，通过多尺度球查询构建共享几何与全局上下文，使计算持续锚定于域结构与运行工况。在气动（DrivAerML、SHIFT-SUV、SHIFT-Wing）与结构碰撞（缓冲梁、整车白车身）基准测试中，GeoTransolver优于Domino与Transolver基线模型，实现更高的升阻R²、更低的L1/L2误差，且对几何与工况变化更鲁棒，同时展现更优的数据效率。
Pose Anything Anywhere:Model-free Object Poses from Arbitrary References
Authors: Hongli Xu, Jiaqi Hu, Junwen Huang, Boyang Zhong, Peter KT Yu, Nassir Navab, Benjamin Busam, Slobodan Ilic
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-22T17:23:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:23:57+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Estimating the 6D pose of unseen objects is a fundamental yet challenging problem for open-world robotics and embodied perception. Model-based methods are accurate but depend on CAD assets or heavy onboarding, while most model-free approaches are still limited to pairwise single-anchor matching and thus fail under occlusion and large viewpoint changes with low query-reference overlap. Therefore, we present PANY, a unified model-free framework that seamlessly supports both RGB and RGB-D inputs, operates on one or sparse pose-free reference views, and generalizes effectively to novel objects. Built on a multi-view transformer geometry backbone, PANY moves beyond pairwise matching by learning view-consistent geometry and cross-view alignment cues that remain stable under wide baselines and limited overlap. When additional unposed assist views are available, PANY aggregates them via pose-graph canonical registration to increase geometric coverage and reinforce the final pose. Extensive experiments show that PANY achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks, substantially outperforming existing model-free methods, improving pose accuracy by +12% on YCB-V and over +20% on LM-O. Furthermore, PANY consistently performs well under both single-reference and sparse-reference settings, demonstrating strong robustness in real-world environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：任意位置姿态估计：无需模型的任意参考物姿态估计
估计未见过物体的6D姿态是开放世界机器人和具身感知中的基础且具有挑战性的问题。基于模型的方法虽然准确，但依赖CAD资产或需要大量预处理，而大多数无模型方法仍受限于成对单锚点匹配，因此在遮挡和大视角变化下，当查询与参考视图重叠度低时会失效。为此，我们提出了PANY，一个统一的无模型框架，无缝支持RGB和RGB-D输入，可在单个或稀疏的无姿态参考视图上运行，并能有效泛化至新物体。PANY基于多视角Transformer几何主干构建，超越了成对匹配，通过学习视图一致的几何结构和跨视图对齐线索，在大基线和有限重叠下保持稳定。当有额外的无姿态辅助视图时，PANY通过姿态图规范配准聚合它们，以增加几何覆盖并强化最终姿态。大量实验表明，PANY在多个基准测试中达到最先进性能，显著优于现有无模型方法，在YCB-V上姿态精度提升12%，在LM-O上提升超过20%。此外，PANY在单参考和稀疏参考设置下均表现稳定，展现出在真实环境中的强鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate 6D pose estimation of unseen objects without relying on CAD models or heavy onboarding, PANY introduces a model-free framework that works with arbitrary reference views and supports both RGB and RGB-D inputs. It leverages a multi-view transformer backbone to learn view-consistent geometry and cross-view alignment cues, enabling robust performance even under occlusion and large viewpoint changes with limited overlap. When assist views are available, PANY uses pose-graph canonical registration to enhance geometric coverage and refine pose estimates. Experiments show PANY achieves state-of-the-art results, improving pose accuracy by +12% on YCB-V and over +20% on LM-O, and maintains strong performance under both single-reference and sparse-reference conditions.
为解决无CAD模型依赖、适用于任意参考视角的6D姿态估计难题，PANY提出一种统一的无模型框架，支持RGB或RGB-D输入及稀疏无姿态参考视图。其基于多视角Transformer结构，学习跨视角几何一致性与对齐线索，提升在遮挡和大视角变化下的鲁棒性。若有辅助视图，通过姿态图规范配准增强几何覆盖。实验表明，PANY在多个基准上达到最先进性能，YCB-V上精度提升12%，LM-O上提升超20%，且在单参考与稀疏参考场景下均表现稳定。
AI Exposure Scores: what they measure, what they miss, and what comes next
Authors: Campbell Lund, Thomas Euyang, Zanele Munyikwa, Marzieh Fadaee
First: 2026-06-22T17:23:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:23:33+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A set of exposure scores calculated in 2023 has become a central empirical input to the future of work debate. Produced by Eloundou et al. (2023) and referred to here as the GPTs are GPTs scores, they define exposure as the share of occupational tasks a large language model can assist with. This work is a genuine methodological contribution, but as the scores travel from the time and place they were produced, the limitations the authors named do not always travel with them. Two gaps have widened as a result. The first is structural, between what static exposure scores measure and what policy questions actually require. Taking the diffusion of these scores as a case study, we show how their temporal, geographic, and ontological limitations compound in policy-facing analyses, and we survey five families of research responding to these limits: dynamic and benchmark-based measures, ensemble methods, task-framework extensions, worker-centered metrics, and adoption and usage data. The second gap is the one we argue needs more attention: the coordination between researchers and policymakers. The policy-relevant work which ask who is harmed, who benefits, how, and when, continues to reference the static GPTs are GPTs scores without engagement with the methodological updates that would let these questions be answered more reliably. We then ask what additional steps towards navigating uncertainty remain: ex-post frameworks and the deliberate, political work of reimagining what futures are worthy of building towards are. Closing the research-policy gap is a shared task: policymakers must widen their evidence base, engage workers as epistemic partners, and shift from prediction to preparedness; researchers must build data infrastructure, adopt participatory methods, and write with policymakers in mind. Better measurement matters, but it will not close the second gap alone.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AI暴露评分：它们衡量什么、遗漏什么以及未来方向
2023年计算的一组暴露评分已成为关于未来工作辩论的核心实证输入。由Eloundou等人（2023）制作并在此称为GPTs评分的评分，将暴露定义为大型语言模型可协助完成的职业任务占比。这项工作是真正的方法论贡献，但当这些评分从其产生的时间和地点传播开来时，作者所指出的局限性并不总能随之传播。结果导致两个差距扩大。第一个是结构性的，即静态暴露评分所衡量的内容与政策实际所需之间存在差距。以这些评分的扩散为案例研究，我们展示了其时间、地理和本体论局限性如何在面向政策的分析中叠加，同时概述了五类应对这些局限的研究：动态与基准型度量、集成方法、任务框架扩展、以劳动者为中心的指标以及采用与使用数据。第二个差距是我们认为需要更多关注的：研究者与政策制定者之间的协调。持续引用静态GPTs评分、却未与方法论更新互动的政策相关研究，仍未能回答“谁受伤害、谁受益、如何以及何时”等关键问题。我们随后探讨了在应对不确定性方面仍需采取的额外步骤：事后框架以及有意识的政治性工作，即重新构想值得建设的未来。弥合研究-政策鸿沟是共同任务：政策制定者必须拓宽证据基础，将劳动者视为知识伙伴，并从预测转向准备；研究者必须构建数据基础设施，采用参与式方法，并以政策制定者为对象撰写研究。更好的测量很重要，但仅靠它无法单独弥合第二个差距。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing influence of static AI exposure scores in policy debates about the future of work, this paper critiques their methodological limitations and their failure to address evolving policy needs. It reviews five emerging research approaches—dynamic measures, ensemble methods, task-framework extensions, worker-centered metrics, and adoption data—to overcome the scores’ temporal, geographic, and ontological constraints. The authors argue that while better measurement is essential, the deeper issue lies in the lack of coordination between researchers and policymakers, who continue to rely on outdated scores without engaging with methodological improvements. The paper concludes that closing this gap requires both sides to adopt participatory methods, expand evidence bases, and shift from predictive modeling to preparedness strategies.
本文旨在批判2023年发布的静态AI暴露得分在政策讨论中的局限性及其未能回应政策实际需求的问题。文章综述了五类方法改进方向——动态测量、集成方法、任务框架扩展、以劳动者为中心的指标及采纳数据——以克服这些得分在时间、地理和本体论上的缺陷。研究发现，尽管这些方法改进提升了测量精度，政策分析仍普遍依赖过时的得分，未与新方法结合。文章指出，弥合研究与政策之间的鸿沟需双方协作：政策制定者应拓展证据基础并纳入劳动者参与，研究者则需构建参与式数据基础设施并面向政策需求设计研究。
AI-driven Optimisation of Quality of Recovery (QoR) in Remote Patient Monitoring
Authors: Yansong Liu, Li-Hsi, Lin, Pramit Khetrapal, Ronnie Stafford, John Kelly, Ivana Drobnjak
First: 2026-06-22T17:23:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:23:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Remote patient monitoring depends on patient-reported data to capture the subjective dimension of recovery that devices cannot measure. The Quality of Recovery (QoR-15) survey is the gold-standard instrument for this purpose. It was designed and validated for occasional in-hospital assessment, yet remote monitoring now administers it to patients daily. In our own post-surgical deployment, only 55% of patients submitted the survey more than 14 days of 30 monitoring days. We developed QoR-compact, a five-item daily input for the RPM prediction pathway. Setting a deployment-driven target of one-third of the daily items, we exhaustively evaluated all 3,003 five-question subsets of the QoR-15 and tested whether the best of them matches the full instrument in predicting near-term postoperative recovery severity. QoR-compact achieves a mean AUC-ROC of 0.968 (95% CI 0.915-0.988), statistically comparable to the 0.964 baseline obtained with one-third of the items. Patient-level backtesting indicates that it tracks readmission events as faithfully as the full form. Its five items span the physical and psychological axes of recovery: Q3 (feeling rested), Q9 (feeling comfortable and in control), Q10 (general well-being), Q12 (severe pain), and Q14 (feeling worried or anxious). The QoR-15 remains the gold-standard measure of recovery; QoR-compact complements it as a shorter daily input designed for prediction. This parity provides the basis for a prospective study of whether a lighter daily input is, in turn, completed more consistently. External validation on larger cohorts is required before clinical use.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能驱动的远程患者监测中恢复质量（QoR）的优化
远程患者监测依赖患者自报数据以捕捉设备无法测量的主观恢复维度。恢复质量（QoR-15）问卷是该目的的金标准工具，其设计和验证用于偶尔的住院评估，但远程监测现每日向患者施测。在我们自己的术后部署中，仅55%的患者在30天监测期内提交问卷超过14天。我们开发了QoR-compact，一种五项每日输入的远程监测预测路径工具。设定以部署驱动的目标为每日项目数的三分之一，我们全面评估了QoR-15所有3003个五题子集，并测试其中最优者是否与完整工具在预测近期术后恢复严重程度方面相当。QoR-compact的平均AUC-ROC为0.968（95%CI 0.915-0.988），统计上与使用三分之一题项获得的0.964基线相当。患者层面回测表明，其对再入院事件的追踪与完整问卷一致。其五项涵盖恢复的身心维度：Q3（感觉休息好）、Q9（感觉舒适且有掌控感）、Q10（总体健康状况）、Q12（剧烈疼痛）和Q14（感觉担忧或焦虑）。QoR-15仍是恢复质量的金标准测量工具；QoR-compact作为更短的每日输入工具，用于预测。此等效性为前瞻性研究提供了基础，即更轻量的每日输入是否能更一致完成。在更大队列中进行外部验证后方可用于临床。
Summary / 总结
Driven by low patient compliance with the full QoR-15 survey in remote monitoring, the study developed QoR-compact — a five-item subset — to enable daily input while preserving predictive power. By exhaustively evaluating all possible five-item combinations from the 15-item QoR-15 and selecting the optimal one, the researchers found that QoR-compact achieved an AUC-ROC of 0.968, statistically comparable to the full instrument’s 0.964 when using one-third of its items. Patient-level backtesting confirmed its fidelity in tracking readmission risk, and the five items cover key physical and psychological recovery dimensions. While QoR-15 remains the gold standard, QoR-compact offers a practical daily alternative for prediction, with potential to improve adherence if validated in larger cohorts.
为解决远程监测中患者每日填写QoR-15问卷依从性低的问题，研究者开发了五项简化版QoR-compact问卷，并通过穷举所有可能的五项组合，筛选出预测效果最优的版本。该版本包含覆盖身体与心理恢复维度的五个问题，其AUC-ROC值达0.968，与完整版0.964无显著差异，且在患者层面能准确追踪再入院事件。这表明简化版问卷可作为可靠的每日输入用于预测建模，但临床应用前仍需外部验证。
Why Machines Misread Pedagogical Quality: Human-Machine Alignment in LLM-Based Pretest Question Evaluation
Authors: Pei-Yu Tseng, Mahir Akgun, Peng Liu
First: 2026-06-22T17:22:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:22:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Designing effective pretest questions is challenging at scale: high-quality questions require careful calibration of openness, cognitive depth, and alignment with learning objectives, yet generating and evaluating them manually is time-consuming. We present an AI-assisted workflow for pretest question development that combines automated generation, rubric-based evaluation, and iterative selection. Because the workflow relies on machine evaluation to filter questions at scale, we investigate the alignment between human and machine judgments across a 2x2 design varying rubric operationalization and evaluation mode. Our findings show that human-machine disagreements are systematic rather than random, that rubric revision has a larger effect on alignment than rationale-first evaluation, and that the two interventions are complementary. These findings highlight that scalable AI-assisted pretesting depends not only on generation capability but on how pedagogical quality is operationalized for machine interpretation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：为何机器误判教学质量：基于大语言模型的预测试题评估中的人机对齐
大规模设计有效的预测试题具有挑战性：高质量试题需精细校准开放性、认知深度与学习目标的契合度，但人工生成与评估耗时。我们提出一种AI辅助的预测试题开发工作流，结合自动化生成、基于评分标准的评估与迭代筛选。因该工作流依赖机器评估大规模筛选试题，我们通过2x2设计（评分标准操作化与评估模式）探究人机判断间的对齐情况。研究发现：人机分歧具有系统性而非随机性；评分标准修订对对齐的影响大于先提供理由的评估方式；两种干预措施互补。这些发现表明，可扩展的AI辅助预测试依赖的不仅是生成能力，更在于教学质量如何被操作化以供机器理解。

The study addresses the challenge of efficiently generating high-quality pretest questions by developing an AI-assisted workflow that integrates automated question generation, rubric-based evaluation, and iterative selection. It investigates human-machine alignment across a 2x2 experimental design varying rubric operationalization and evaluation mode, revealing that disagreements are systematic rather than random, that revising rubrics has a greater impact on alignment than rationale-first evaluation, and that the two interventions work best together. 该研究旨在解决大规模生成高质量预测试题的难题，提出了一种结合自动生题、基于评分标准的评估与迭代筛选的AI辅助工作流。研究通过2x2设计比较不同评分标准表述与评估模式下人机判断的一致性，发现分歧具有系统性而非随机性，修订评分标准比先提供理由的评估方式对提升一致性更有效，且二者可互补。结果表明，可扩展的AI辅助预测试题开发不仅依赖生成能力，更取决于教学质量如何被转化为机器可理解的评估标准。
ViMedCSS: A Vietnamese Medical Code-Switching Speech Dataset & Benchmark
Authors: Tung X. Nguyen, Nhu Vo, Giang-Son Nguyen, Duy Mai Hoang, Chien Dinh Huynh, Inigo Jauregi Unanue, Massimo Piccardi, Wray Buntine, Dung D. Le
First: 2026-02-13T13:17:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:21:53+00:00
Comments: Accepted at LREC 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Code-switching (CS), which is when Vietnamese speech uses English words like drug names or procedures, is a common phenomenon in Vietnamese medical communication. This creates challenges for Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) systems, especially in low-resource languages like Vietnamese. Current most ASR systems struggle to recognize correctly English medical terms within Vietnamese sentences, and no benchmark addresses this challenge. In this paper, we construct a 34-hour Vietnamese Medical Code-Switching Speech dataset (ViMedCSS) containing 16,576 utterances. Each utterance includes at least one English medical term drawn from a curated bilingual lexicon covering five medical topics. Using this dataset, we evaluate several state-of-the-art ASR models and examine different specific fine-tuning strategies for improving medical term recognition to investigate the best approach to solve in the dataset. Experimental results show that Vietnamese-optimized models perform better on general segments, while multilingual pretraining helps capture English insertions. The combination of both approaches yields the best balance between overall and code-switched accuracy. This work provides the first benchmark for Vietnamese medical code-switching and offers insights into effective domain adaptation for low-resource, multilingual ASR systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ViMedCSS：越南语医学语码转换语音数据集与基准
语码转换（CS）是指越南语口语中使用英语词汇（如药品名称或程序术语），在越南医学交流中十分常见。这给自动语音识别（ASR）系统带来挑战，尤其对资源匮乏语言如越南语而言。当前大多数ASR系统难以正确识别越南语句子中的英语医学术语，且尚无基准测试针对此挑战。本文构建了一个34小时的越南语医学语码转换语音数据集（ViMedCSS），包含16,576个语句，每个语句至少包含一个来自五类医学主题精选双语词典中的英语医学术语。利用该数据集，我们评估了多个最先进的ASR模型，并研究不同特定微调策略以提升医学术语识别效果，旨在探索最佳解决方案。实验结果表明，针对越南语优化的模型在普通段落表现更优，而多语言预训练有助于捕捉英语插入内容。两种方法结合可实现整体准确率与语码转换准确率的最佳平衡。本工作首次为越南语医学语码转换提供基准，并为低资源、多语言ASR系统的有效领域适应提供见解。

为应对越南语医疗场景中频繁出现的英语医学术语嵌入所导致的语音识别难题，本研究构建了首个越南语医疗代码切换语音数据集ViMedCSS，包含34小时、16576条含至少一个英语医学术语的语句。研究评估了多种主流语音识别模型，并测试不同微调策略以提升英语术语识别效果。实验表明，越南语优化模型在普通语句识别上表现更优，而多语言预训练更擅长捕捉代码切换内容；二者结合策略在整体与代码切换准确率上达到最佳平衡，为低资源多语言语音识别的领域适配提供了参考。
Diffusion Models Adapt to Low-Dimensional Structure Under Flexible Coefficient Choices
Authors: Changxiao Cai, Yuchen Jiao, Gen Li
First: 2026-06-22T17:20:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:20:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion models are known to exploit unknown low-dimensional structure to accelerate sampling. However, existing convergence theory under low-dimensional data structure has largely focused on update rules with narrowly prescribed coefficient choices. This raises a fundamental question: is adaptation to low-dimensional structure sensitive to the precise choice of update coefficients? In this paper, we show that such adaptation is a robust property of diffusion models. For a broad class of update coefficients, we prove that $\widetilde{O}(k/\varepsilon)$ iterations suffice to generate an $\varepsilon$-accurate sample in total variation (TV) distance, independently of the ambient dimension. Our framework substantially broadens the class of diffusion samplers known to enjoy low dimensional adaptation and applies to several commonly used methods in practice. These results provide a theoretical justification for the empirical effectiveness of diffusion samplers across different coefficient choices when applied to structured, high-dimensional data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扩散模型在灵活系数选择下适应低维结构
扩散模型已知能利用未知的低维结构加速采样。然而，现有在低维数据结构下的收敛理论主要聚焦于狭窄指定的系数选择更新规则。这引发了一个根本性问题：对低维结构的适应是否依赖于更新系数的精确选择？本文表明，这种适应是扩散模型的稳健属性。对于广泛的一类更新系数，我们证明了 $\widetilde{O}(k/\varepsilon)$ 次迭代足以在总变差（TV）距离下生成精度为 $\varepsilon$ 的样本，且与环境维度无关。我们的框架大幅扩展了已知享受低维适应的扩散采样器类别，并适用于实践中常用的多种方法。这些结果为扩散采样器在不同系数选择下应用于结构化高维数据时的实证有效性提供了理论依据。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand whether diffusion models' ability to adapt to low-dimensional structure depends on specific coefficient choices, this paper investigates the robustness of such adaptation across a broad class of update rules. It proves that for many coefficient configurations, diffusion models can generate ε-accurate samples in total variation distance within \widetilde{O}(k/ε) iterations, independent of ambient dimension. This theoretical result demonstrates that low-dimensional adaptation is not sensitive to coefficient selection and extends the range of applicable diffusion samplers, supporting their empirical success on structured high-dimensional data.
为探究扩散模型对低维结构的适应能力是否依赖于特定的更新系数选择，本文研究了该适应性在广泛系数配置下的鲁棒性。作者证明，对于许多系数配置，扩散模型可在总变差距离下，用\widetilde{O}(k/ε)次迭代生成ε精度的样本，且该复杂度与环境维度无关。该结果表明低维适应性对系数选择不敏感，并为实际中广泛使用的扩散采样器提供了理论支撑。
DiT-Reward: Generative Representations for Text-to-Image Reward Modeling
Authors: Yuanming Yang, Guoqing Ma, Bo Wang, Yuan Zhang, Wei Tang, Chenyi Li, Haoyang Huang, Nan Duan
First: 2026-06-22T17:19:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:19:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Can representations learned for image generation also support the evaluation of generated images? We study text-to-image reward prediction as a downstream task of generative representation learning. To this end, we introduce DiT-Reward, which converts a pretrained text-to-image Diffusion Transformer into a reward model by processing near-clean image latents and aggregating text-conditioned image representations across transformer layers. Under the same training data mixture as HPSv3, DiT-Reward outperforms HPSv3 on all four evaluated preference benchmarks, reaching 85.6% on HPDv2 and 77.6% on HPDv3. When the generative backbone is frozen, a lightweight learned head can still extract meaningful preference predictions from its representations. Probing across depth further reveals that downstream reward performance is strongest in the middle-to-late layers and benefits from combining representations across different stages. We also observe consistent positive scaling with generative backbone capacity. Finally, when used to optimize Stable Diffusion 3.5 Large with Flow-GRPO, DiT-Reward outperforms HPSv3 along the matched training trajectory, with particularly clear gains in realism. Direct latent scoring also achieves a 1.65x inference speedup over HPSv3 with comparable peak memory. These results show that pretrained generative DiTs provide transferable representations for reward modeling and policy optimization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DiT-Reward：用于文本到图像奖励建模的生成表示
用于图像生成所学习的表示能否支持生成图像的评估？我们将文本到图像奖励预测作为生成表示学习的下游任务进行研究。为此，我们引入DiT-Reward，通过处理近似干净的图像潜变量，并在Transformer各层聚合文本条件图像表示，将预训练的文本到图像扩散Transformer转换为奖励模型。在与HPSv3相同的训练数据混合下，DiT-Reward在所有四个评估偏好基准上均优于HPSv3，分别在HPDv2和HPDv3上达到85.6%和77.6%。当生成主干网络被冻结时，轻量级学习头仍能从其表示中提取有意义的偏好预测。进一步的深度探查表明，下游奖励性能在中后期层表现最强，且受益于不同阶段表示的结合。我们还观察到，奖励性能随生成主干容量呈一致正向缩放。最后，当用于优化Stable Diffusion 3.5 Large时，DiT-Reward沿匹配训练轨迹优于HPSv3，尤其在真实感方面表现明显。直接潜变量评分也比HPSv3实现1.65倍推理加速，且峰值内存消耗相当。这些结果表明，预训练的生成DiT提供了可迁移的表示，可用于奖励建模和策略优化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the question of whether generative image representations can support reward modeling, the authors introduce DiT-Reward, a method that repurposes a pretrained text-to-image Diffusion Transformer by processing near-clean latents and aggregating text-conditioned representations across transformer layers to form a reward model. Evaluated against HPSv3 on four preference benchmarks, DiT-Reward achieves superior performance, reaching 85.6% on HPDv2 and 77.6% on HPDv3, even when the generative backbone is frozen. Probing layer depth reveals peak reward performance in middle-to-late layers, and reward quality scales positively with backbone capacity. When used to optimize Stable Diffusion 3.5 Large with Flow-GRPO, DiT-Reward yields better realism than HPSv3, and direct latent scoring offers a 1.65x speedup with similar memory usage, demonstrating the transferability of generative DiT representations for reward modeling and policy optimization.
为探究生成式图像表示是否可用于奖励建模，作者提出DiT-Reward方法，将预训练的文本到图像扩散Transformer通过处理近干净潜变量并聚合跨层文本条件图像表示，转化为奖励模型。在四个偏好基准上，DiT-Reward优于HPSv3，分别达到85.6%（HPDv2）和77.6%（HPDv3），即使冻结生成主干并仅使用轻量头也能实现有效偏好预测。层间探查显示中后期层表现最佳，跨阶段组合表示可提升性能。DiT-Reward随生成主干容量增长而稳定提升，用于优化Stable Diffusion 3.5 Large时在匹配训练轨迹上表现更优，尤其在真实感方面提升明显。直接潜变量评分相较HPSv3实现1.65倍推理加速，内存开销相近。
Learning to See While Learning to Act: Diffusion Models for Active Perception in Robot Imitation
Authors: Kuancheng Wang, Vaibhav Saxena, Shuo Cheng, Yotto Koga, Danfei Xu
First: 2026-06-22T17:19:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:19:57+00:00
Comments: Project website: see2act.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Most imitation learning methods assume full observability in table-top settings. In practice, objects are often occluded, requiring robots to both search and act, and learning this coupled behavior from limited demonstrations remains challenging. We propose See2Act, an imitation learning approach that conditions action prediction on a sequence of actively-inferred viewpoints at test time, by coupling action denoising with viewpoint refinement. The policy is trained using camera poses anchored to keyframe actions from offline demonstrations, enabling implicit learning of where to see, while learning how to act. We empirically demonstrate that in Ravens the policy recovers informative viewpoints under severe occlusions, and on RLBench tasks it improves performance by up to 34% over prior methods. In the real world, we collect 50 demonstrations in a digital twin and achieve zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on pick-and-place tasks using depth observations. The policy handles significant occlusions, showing that learned viewpoint reasoning enables robust manipulation under partial observability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：边学习感知边学习行动：机器人模仿中的扩散模型主动感知
大多数模仿学习方法假设桌面场景下完全可观测。实际中，物体常被遮挡，要求机器人同时搜索与行动，从有限演示中学习这种耦合行为仍具挑战。我们提出See2Act，一种模仿学习方法，通过将动作预测与主动推断的视角序列耦合，并结合动作去噪与视角优化。策略利用离线演示中的关键帧动作锚定的相机位姿进行训练，隐式学习“应看何处”，同时学习“如何行动”。实验证明，在Ravens中，该策略在严重遮挡下仍能恢复信息性视角；在RLBench任务中，性能较前方法提升最高34%。在真实世界中，我们在数字孪生中收集50个演示，利用深度观测实现抓取放置任务的零样本仿真到现实迁移。该策略可处理显著遮挡，表明学习视角推理可在部分可观测条件下实现鲁棒操作。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of learning robotic manipulation under partial observability where objects are often occluded, this work introduces See2Act, a diffusion-based imitation learning method that jointly learns to act and to select informative viewpoints. The approach conditions action prediction on a sequence of actively inferred camera viewpoints, coupling action denoising with viewpoint refinement during inference. Trained using camera poses aligned to keyframe actions from offline demonstrations, the policy implicitly learns where to look while learning how to act. Experiments show that on Ravens, the method recovers useful viewpoints under severe occlusions, and on RLBench tasks it improves performance by up to 34% over prior methods. In real-world deployment, using a digital twin, it achieves zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on pick-and-place tasks with depth observations, demonstrating robust manipulation under occlusion.
为了解决机器人在物体被遮挡的环境下进行模仿学习时观测不完整的问题，该论文提出了See2Act方法，这是一种结合动作预测与视角推理的扩散模型。该方法在推理时根据主动推断的视角序列预测动作，将动作去噪与视角优化联合建模。模型通过将离线演示中的关键帧动作与相机位姿对齐进行训练，隐式学习“该看哪里”与“该如何操作”。实验表明，See2Act在Ravens中能在严重遮挡下恢复有用视角，在RLBench任务上性能提升最高达34%。在真实世界中，通过数字孪生收集50个演示后，该方法仅用深度观测即可实现零样本仿真到现实的迁移，在抓取放置任务中表现稳健，有效应对遮挡问题。
Label-Efficient 3D Forest Mapping: Self-Supervised and Transfer Learning for Instance Segmentation, Semantic Segmentation, and Species Classification
Authors: Aldino Rizaldy, Fabian Ewald Fassnacht, Ahmed Jamal Afifi, Hua Jiang, Richard Gloaguen, Pedram Ghamisi
First: 2025-11-09T11:16:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:19:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Detailed structural and species information on individual tree level is increasingly important to support precision forestry, biodiversity conservation, and provide reference data for biomass and carbon mapping. Point clouds from airborne and ground-based laser scanning are currently the most suitable data source to rapidly derive such information at scale. Recent advancements in deep learning improved segmenting and classifying individual trees and identifying semantic tree components. However, deep learning models typically require large amounts of annotated training data which limits further improvement. Producing dense, high-quality annotations for 3D point clouds, especially in complex forests, is labor-intensive and challenging to scale. We explore strategies to reduce dependence on large annotated datasets using self-supervised and transfer learning. Our objective is to improve performance across three tasks: instance segmentation, semantic segmentation, and tree classification using realistic and operational training sets. We observe improvements across all tasks, compared to training from scratch, evaluated with their respective metrics. For instance segmentation, self-supervised learning combined with domain adaptation improves AP50 by 16.98%. For semantic segmentation, self-supervised learning alone improves mIoU by 1.79%. For tree classification, hierarchical transfer learning improves mean Jaccard by 6.07%. To simplify use and encourage uptake, we integrated the tasks into a unified framework, streamlining the process from raw point clouds to tree delineation, structural analysis, and species classification. Pretrained models reduce energy consumption and carbon emissions by ~21%. This open-source contribution aims to accelerate operational extraction of individual tree information from laser scanning point clouds to support forestry, biodiversity, and carbon mapping.
中文标题/摘要
标题：高效标签的三维森林制图：实例分割、语义分割与树种分类的自监督与迁移学习
个体树木的详细结构与物种信息对精准林业、生物多样性保护及生物量与碳储量制图至关重要。目前，机载与地面激光扫描点云是快速大规模获取此类信息最合适的来源。深度学习的最新进展提升了对单棵树的分割与分类及语义树部件识别能力。然而，深度学习模型通常需要大量标注训练数据，限制了进一步改进。为复杂森林生成密集高质量的3D点云标注既耗时又难以规模化。我们探索通过自监督与迁移学习减少对大规模标注数据的依赖。目标是在实例分割、语义分割和树种分类三个任务中，使用现实且可操作的训练集提升性能。与从零训练相比，所有任务均取得改进：实例分割中，自监督学习结合领域自适应使AP50提升16.98%；语义分割中，自监督学习单独使mIoU提升1.79%；树种分类中，分层迁移学习使平均Jaccard指数提升6.07%。为简化使用并促进应用，我们将三任务整合为统一框架，从原始点云到树木轮廓提取、结构分析与物种分类流程简化。预训练模型可降低约21%的能耗与碳排放。本开源贡献旨在加速激光扫描点云中单棵树信息的提取，支持林业、生物多样性与碳储量制图。

To address the high annotation cost and scalability challenges in 3D forest mapping, this study leverages self-supervised learning and transfer learning to improve instance segmentation, semantic segmentation, and tree species classification using laser scanning point clouds. The method integrates domain adaptation and hierarchical transfer learning within a unified framework to reduce reliance on large annotated datasets. Experimental results show performance gains: 16.98% increase in AP50 for instance segmentation, 1.79% rise in mIoU for semantic segmentation, and 6.07% improvement in mean Jaccard for tree classification compared to training from scratch. Additionally, pretrained models reduce computational energy use by approximately 21%, supporting more sustainable and efficient forest data extraction.
