dVLA-RL: Reinforcement Learning over Denoising Trajectories for Discrete Diffusion Vision-Language-Action Models
Authors: Yuhao Wu, Yitian Liu, Weijie Shen, Mishuo Han, Wenjie Xu, Haotian Liang, Zhongshan Liu, Yinan Mao, Lei Xu, Xinping Guan, Ru Ying, Ran Zheng, Wei Sui, Xiaokang Yang, Wenbo Ding, Yao Mu
First: 2026-06-22T17:19:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:19:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have established a powerful paradigm for generalist robotic manipulation by grounding control into the semantic reasoning of VLMs. Prevailing architectures typically model actions continuously via diffusion or flow processes, or discretely through either autoregressive generation or parallel decoding. Recently, Discrete Diffusion VLAs (dVLAs) have emerged as a distinct alternative, unifying vision, language, and action into a single discrete token space via masked generative modeling. While combining iterative refinement with unified representations, its training has thus far been restricted to Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), leaving the potential of Reinforcement Learning (RL) for further policy refinement largely unexplored. A fundamental challenge in RL for dVLAs is that the marginal probability of the final action generated by dVLAs remains intractable. To solve this problem, we propose \textbf{dVLA-RL}, shifting the learning objective from the marginal action probability to the joint probability of the sampled generation path. Specifically, by modeling the denoising process as a Markov Decision Process (MDP), we mathematically formulate this path probability as a product of step-wise transitions. This trajectory-level objective provides a unified formulation that natively accommodates variable denoising steps. Leveraging this intrinsic fexibility, we introduce a unified step scheduling approach for complex multi-task learning, tailoring denoising steps to specific task complexities to maximize both success rates and computational effciency. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our approach achieves a success rate of \textbf{99.7\%} on LIBERO. Furthermore, it establishes strong VLA-based results on RoboTwin 2.0 by delivering a \textbf{30.6\%} improvement over the SFT baseline, remaining competitive with strong World-Action Model baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：dVLA-RL：基于去噪轨迹的强化学习用于离散扩散视觉-语言-动作模型
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型通过将控制锚定在视觉语言模型（VLM）的语义推理中，为通用机器人操作建立了强大范式。主流架构通常通过扩散或流过程建模连续动作，或通过自回归生成或并行解码建模离散动作。近期，离散扩散VLA（dVLA）作为一种独特替代方案出现，通过掩码生成建模将视觉、语言和动作统一到单一离散标记空间。虽然其结合了迭代优化与统一表示，但训练迄今仅限于监督微调（SFT），强化学习（RL）在进一步策略优化方面的潜力尚未被充分探索。dVLA在RL中的根本挑战在于，其最终动作的边际概率不可计算。为解决此问题，我们提出\textbf{dVLA-RL}，将学习目标从边际动作概率转向采样生成路径的联合概率。具体而言，通过将去噪过程建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），我们将该路径概率数学上表述为分步转移的乘积。该轨迹级目标提供了一种原生支持可变去噪步数的统一公式。利用其内在灵活性，我们引入统一的步长调度方法，用于复杂多任务学习，根据特定任务复杂度调整去噪步数，以最大化成功率与计算效率。大量评估表明，我们的方法在LIBERO上实现\textbf{99.7\%}的成功率。此外，它在RoboTwin 2.0上建立了强大的VLA基线结果，相较SFT基线提升\textbf{30.6\%}，并保持与强世界动作模型基线的竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance robotic manipulation through reinforcement learning beyond supervised fine-tuning, this work introduces dVLA-RL, a method that redefines the RL objective for discrete diffusion vision-language-action models by shifting focus from marginal action probabilities to joint trajectory probabilities. By modeling the denoising process as a Markov Decision Process, the method formulates the learning signal as a product of step-wise transitions, enabling flexible handling of variable denoising steps. This formulation supports a unified step scheduling strategy that adapts denoising complexity to task demands, improving both success rates and efficiency. Experiments show dVLA-RL achieves 99.7% success on LIBERO and a 30.6% performance gain over SFT on RoboTwin 2.0, while remaining competitive with strong world-action model baselines.
为提升机器人操作能力，该研究提出dVLA-RL方法，通过将强化学习目标从边际动作概率转向采样轨迹的联合概率，突破了离散扩散视觉-语言-动作模型在强化学习中的瓶颈。该方法将去噪过程建模为马尔可夫决策过程，将轨迹概率表示为步骤间转移的乘积，从而天然支持可变去噪步数。基于此，提出统一的步长调度策略，根据任务复杂度动态调整去噪步数，兼顾成功率与计算效率。实验表明，该方法在LIBERO上达成99.7%成功率，在RoboTwin 2.0上较SFT基线提升30.6%，且与强世界动作模型基线保持竞争力。
RECALL: Recovery Experience Collection for Active Lifelong Learning in Vision-Language-Action Models
Authors: Ulas Berk Karli, Tesca Fitzgerald
First: 2026-06-22T17:12:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:12:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are commonly fine-tuned through passive imitation learning, where additional demonstrations are collected for tasks where the policy performs poorly. This approach incurs several downsides: it requires the robot to fail before data collection is triggered, provides little guidance about which states require supervision, and wastes demonstrator effort on redundant parts of the task where the policy already performs well. In this paper, we propose an active, continual learning paradigm for VLAs. We demonstrate that active, uncertainty-guided data collection leads to more efficient fine-tuning than when using passively-collected demonstrations. However, we also find that fine-tuning only on actively-collected recovery data leads to catastrophic forgetting. We evaluate techniques for continual learning, including replay-based data mixing and elastic weight consolidation, and identify tradeoffs between plasticity to uncertainty-guided recovery data and retention of previously learned behaviors. Overall, our work contributes an empirical study of active continual learning for autoregressive VLAs, establishing that uncertainty-guided recovery demonstrations can improve adaptation efficiency while also revealing open challenges when targeted new data is incorporated into large robot policies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：召回：视觉-语言-动作模型中用于主动终身学习的恢复经验收集
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型通常通过被动模仿学习进行微调，即在策略表现不佳的任务中收集额外演示。该方法存在若干缺点：需机器人先失败才能触发数据收集，对哪些状态需要监督缺乏指导，且浪费演示者在策略已表现良好的任务冗余部分上的努力。本文提出了一种针对VLA的主动持续学习范式。我们证明，主动、基于不确定性的数据收集比被动收集演示更高效。然而，我们也发现，仅在主动收集的恢复数据上微调会导致灾难性遗忘。我们评估了持续学习技术，包括基于回放的数据混合与弹性权重固化，并识别了对不确定性引导的恢复数据的可塑性与保留先前学习行为之间的权衡。总体而言，我们的工作为自回归VLA的主动持续学习提供了实证研究，表明不确定性引导的恢复演示可提升适应效率，同时也揭示了在大型机器人策略中引入目标新数据时面临的开放性挑战。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiencies of passive imitation learning in Vision-Language-Action models—such as requiring failures to trigger data collection and wasting demonstrator effort—the paper introduces an active, uncertainty-guided recovery data collection method to improve fine-tuning efficiency. Experiments show that active recovery data enhances adaptation speed, but also causes catastrophic forgetting when used alone. The study evaluates continual learning techniques like replay mixing and elastic weight consolidation, revealing a tradeoff between learning new recovery data and retaining prior skills, highlighting the need for better integration strategies in large-scale robot policies.
为了解决视觉-语言-动作模型被动模仿学习中存在的效率低下问题（如需失败才收集数据、浪费示范者精力），本文提出一种主动、基于不确定性的数据收集方法。实验表明，该主动方法比被动数据更高效，但仅依赖主动收集的恢复数据会导致灾难性遗忘。研究评估了重放混合与弹性权重固化等持续学习方法，发现其在适应新不确定性数据与保留已有知识之间存在权衡，揭示了大型机器人策略中主动持续学习的潜力与挑战。
Contrastive Training with LLM-generated Near-Misses for Robust Code-Switching Speech Recognition
Authors: Tung X. Nguyen, Hieu Minh Truong, Giang Son Nguyen, Nhu Vo, Wray Buntine, Dung D. Le
First: 2026-06-05T07:24:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:12:48+00:00
Comments: Accepted at INTERSPEECH 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Code-switching (CS), the alternation between multiple languages within a single utterance, remains challenging for Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR). To address this issue, we propose a Point-of-Interest (POI)-aware contrastive training framework that improves recognition at CS-critical regions. We first identify CS spans by adopting POI detection method from literature, then construct acoustically plausible near-miss hypotheses by perturbing POIs in ASR N-best outputs and expanding candidates with a large language model. Hard but plausible negatives are retained through filtering with acoustic, phonemic, and textual constraints. Finally, we fine-tune Whisper-small with LoRA using a POI-weighted cross-entropy anchor objective together with a multi-negative contrastive ranking loss. Experiments on CS-FLEURS (cmn-eng) and ViMedCSS (vie-eng) show consistent reductions of over 2% in both general and CS-aware error rates compared to standard LoRA fine-tuning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用大语言模型生成的近似样本进行对比训练以提升代码切换语音识别的鲁棒性
代码切换（CS）指在单句中交替使用多种语言，这对自动语音识别（ASR）仍具挑战性。为此，我们提出一种关注关键点（POI）的对比训练框架，以提升CS关键区域的识别性能。首先，采用文献中的POI检测方法识别CS片段；接着，通过扰动ASR N-best输出中的POI并结合大语言模型扩展候选，构建声学上合理的近似样本；通过声学、音素和文本约束筛选，保留硬但合理的负样本；最后，使用POI加权交叉熵锚目标与多负对比排序损失，以LoRA方式微调Whisper-small。在CS-FLEURS（cmn-eng）和ViMedCSS（vie-eng）上的实验表明，相比标准LoRA微调，通用及CS感知错误率均降低超过2%。
Summary / 总结
To improve Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) performance on code-switching speech, which alternates between languages within a single utterance, this work introduces a POI-aware contrastive training framework. The method first identifies code-switching spans using point-of-interest detection, then generates acoustically plausible near-miss hypotheses by perturbing these POIs in ASR N-best outputs and expanding candidates with a large language model. Hard but plausible negatives are filtered using acoustic, phonemic, and textual constraints. The model, Whisper-small fine-tuned with LoRA, is trained using a POI-weighted cross-entropy loss alongside a multi-negative contrastive ranking loss. Experiments on CS-FLEURS (cmn-eng) and ViMedCSS (vie-eng) show consistent error rate reductions of over 2% in both general and code-switching-aware metrics compared to standard LoRA fine-tuning.
为提升多语言交替（代码切换）场景下的语音识别性能，该研究提出一种基于POI感知的对比训练框架。首先通过文献中的POI检测方法定位代码切换片段，再通过扰动ASR N-best输出中的POI并结合大语言模型生成声学上合理的近似负样本，再经声学、音素和文本约束筛选出硬但合理的负样本。模型采用Whisper-small配合LoRA微调，结合POI加权交叉熵损失与多负对比排序损失进行训练。在CS-FLEURS（cmn-eng）和ViMedCSS（vie-eng）数据集上的实验表明，相比标准LoRA微调，该方法在通用及代码切换感知误差率上均实现超过2%的稳定下降。
Hedgementation = Hedgerow Segmentation: A Remote Sensing Benchmark
Authors: Nathan Senyard, Salem Hamdani, Astrid Zhang, Derek Wang, Evan Shelhamer, Mathias Lécuyer, Joséphine Gantois
First: 2026-06-22T17:12:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:12:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We propose Hedgementation: a new benchmark to evaluate machine learning models for hedgerow mapping from remote sensing data at country scale and 10m$^2$ spatial resolution. We combine and harmonize multiple remote sensing data products and ground truth labels sourced from a hedgerow inventory in France. We measure the ability of three baseline models to generalize across spatial distance, and across climatic zones, a more explicitly challenging task. Our benchmark tests both supervised and self-supervised learning approaches for remote sensing, applied to tracking fine-scale features of high agricultural importance. The code to reproduce the benchmark and baselines results is available at https://github.com/hedgementation/hedgementation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Hedgementation = 灌木篱笆分割：遥感基准
我们提出了Hedgementation：一个用于评估从遥感数据在国家尺度和10m²空间分辨率下进行灌木篱笆制图的机器学习模型的新基准。我们整合并统一了来自法国灌木篱笆库存的多种遥感数据产品和地面真实标签。我们测量了三个基线模型在跨空间距离和跨气候区上的泛化能力，这是一个更具挑战性的任务。我们的基准测试了遥感领域中监督学习和自监督学习方法，用于追踪具有重要农业意义的细尺度特征。复现该基准和基线结果的代码可在https://github.com/hedgementation/hedgementation获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for standardized evaluation of hedgerow mapping at fine spatial resolution across large geographic areas, the authors introduce Hedgementation, a remote sensing benchmark using harmonized satellite data and ground truth from France. They assess three baseline models—supervised and self-supervised—for their ability to generalize across spatial distances and climatic zones, a key challenge in agricultural remote sensing. Results show that models struggle with generalization, particularly across climatic zones, highlighting the difficulty of capturing fine-scale hedgerow features without location-specific training.
为评估大范围高分辨率遥感数据下田埂分割模型的性能，作者构建了Hedgementation基准数据集，整合法国田埂普查数据与多源遥感影像。通过测试三种基线模型在跨空间距离与气候区条件下的泛化能力，比较监督与自监督学习方法对农业细粒度特征的检测效果。实验表明，模型在跨区域泛化时表现不佳，凸显了国家尺度下细粒度遥感任务的挑战性。
Data Selection Through Iterative Self-Filtering for Vision-Language Settings
Authors: Andrei Liviu Nicolicioiu, Sarvjeet Singh Ghotra, Morgane M. Moss, Aaron Courville
First: 2026-06-22T17:11:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:11:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The availability of large amounts of clean data is paramount to training neural networks. However, at large scales, manual oversight is impractical, resulting in sizeable datasets that can be very noisy. Attempts to mitigate this obstacle to producing performant vision-language models have so far involved heuristics, curated reference datasets, and using pre-trained models. Here we propose a novel, bootstrapped method in which a CLIP model is trained on an evolving, self-selected dataset. This evolving dataset constitutes a balance of filtered, highly probable clean samples as well as diverse samples from the entire distribution. Our proposed Self-Filtering method iterates between training the model and selecting a subsequently improved data mixture. Training on vision-language datasets filtered by the proposed approach improves downstream performance without the need for additional data or pre-trained models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向视觉-语言设置的迭代自过滤数据选择方法
大量干净数据的可用性对神经网络训练至关重要。然而，在大规模情况下，人工审核不切实际，导致数据集存在大量噪声。此前为缓解这一障碍以构建高性能视觉-语言模型，方法多依赖启发式规则、人工筛选的参考数据集或预训练模型。本文提出一种新颖的自引导方法：通过不断迭代训练CLIP模型，使其在自我选择的动态数据集上进行学习。该动态数据集包含经过过滤的高概率干净样本，以及来自完整分布的多样化样本。所提出的自过滤方法在模型训练与数据混合选择之间循环迭代，以逐步优化数据质量。采用该方法过滤后的视觉-语言数据集进行训练，可提升下游任务性能，无需额外数据或预训练模型。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of noisy large-scale vision-language datasets that hinder model performance, this paper introduces a bootstrapped self-filtering method that iteratively refines a training dataset using a CLIP model. The method alternates between training the model and selecting higher-confidence, cleaner samples while preserving dataset diversity, thereby improving downstream performance without requiring additional data or pre-trained models. Experimental results show that training on datasets filtered by this self-filtering approach consistently outperforms baselines using raw or heuristically cleaned data.
为解决大规模视觉-语言数据集中噪声干扰模型性能的问题，本文提出一种自迭代过滤方法，通过CLIP模型不断训练与筛选数据，动态平衡高置信度干净样本与分布多样性，从而提升下游任务表现。实验表明，采用该方法过滤后的数据集训练出的模型，在无需额外数据或预训练模型的情况下，性能显著优于使用原始或启发式筛选数据的基线方法。
Vera: A Layered Diffusion Model for Content-Preserving Video Editing
Authors: Hongkai Zheng, Ta-Ying Cheng, Benjamin Klein, Yisong Yue, Zhuoning Yuan
First: 2026-06-22T17:11:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:11:11+00:00
Comments: https://vera-layered-diffusion.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Video diffusion models have enabled remarkable progress in video generation and editing. However, content preservation remains a core challenge: existing methods regenerate every pixel and often alter elements that should remain unchanged, such as characters or background scenes. We introduce Vera, a layered diffusion framework for content-preserving video editing. Instead of regenerating the entire video, Vera generates an edit layer along with an alpha matte for compositing with the source video, separating creative editing from content preservation by design. To encourage coherent composition with the source video, we extend the text-to-video DiT into a Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture, with separate DiTs for each layer that interact through joint self-attention. To support the training of Vera, we further construct a high-quality layered dataset with accurate alpha mattes, diverse scenes and dynamics, and visual effects. Across our quantitative benchmark and human preference study, Vera outperforms leading open-source video editing models in content preservation while remaining competitive in edit quality, using 486K frames of layered training data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Vera：一种用于内容保留视频编辑的分层扩散模型
视频扩散模型已在视频生成与编辑领域取得显著进展，但内容保留仍是核心挑战：现有方法通常重新生成所有像素，常改变本应保持不变的元素，如人物或背景场景。我们提出Vera，一种用于内容保留视频编辑的分层扩散框架。与重新生成整个视频不同，Vera生成一个编辑层及一个用于合成的Alpha遮罩，通过设计将创意编辑与内容保留分离。为促进与源视频的连贯合成，我们将文本到视频的DiT扩展为混合变换器（MoT）架构，各层配备独立的DiT并通过联合自注意力交互。为支持Vera训练，我们进一步构建了一个高质量的分层数据集，包含精确的Alpha遮罩、多样化的场景与动态及视觉效果。在我们的定量基准测试与人类偏好研究中，Vera在内容保留方面优于主流开源视频编辑模型，同时在编辑质量上保持竞争力，使用48.6万帧分层训练数据。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of preserving content during video editing while enabling creative modifications, Vera introduces a layered diffusion model that generates an edit layer and alpha matte separately from the source video, allowing content preservation and editing to be decoupled. The model extends the text-to-video DiT into a Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture, where multiple DiTs handle different layers and interact via joint self-attention to ensure coherent compositing. Trained on a high-quality layered dataset with accurate alpha mattes and diverse dynamics, Vera achieves superior content preservation compared to existing open-source models while maintaining competitive edit quality, validated through both quantitative metrics and human preference studies using 486K layered training frames.
为解决视频编辑中内容保持困难的问题，Vera提出了一种分层扩散模型，通过生成独立的编辑层与Alpha遮罩，实现对视频局部修改而不重绘全部帧。该方法将文本到视频的DiT扩展为混合Transformer架构，使各层通过联合自注意力交互以确保合成一致性。在定量基准和人类偏好测试中，Vera在内容保留方面显著优于主流开源模型，同时保持编辑质量竞争力，训练数据为48.6万帧分层视频。
Discovering Latent Groups for Robust Classification
Authors: Ankur Garg, Ulrich Aïvodji, Samira Ebrahimi Kahou, Vincent Michalski
First: 2026-06-22T17:09:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:09:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Machine learning models exploit spurious correlations, achieving high average accuracy but failing disproportionately on underrepresented subgroups. Existing methods address this by adjusting network parameters, guided either by subgroup annotations or inferred pseudo-group labels. Yet at inference, these methods produce only a class prediction, with no insight into a sample's latent subgroup. We propose neural classification trees (NCT), a framework that achieves robustness by encoding subgroup structure in its tree-shaped architecture. By routing each sample to an "easy" or "hard" node of this tree -- based on prediction correctness -- and reusing these routes as pseudo-labels for the next iteration, NCT disentangles conflicting subgroups, without requiring subgroup supervision. We evaluate NCT on five benchmarks spanning binary and multi-class spurious correlations. Our experiments show that the learned tree topology provides strong interpretability by consistently isolating minority subgroups, which provides a transparent mapping between the model architecture and the data's latent group structure, while yielding competitive robustness with state-of-the-art methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：发现鲁棒分类中的潜在群体
机器学习模型利用虚假相关性，获得高平均准确率，但在代表性不足的子群体上表现不佳。现有方法通过调整网络参数来解决此问题，依据子群体标注或推断的伪群体标签进行引导。然而，在推理阶段，这些方法仅输出类别预测，无法揭示样本的潜在子群体。我们提出神经分类树（NCT），通过其树状架构编码子群体结构以实现鲁棒性。通过将每个样本路由至树中的“易”或“难”节点（基于预测正确性），并重用这些路由作为下一轮的伪标签，NCT在无需子群体监督的情况下分离冲突子群体。我们在五个涵盖二分类和多分类虚假相关性的基准数据集上评估NCT。实验表明，学习到的树拓扑结构具有强可解释性，能持续隔离少数群体，从而在模型架构与数据潜在群体结构之间建立透明映射，同时实现与最先进方法相当的鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the issue that machine learning models often fail on underrepresented subgroups despite high average accuracy, this paper introduces neural classification trees (NCT), a method that embeds subgroup structure into its tree architecture to improve robustness. NCT routes each sample to an "easy" or "hard" node based on prediction correctness, then reuses these routing decisions as pseudo-labels to iteratively disentangle conflicting subgroups without requiring explicit subgroup annotations. Experiments across five benchmarks demonstrate that NCT consistently isolates minority subgroups through its learned tree topology, offering interpretable alignment with latent group structure while achieving competitive robustness compared to state-of-the-art methods.
为了解决机器学习模型在高平均准确率下对少数群体表现不佳的问题，本文提出神经分类树（NCT），通过树状结构编码子群体信息以提升鲁棒性。NCT根据预测正确性将样本路由至“易”或“难”节点，并将这些路由复用为伪标签，迭代解耦冲突子群体，无需显式子群体标注。在五个基准测试中，NCT通过学习的树拓扑结构持续隔离少数群体，提供可解释的潜在群体结构映射，同时在鲁棒性上达到与最先进方法相当的水平。
Causal Discovery in the Era of Agents
Authors: Yujia Zheng, Vishal Verma, Mantej Gill, Haoyue Dai, Peter Spirtes, Kun Zhang
First: 2026-06-22T17:09:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:09:22+00:00
Comments: Platform is available at causallearn.com
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent attempts to combine large language models (LLMs) with causal discovery ask models to infer pairwise directions, propose graph structures, or inject language-model outputs as priors and constraints. These approaches promise faster analysis, but they also obscure whether a causal evidence is supported by data and assumptions or by textual associations, prompt artifacts and hallucinated mechanisms. We argue for a different role for agents in causal discovery. Agents should inspect data, retrieve context, explain method assumptions and clarify graph outputs, but they should not supply edges, orientations, priors, constraints or causal conclusions. We propose the principle that agents assist the workflow, while causal claims remain grounded in data, explicit assumptions, formal algorithms, diagnostics and user or domain-expert decisions. We instantiate this principle in causal-learn+, an online platform that coordinates data analysis, preprocessing, method recommendation, expert-knowledge incorporation, formal discovery and interpretation around the algorithmic ecosystem of causal-learn. A case study on Big Five personality data illustrates agent-assisted pipeline of causal discovery without turning language-model unreliability into causal evidence. The platform is available at causallearn.com.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理时代下的因果发现
近期尝试将大语言模型（LLMs）与因果发现结合，要求模型推断成对方向、提出图结构，或注入语言模型输出作为先验和约束。这些方法虽承诺更快分析，但也模糊了因果证据是基于数据和假设，还是基于文本关联、提示偏差和虚构机制。我们主张代理在因果发现中应扮演不同角色：代理应检查数据、检索上下文、解释方法假设并澄清图输出，但不应提供边、方向、先验、约束或因果结论。我们提出原则：代理辅助工作流程，而因果主张仍应扎根于数据、明确假设、正式算法、诊断及用户或领域专家决策。我们通过causal-learn+平台实例化该原则，该平台协调数据探索、预处理、方法推荐、专家知识整合、正式发现与解释，围绕causal-learn算法生态构建。以大五人格数据的案例研究展示了代理辅助的因果发现流程，避免将语言模型不可靠性转化为因果证据。平台可在causallearn.com访问。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by concerns that large language models may mislead causal inference by generating spurious edges or conclusions from textual artifacts, this work proposes that agents should serve as supportive tools rather than causal decision-makers. The method centers on a platform called causal-learn+, which orchestrates data inspection, method selection, expert input, and formal causal discovery while strictly prohibiting agents from proposing causal structures or conclusions. Experimental results from a case study on Big Five personality data demonstrate that the agent-assisted workflow successfully avoids hallucinated causal claims, maintaining rigor by grounding all causal assertions in data, formal algorithms, diagnostics, and human oversight.
鉴于大语言模型可能通过文本关联或幻觉机制误导因果推断，本文主张在因果发现中，代理应仅作为辅助工具而非决策主体。该方法通过构建causal-learn+平台，协调数据检查、预处理、方法推荐、专家知识整合与形式化因果发现，但严格禁止代理提出因果结构或结论。基于大五人格数据的案例研究表明，该代理辅助流程有效避免了幻觉因果推断，确保所有因果断言均基于数据、形式化算法、诊断结果及专家判断。
Scaling Linear Mode Connectivity and Merging to Billion Parameter Pretrained Transformers
Authors: Tianyi Li, Zhiqiang Shen
First: 2026-06-22T17:08:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:08:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Linear mode connectivity (LMC) provides a promising foundation for understanding and merging independently trained neural networks, but existing methods typically optimize the interpolation path from only one model endpoint, limiting their scalability and effectiveness for large pretrained transformers. We propose a novel and scalable framework for enabling LMC-based model merging to {\em billion-parameter pretrained transformers}. Our method applies properly parameterized functionality-preserving weight transformations to align functionally equivalent solutions, and introduces a dual learning procedure in which both models jointly learn their corresponding transformations toward a shared linear interpolation path. This bidirectional optimization substantially reduces interpolation barriers and enables more reliable merging across large-scale architectures. Empirically, we show that our approach achieves near-zero loss barriers on WikiText for language models with medium-sized parameters, representing, to our knowledge, the first demonstration of near-barrier-free linear connectivity at this scale. In the vision domain, ViT-L maintains above 69\% ImageNet top-1 accuracy throughout the interpolation path, while modern billion-parameter LLMs exhibit only small loss barriers. These results suggest that properly resolving parameter symmetries enables large pretrained Transformers to be connected and merged through simple linear paths with substantially improved interpolation performance. Code: https://github.com/VILA-Lab/Dual-Learned-Matching .
中文标题/摘要
标题：扩展线性模式连通性与合并至十亿参数预训练Transformer
线性模式连通性（LMC）为理解与合并独立训练的神经网络提供了有前景的基础，但现有方法通常仅优化从单一模型端点出发的插值路径，限制了其在大型预训练Transformer中的可扩展性和有效性。我们提出了一种新颖且可扩展的框架，以实现基于LMC的模型合并至{\em 十亿参数预训练Transformer}。该方法应用适当参数化的功能保持权重变换，以对齐功能等价的解，并引入双学习过程，使两个模型共同学习其对应的变换，朝向共享的线性插值路径。这种双向优化显著降低了插值障碍，使大规模架构间的合并更加可靠。实验证明，我们的方法在WikiText数据集上对中等规模参数的语言模型实现了近乎零损失障碍，据我们所知，这是首次在该规模下实现近乎无障碍的线性连通性。在视觉领域，ViT-L在整个插值路径中保持超过69%的ImageNet top-1准确率，而现代十亿参数大语言模型仅表现出微小的损失障碍。这些结果表明，正确解决参数对称性可使大型预训练Transformer通过简单的线性路径连接与合并，显著提升插值性能。代码：https://github.com/VILA-Lab/Dual-Learned-Matching。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to scale linear mode connectivity (LMC) for merging large pretrained transformers, the authors introduce a dual learning framework that jointly optimizes parameter transformations for two models to align their functional equivalence along a shared linear interpolation path. By applying functionality-preserving weight transformations and enabling bidirectional optimization, the method reduces interpolation barriers significantly. Experiments demonstrate near-zero loss barriers on WikiText for medium-sized models and minimal accuracy drops (above 69% top-1 on ImageNet) for Vision Transformers and billion-parameter LLMs, indicating that resolving parameter symmetries enables reliable linear merging at scale.
为实现大规模预训练Transformer的线性模式连通性与合并，该研究提出一种双学习框架，通过联合优化两个模型的参数变换以对齐共享线性插值路径。采用功能保持的权重变换并支持双向优化，显著降低插值障碍。实验表明，在WikiText上中等规模模型实现近零损失障碍，视觉Transformer ViT-L在插值路径中保持69%以上ImageNet准确率，千亿参数大模型损失障碍极小，证明合理解决参数对称性可实现大规模Transformer的可靠线性合并。
Autonomous Subsea Cable Search and Tracking with Graph-Optimised Priors and Visual Tracking
Authors: Ibrahim Fadhil Djauhari, Adrian Bodenmann, Samuel Simmons, Cailei Liang, David White, Susan Gourvenec, Tom Bennetts, Darryl Newborough, Blair Thornton
First: 2026-06-22T17:08:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:08:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Global communications rely on subsea cable infrastructure that remains vulnerable to damage from natural hazards and human activity. Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) offer an efficient means to inspect long sections of exposed cable, but uncertainty in cable route maps, small cable diameters and partial burial makes continuous tracking a challenge. This paper presents a novel cable search and tracking method that leverages uncertain prior cable route maps. Graph-based optimisation continuously update the cable route to remain consistent with visual observations. Route uncertainty is constrained as a function of distance from observations using physics-based catenary models that account for cable parameters (i.e., lay depth, diameter, and density), bounding the search space to physically feasible regions and improving search efficiency. Cable detection is performed using a semi-supervised classifier running in real-time on-board a camera-equipped AUV. These detections both update the graph-based optimisation and enable visual cable tracking. When tracking is lost due to misclassification, burial or imperfect control, the bounded search space enables efficient recovery. The approach was demonstrated in field trials using the University of Southampton's Smarty200 AUV. The system successfully located the cable despite deliberate errors in it initial cable route map, updating this to be consistent with observations and using visual tracking to inspect up to 59% of a 120m test cable, with successful recovered after tracking loss.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于图优化先验与视觉跟踪的自主水下电缆搜索与追踪
全球通信依赖海底电缆基础设施，其易受自然灾害和人为活动破坏。自主水下航行器（AUV）可高效检查长段暴露电缆，但电缆路径图的不确定性、电缆直径小及部分埋藏使连续追踪困难。本文提出一种新颖的电缆搜索与追踪方法，利用不确定的先验电缆路径图。基于图的优化持续更新电缆路径，使其与视觉观测保持一致。路径不确定性通过物理模型（考虑电缆参数如铺设深度、直径和密度）约束为观测距离的函数，将搜索空间限定在物理可行区域，提升搜索效率。电缆检测采用运行于配备摄像头的AUV上的半监督分类器，实时完成。这些检测结果既更新图优化，又支持视觉电缆追踪。当因误分类、埋藏或控制不精确导致追踪丢失时，受限搜索空间可高效恢复。该方法在南安普顿大学Smarty200 AUV实地试验中验证，系统成功定位电缆，即使初始路径图存在故意错误，亦能更新路径以匹配观测，并通过视觉追踪完成120米测试电缆59%的检查，且在追踪丢失后成功恢复。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to inspect vulnerable subsea cables using autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs), this study introduces a method that combines graph-based route optimization with visual tracking to handle uncertain cable maps and physical constraints. The system uses physics-based catenary models to constrain the search space around cable parameters, enabling efficient tracking even when visual detection fails. Field trials with the Smarty200 AUV demonstrated the method’s ability to locate cables despite inaccurate initial maps, update routes based on real-time observations, and recover tracking after loss, successfully inspecting up to 59% of a 120-meter test cable.
为应对海底电缆易受自然与人为因素破坏的问题，本文提出一种结合图优化先验与视觉跟踪的自主水下机器人（AUV）电缆搜索方法。该方法利用基于物理的悬链线模型，根据电缆参数约束路径不确定性，缩小搜索空间至物理可行区域。AUV搭载的半监督分类器实时检测电缆段，更新图优化路径并支持视觉跟踪；当因误检、埋藏或控制误差导致跟踪丢失时，受限搜索空间可高效恢复。实地试验中，Smarty200 AUV在初始路径图存在误差的情况下成功定位电缆，完成120米测试电缆59%的视觉检测，并在跟踪丢失后实现有效恢复。
Polycepta: Object-Centric Appearance Estimation for Multi-Object Tracking
Authors: Mohamed Nagy, Naoufel Werghi, Jorge Dias, Majid Khonji
First: 2026-06-22T17:06:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:06:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The tracking-by-detection paradigm in multi-object tracking (MOT) typically relies on static appearance descriptors to complement motion estimation. However, these descriptors are frame-independent, limiting their robustness as visual cues. Since such descriptors are often obtained from computationally intensive pretrained backbones, real-time MOT systems frequently abandon appearance cues altogether and rely solely on motion prediction and geometric association. In this work, we introduce Polycepta, an object-centric appearance state estimation framework that reformulates appearance modeling as a recursive estimation problem rather than a frame-wise matching task. Polycepta constructs and continuously updates an independent appearance state for each tracked object, enabling future appearance representations to be estimated from accumulated observations. Polycepta is encouraged to learn the appearance-state construction of object-specific representations rather than memorize them through a proposed learning strategy, enabling appearance estimation for unseen classes. A key property of Polycepta is that the quality of appearance estimation improves as object states evolve during inference. While conventional appearance descriptors remain static or degrade over time, Polycepta progressively refines appearance estimates as additional observations are accumulated. Extensive experiments on KITTI, the Waymo Open Dataset, and MOT17 demonstrate consistent reductions in identity switches and improvements in tracking performance when integrated into the tracking-by-detection pipelines. Polycepta operates at 90.57 Hz and delivers state-of-the-art performance on the KITTI benchmark when integrated into the RobMOT framework, achieving a MOTA of 92.27\%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Polycepta：面向多目标跟踪的以对象为中心的外观估计
多目标跟踪（MOT）中的检测跟踪范式通常依赖静态外观描述符来补充运动估计。然而，这些描述符帧间独立，限制了其作为视觉线索的鲁棒性。由于此类描述符常由计算密集型预训练骨干网络获得，实时MOT系统通常完全放弃外观线索，仅依赖运动预测和几何关联。本文提出Polycepta，一种以对象为中心的外观状态估计框架，将外观建模重构为递归估计问题而非帧间匹配任务。Polycepta为每个跟踪对象构建并持续更新独立的外观状态，使未来外观表示可从累积观测中估计。通过所提学习策略，Polycepta被鼓励学习对象特定表示的外观状态构建而非通过记忆，从而实现对未见类别的外观估计。Polycepta的关键特性是：在推理过程中，随着对象状态演化，外观估计质量持续提升。与传统外观描述符保持静态或随时间退化不同，Polycepta在累积更多观测时逐步优化外观估计。在KITTI、Waymo开放数据集和MOT17上的大量实验表明，集成到检测跟踪框架中可稳定降低身份切换并提升跟踪性能。Polycepta运行频率达90.57Hz，在集成至RobMOT框架后于KITTI基准上达到当前最优性能，MOTA为92.27%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of static appearance descriptors in multi-object tracking, which hinder robustness and real-time performance, Polycepta introduces an object-centric appearance estimation framework that treats appearance modeling as a recursive estimation problem. Instead of frame-wise matching, it maintains and continuously updates independent appearance states for each tracked object, enabling progressive refinement of appearance representations through accumulated observations. This approach allows Polycepta to learn object-specific appearance states without memorization, supporting unseen classes. Experiments on KITTI, Waymo Open Dataset, and MOT17 show consistent reductions in identity switches and improved tracking performance, with Polycepta achieving 92.27% MOTA on KITTI when integrated into RobMOT, operating at 90.57 Hz.
为了解决传统多目标跟踪中静态外观描述符鲁棒性差、难以实时应用的问题，Polycepta提出一种以对象为中心的外观状态估计框架，将外观建模视为递归估计问题，而非帧间匹配任务。它为每个跟踪对象独立维护并持续更新外观状态，使外观表示能随观测积累逐步优化。在KITTI、Waymo Open Dataset和MOT17上的实验表明，该方法显著降低身份切换率并提升跟踪性能，在RobMOT框架下于KITTI上达到92.27%的MOTA，运行频率达90.57Hz。
MORL-A2C: Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning Reranker for Optimizing Healthiness in MOPI-HFRS
Authors: Aarya Vasantlal, Joshua Zolla
First: 2026-06-22T17:06:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:06:30+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the International Workshop on Resource-Efficient Learning for Knowledge Discovery (RelKD) at ACM SIGKDD 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Unhealthy dietary behavior continues to be a persistent public health issue in the United States, exacerbated by recommendation systems that prioritize user preference without considering nutritional health. The Multi-Objective Personalized Interpretable Health-aware Food Recommendation System (MOPI-HFRS), from which this work extends, addresses this by jointly optimizing preference, health, and diversity through Pareto-based optimization. However, this approach relies on static, per-step tradeoff solutions that fail to capture the sequential nature of dietary decision-making. We introduce MORL-A2C, a sequential decision-making extension to MOPI-HFRS targeting the health-preference axis. Leveraging frozen GNN embeddings, MORL-A2C formulates recommendation as a K-step reranking problem using an Advantage Actor-Critic algorithm with a scalarized relevance/health reward. The policy is warm-started via behavior cloning against a dot-product ranker derived from frozen embeddings. We also identify and correct a non-trivial bug in the MOPI-HFRS evaluation pipeline that understated baseline performance; all results are reported against the corrected baseline. On the macro-nutrient benchmark, MORL-A2C achieves a modest reduction in ranking quality (Recall@20: 25.64% to 23.61%, NDCG@20: 23.52% to 20.64%) in exchange for a substantial improvement in health alignment (H-Score@20: 46.05% to 69.57%), with consistent trends on the full-nutrient benchmark. These findings validate that policy-driven sequential optimization can effectively navigate the health-preference trade-off in multi-objective food recommendation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MORL-A2C：用于优化MOPI-HFRS中健康性的多目标强化学习重排序器
不健康的饮食行为仍是美国持续存在的公共卫生问题，且推荐系统因优先考虑用户偏好而忽视营养健康，加剧了这一问题。本研究扩展的多目标个性化可解释健康导向食品推荐系统（MOPI-HFRS）通过基于帕累托的优化联合优化偏好、健康与多样性。然而，该方法依赖静态、每步的权衡解决方案，未能捕捉饮食决策的序列性。我们引入MORL-A2C，作为MOPI-HFRS在健康-偏好轴上的序列决策扩展。利用冻结的GNN嵌入，MORL-A2C将推荐建模为K步重排序问题，采用具有标量化相关性/健康奖励的Advantage Actor-Critic算法。策略通过行为克隆预热，以冻结嵌入导出的点积排序器为基准。我们还识别并修正了MOPI-HFRS评估流程中的一个非平凡错误，该错误低估了基线性能；所有结果均基于修正后的基线报告。在宏营养素基准上，MORL-A2C虽略微降低排序质量（Recall@20：25.64%降至23.61%，NDCG@20：23.52%降至20.64%），但显著提升了健康对齐度（H-Score@20：46.05%升至69.57%），并在全营养素基准上保持一致趋势。这些发现验证了基于策略的序列优化能有效应对多目标食品推荐中的健康-偏好权衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to balance user preference and nutritional health in dietary recommendations, this work extends the MOPI-HFRS system by introducing MORL-A2C, a sequential decision-making method that uses Advantage Actor-Critic with scalarized rewards to rerank food items over K steps. Leveraging frozen GNN embeddings and warm-starting via behavior cloning against a dot-product ranker, the method improves health alignment without sacrificing too much ranking quality. Experimental results show a modest drop in Recall@20 and NDCG@20, but a significant increase in H-Score@20, confirming that sequential policy optimization effectively manages the health-preference trade-off in multi-objective food recommendation.
为应对推荐系统过度关注用户偏好而忽视营养健康的饮食行为问题，本文在MOPI-HFRS框架基础上提出MORL-A2C方法，通过Advantage Actor-Critic算法在K步重排序中联合优化健康与偏好，奖励函数采用标量化相关性与健康度。该方法利用冻结的GNN嵌入进行行为克隆初始化，以提升策略学习效率。实验表明，在宏营养素与全营养素基准上，尽管排名指标略有下降（Recall@20：25.64%→23.61%，NDCG@20：23.52%→20.64%），但健康对齐度显著提升（H-Score@20：46.05%→69.57%），验证了序列化策略优化在多目标饮食推荐中有效平衡健康与偏好的能力。
Neural Networks as Linear Regression: An Introduction for Statisticians
Authors: Abigail Loe, Susan Murray, Zhenke Wu
First: 2026-06-22T17:04:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:04:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural networks are a commonly used prediction tool in computer science and statistics. However, the barrier to entry of this interesting field remains high, particularly for classical statisticians trained in a frequentist perspective. In this letter, we demystify neural networks by describing networks that approximate a linear regression and describe common customizations that provide a foundation for further study.
中文标题/摘要

FairSAM: Fair Classification on Corrupted Image Data Through Sharpness-Aware Minimization
Authors: Yucong Dai, Jie Ji, Xiaolong Ma, Yongkai Wu
First: 2025-03-29T01:51:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:03:25+00:00
Comments: Accepted by TMLR: https://openreview.net/forum?id=W2QKvn57yw
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Image classification models trained on clean data often degrade sharply when exposed to corrupted test or deployment data, such as images with impulse noise, Gaussian noise, or environmental noise. This degradation reduces overall performance and disproportionately affects demographic subgroups, raising algorithmic bias concerns. Although robust learning algorithms such as Sharpness-Aware Minimization improve overall robustness and generalization, they do not address biased performance degradation across demographic subgroups. Existing fairness-aware machine learning methods reduce performance disparities but struggle to maintain robust and equitable accuracy across demographic subgroups under data corruption. This limitation reveals an inherent tension between robustness and fairness under corrupted data. To address these challenges, we introduce a metric to assess performance degradation across subgroups under data corruption. We propose FairSAM, a framework that integrates Fairness-oriented strategies into SAM to equalize performance across demographic groups under corrupted conditions. Experiments on multiple real-world datasets and prediction tasks show that FairSAM balances robustness and fairness in corrupted image classification. The framework yields a structured solution for fair and robust image classification in the presence of data corruption.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FairSAM：通过锐度感知最小化在损坏图像数据上实现公平分类
在干净数据上训练的图像分类模型，当面对损坏的测试或部署数据（如脉冲噪声、高斯噪声或环境噪声图像）时，性能往往急剧下降。这种退化会降低整体性能，并对不同人口子群体产生不成比例的影响，引发算法偏见问题。尽管锐度感知最小化等鲁棒学习算法能提升整体鲁棒性和泛化能力，但它们并未解决不同人口子群体间性能退化的偏倚问题。现有公平性感知机器学习方法虽能减少性能差异，但在数据损坏情况下难以维持各子群体间鲁棒且公平的准确率。这一局限揭示了在损坏数据下鲁棒性与公平性之间固有的张力。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了一种评估数据损坏下各子群体性能退化程度的指标，并提出了FairSAM框架，将面向公平性的策略整合进SAM中，以在损坏条件下实现各人口群体间性能均衡。在多个真实数据集和预测任务上的实验表明，FairSAM在损坏图像分类中实现了鲁棒性与公平性的平衡，为在数据损坏情况下实现公平且鲁棒的图像分类提供了结构化解决方案。

Experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that FairSAM achieves balanced robustness and fairness, outperforming standard robust methods and fairness-aware baselines in maintaining consistent accuracy across subgroups under noisy conditions.
为解决图像分类模型在受污染数据下对不同群体性能不平等的问题，本文提出FairSAM框架，将公平性策略融入Sharpness-Aware Minimization中，以在数据污染条件下均衡各群体的性能表现。该方法通过量化各群体在数据污染下的性能退化，并调整优化过程以实现跨群体精度均衡。实验结果表明，FairSAM在多个真实数据集上有效平衡了鲁棒性与公平性，在噪声和环境干扰下保持了更均衡的准确率，优于基线方法。

Abstract
I discuss conditions under which maximizing a proxy utility function is harmful and suggest this poses problems for applying decision theory.
