SPIRAL: Learning to Search and Aggregate
Authors: Jubayer Ibn Hamid, Ifdita Hasan Orney, Michael Y. Li, Omar Shaikh, Yoonho Lee, Dorsa Sadigh, Chelsea Finn, Noah Goodman
First: 2026-06-22T17:02:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:02:09+00:00
Comments: Ongoing Work
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language model reasoning can be substantially improved at test time via scaffolds that scale inference compute across different primitives -- sequential reasoning within a trace, independently sampled parallel traces, and aggregation of multiple reasoning traces into a final response. During post-training, however, language models are optimized only for sequential reasoning within a single trace. We introduce Sequential-Parallel-Aggregative Reinforcement Learning (SPIRAL), a framework in which a language model is trained to use all three primitives, as part of a unified inference compute pipeline. Concretely, the language model first samples a set of independent traces in parallel, each produced through sequential chain-of-thought reasoning, and then generates a final aggregation trace conditioned on those traces; all components are optimized end-to-end against the reward of the final aggregated response. To train this system, SPIRAL uses set reinforcement learning to teach models to produce a set of traces that are collectively useful for an aggregator and standard reinforcement learning to teach models to aggregate the set into improved final responses. Our experiments on reasoning tasks show that SPIRAL effectively scales with inference compute, outperforming GRPO by up to 11$\times$ scaling efficiency and 15% higher performance when all three compute primitives are scaled.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SPIRAL：学习搜索与聚合
通过在推理过程中扩展计算资源至不同基本操作——单条轨迹内的顺序推理、独立采样的并行轨迹以及多个推理轨迹的聚合——语言模型的推理能力可在测试时显著提升。然而，在后训练阶段，语言模型仅被优化用于单条轨迹内的顺序推理。我们提出了顺序-并行-聚合强化学习（SPIRAL）框架，使语言模型在统一的推理计算管道中学习使用这三种基本操作。具体而言，语言模型首先并行采样一组独立轨迹，每条轨迹通过顺序思维链推理生成，然后基于这些轨迹生成最终的聚合轨迹；所有组件均端到端优化，以最终聚合响应的奖励为目标。为训练该系统，SPIRAL采用集合强化学习训练模型生成对聚合器集体有用的轨迹集合，并使用标准强化学习训练模型将该集合聚合为更优的最终响应。在推理任务上的实验表明，SPIRAL能有效随推理计算扩展，当三种计算原语均扩展时，其效率比GRPO高11倍，性能高出15%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance language model reasoning through scalable inference compute, SPIRAL introduces a unified framework that trains models to leverage sequential reasoning within individual traces, parallel sampling of multiple traces, and aggregation of those traces into a final response. The method employs set reinforcement learning to guide the generation of useful trace sets and standard reinforcement learning to optimize aggregation, all optimized end-to-end against the final response reward. Experimental results demonstrate that SPIRAL scales more efficiently with compute, achieving up to 11x better scaling efficiency and 15% higher performance compared to GRPO when all three primitives are utilized.
为提升语言模型推理能力并实现推理计算的可扩展性，SPIRAL提出一种统一框架，使模型能同时利用顺序推理、并行轨迹采样和轨迹聚合生成最终回答。该方法通过集合强化学习引导生成多个对聚合器有用的推理轨迹，并通过标准强化学习优化轨迹聚合过程，所有组件端到端优化最终响应奖励。实验表明，当同时扩展三种计算原语时，SPIRAL相比GRPO效率提升最高达11倍，性能高出15%。
Real-Time Multimodal Activity-Aware Error Detection in Robot-Assisted Surgery
Authors: Seyed Hamid Reza Roodabeh, Zongyu Li, Homa Alemzadeh
First: 2026-06-22T17:01:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:01:45+00:00
Comments: This work has been submitted to the IEEE for possible publication
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery improves surgical precision but introduces complexity, making technical error detection essential for ensuring patient safety. Current executional error detection methods using video data often overlook fine-grained contextual descriptions of activities and error types within the hierarchical structure of surgical procedures. They also under-utilize complementary multimodal information. We propose a unified framework for executional error detection that leverages multimodal input, including video, kinematics, and descriptive textual prompts. Through activity prompting, we integrate descriptive language in gesture-level activities, instrument-object interactions, and error definitions. We also introduce activity-aware visual embeddings derived from vision encoders pretrained on surgical activity labels to compare the effectiveness of contrastive language-image embeddings with traditional image-based embeddings for error detection. By seamlessly integrating kinematic data with video and textual modalities, our framework significantly improves error detection performance. Achieving up to 5\% and 16.6\% F1 score improvements over state-of-the-art baselines on the JIGSAWS and SAR-RARP50 datasets, respectively, we demonstrate the value of combining curated textual prompts with multimodal data for accurate error detection.
中文标题/摘要
标题：机器人辅助手术中的实时多模态活动感知执行错误检测
机器人辅助微创手术提升了手术精度，但也增加了复杂性，因此技术性错误检测对保障患者安全至关重要。当前基于视频数据的执行错误检测方法常忽略手术程序层级结构中活动与错误类型的细粒度上下文描述，且未能充分利用互补的多模态信息。我们提出一种统一框架，融合视频、运动学及描述性文本提示等多模态输入。通过活动提示，将手势级活动、器械-物体交互及错误定义等描述性语言整合进来。同时，我们引入基于视觉编码器预训练于手术活动标签的活动感知视觉嵌入，对比对比语言-图像嵌入与传统图像嵌入在错误检测中的有效性。通过无缝融合运动学数据与视频及文本模态，我们的框架显著提升了错误检测性能。在JIGSAWS和SAR-RARP50数据集上，相比现有最先进基线方法，F1分数分别提升5%和16.6%，证明了结合精心编写的文本提示与多模态数据对准确错误检测的价值。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance safety in robot-assisted surgery through precise error detection, this study proposes a multimodal framework that integrates video, kinematic data, and textual prompts to capture fine-grained surgical activities and error contexts. The method employs activity-aware visual embeddings derived from vision encoders pretrained on surgical activity labels, and compares their performance against traditional image embeddings. Experimental results on JIGSAWS and SAR-RARP50 datasets show that the proposed approach achieves up to 5% and 16.6% F1 score improvements over existing state-of-the-art methods, demonstrating the effectiveness of combining structured textual descriptions with multimodal inputs for error detection.
为提升机器人辅助手术中的安全性，本研究提出一种融合视频、运动学数据与文本提示的多模态框架，以捕捉手术活动中细粒度的操作与错误上下文。该方法采用基于手术活动标签预训练的视觉编码器生成活动感知视觉嵌入，并与传统图像嵌入进行对比。在JIGSAWS和SAR-RARP50数据集上的实验表明，该方法相较现有最先进方法F1分数分别提升5%和16.6%，验证了结构化文本提示与多模态数据结合在错误检测中的有效性。
RubricRL: Simple Generalizable Rewards for Text-to-Image Generation
Authors: Xuelu Feng, Yunsheng Li, Ziyu Wan, Zixuan Gao, Junsong Yuan, Dongdong Chen, Chunming Qiao
First: 2025-11-25T18:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:00:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning (RL) has recently emerged as a promising approach for aligning text-to-image generative models with human preferences. A key challenge, however, lies in designing effective and interpretable rewards. Existing methods often rely on either composite metrics (e.g., CLIP, OCR, and realism scores) with fixed weights or a single scalar reward distilled from human preference models, which can limit interpretability and flexibility. We propose RubricRL, a simple and general framework for rubric-based reward design that offers greater interpretability, composability, and user control. Instead of using a black-box scalar signal, RubricRL dynamically constructs a structured rubric for each prompt--a decomposable checklist of fine-grained visual criteria such as object correctness, attribute accuracy, OCR fidelity, and realism--tailored to the input text. Each criterion is independently evaluated by a multimodal judge (e.g., o4-mini), and a prompt-adaptive weighting mechanism emphasizes the most relevant dimensions. This design not only produces interpretable and modular supervision signals for policy optimization (e.g., GRPO or PPO), but also enables users to directly adjust which aspects to reward or penalize. Experiments with an autoregressive text-to-image model demonstrate that RubricRL improves prompt faithfulness, visual detail, and generalizability, while offering a flexible and extensible foundation for interpretable RL alignment across text-to-image architectures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RubricRL：面向文本到图像生成的简单通用奖励机制
强化学习（RL）近期被证明是使文本到图像生成模型与人类偏好对齐的有效方法。然而，设计有效且可解释的奖励仍是关键挑战。现有方法常依赖复合指标（如CLIP、OCR和真实感评分）及其固定权重，或从人类偏好模型蒸馏出的单一标量奖励，这限制了可解释性和灵活性。我们提出RubricRL，一种基于评分标准的简单通用奖励框架，提供更高的可解释性、可组合性与用户可控性。RubricRL不使用黑盒标量信号，而是为每个提示动态构建结构化评分标准——一个细粒度视觉标准的可分解检查清单（如物体正确性、属性准确性、OCR保真度和真实感），并根据输入文本定制。每个标准由多模态评判器（如o4-mini）独立评估，且提示自适应加权机制强调最相关维度。该设计不仅为策略优化（如GRPO或PPO）生成可解释且模块化的监督信号，还允许用户直接调整奖励或惩罚的方面。在自回归文本到图像模型上的实验表明，RubricRL提升了提示忠实度、视觉细节和泛化能力，同时为跨文本到图像架构的可解释RL对齐提供了灵活可扩展的基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for interpretable and flexible reward design in text-to-image reinforcement learning, RubricRL introduces a framework that dynamically constructs prompt-specific rubrics composed of fine-grained visual criteria such as object correctness and OCR fidelity, each evaluated by a multimodal judge with adaptive weights. This approach replaces fixed composite metrics or black-box scalar rewards with modular, user-controllable supervision signals. Experiments show that RubricRL enhances prompt faithfulness, visual detail, and generalizability compared to baseline methods while enabling direct manipulation of reward dimensions for improved alignment.
为提升文本到图像生成中强化学习奖励的可解释性和灵活性，RubricRL提出一种基于评分表的框架，针对每个提示动态构建细粒度视觉标准（如物体正确性、OCR准确性等），并由多模态评判器独立评估。通过提示自适应加权机制突出关键维度，赋予用户对奖励维度的直接控制权。实验表明，该方法相较固定组合或标量奖励基线，在提示忠实度、视觉细节和泛化能力上均有提升，同时保持模块化与可解释性，适用于多种文本到图像架构的对齐任务。
Quantifying the Agreement Between Data-Influence and Data-Similarity to Understand LLM Behavior
Authors: Christopher J. Anders, Henrique Da Silva Gameiro, Nico Daheim, Mohammad Emtiyaz Khan
First: 2026-06-22T17:00:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T17:00:04+00:00
Comments: 37 pages, 35 figures, preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
One way to understand LLM behavior is to trace its output back to the training data. Two types of measures are commonly used for output tracing: data-similarity and data-influence. The former is cheaper while the latter is believed to be more accurate. Even though many works have compared them for ground-truth tasks, no such comparisons exist for output tracing. Here, we fill this gap and precisely quantify the commonalities and differences between the two measures. We do this by first ranking the training documents according to each measure and then computing the overlap between the two rankings. Our main finding is that the two rankings agree significantly, but there is an asymmetry between them: The top documents of data-similarity are assigned more consistent ranks by data-influence than the other way around. This result is valid across a range of experiments involving OLMo2-1B, Qwen3-1.7B, LlaMa3.2-1B, Gemma3-1B, and GPT2. We exploit the asymmetry to obtain a favorable cost-accuracy trade-off by using the costly data-influence to refine the results of data-similarity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：量化数据影响与数据相似性之间的协议，以理解大语言模型行为
理解大语言模型（LLM）行为的一种方法是将其输出追溯至训练数据。目前常用两种度量方法进行输出追溯：数据相似性与数据影响。前者成本较低，后者被认为更准确。尽管已有大量研究对比了它们在真实任务中的表现，但尚无此类对比针对输出追溯场景。本文填补了这一空白，精确量化了两种度量方法的共性与差异。我们首先根据每种度量方法对训练文档进行排序，再计算两组排序之间的重叠度。主要发现是：两种排序高度一致，但存在不对称性——数据相似性排名靠前的文档，其数据影响排名更一致，反之则不然。该结果在涉及OLMo2-1B、Qwen3-1.7B、LlaMa3.2-1B、Gemma3-1B和GPT2的多项实验中均成立。我们利用这种不对称性，通过成本较高的数据影响方法优化数据相似性结果，从而获得更优的成本-精度权衡。
Summary / 总结
To better understand how large language models (LLMs) generate outputs by tracing back to training data, this study compares two common tracing methods: data-similarity (cheaper) and data-influence (more accurate). The authors rank training documents using each method and measure overlap between the rankings to quantify agreement. Their key finding is that while the two methods largely agree, data-influence assigns more consistent top ranks to documents identified by data-similarity, revealing an asymmetry. This insight enables a cost-effective strategy: using data-influence to refine data-similarity results, improving accuracy without full data-influence computation across multiple LLMs including OLMo2-1B, Qwen3-1.7B, LlaMa3.2-1B, Gemma3-1B, and GPT2.
为理解大语言模型如何通过训练数据生成输出，该研究比较了两种常用追溯方法：数据相似性（成本低）与数据影响度（更准确）。作者分别用两种方法对训练文档进行排序，并计算排序间的重合度以量化一致性。主要发现是，尽管两种方法总体上高度一致，但数据影响度对数据相似性选出的高相关文档赋予更稳定的排名，存在明显不对称性。这一不对称性在OLMo2-1B、Qwen3-1.7B、Llama3.2-1B、Gemma3-1B和GPT2等多个模型中均成立，可据此策略：用成本较高的数据影响度优化数据相似性结果，实现成本与准确度的有利平衡。
Causally Fair Node Classification on Non-IID Graph Data
Authors: Yucong Dai, Lu Zhang, Yaowei Hu, Susan Gauch, Yongkai Wu
First: 2025-05-03T02:05:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:59:30+00:00
Comments: Accepted by TMLR: https://openreview.net/forum?id=AwptwzGld5
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fair machine learning seeks to identify and mitigate biases in predictions against unfavorable populations characterized by demographic attributes, such as race and gender. Recent research has extended fairness to graph data, such as social networks, but many studies neglect the causal relationships among data instances. This paper addresses a prevalent challenge in many fair machine learning research, which typically assumes independent and identically distributed (IID) data, from the causal perspective. Specifically, this work targets the circumstance where nodes with different neighborhood structures follow different causal mechanisms, violating the invariance assumptions required for classical structural causal models and do-calculus. We base our research on the Network Structural Causal Model (NSCM) framework and develop a Message Passing Variational Autoencoder for Causal Inference (MPVA) to compute interventional distributions for causally fair node classification. We establish theoretical soundness under two conditions: Decomposability and Graph Independence. These conditions formalize when causal mechanism heterogeneity can be overcome by constructing a structural representation that restores invariance and facilitates the computation of interventional distributions using do-calculus in non-IID settings. Empirical evaluations on semi-synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that MPVA outperforms conventional methods by effectively approximating interventional distributions and mitigating bias. Our findings demonstrate the potential of causality-based fairness in complex ML applications and motivate future work on relaxing the classic assumptions in algorithmic fairness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：非独立同分布图数据上的因果公平节点分类
公平机器学习旨在识别并减轻针对种族、性别等人口统计属性所定义的不利群体的预测偏见。近期研究已将公平性扩展至图数据（如社交网络），但许多研究忽略了数据实例间的因果关系。本文从因果视角解决公平机器学习研究中普遍存在的挑战——即通常假设数据为独立同分布（IID），而实际中节点具有不同邻域结构并遵循不同因果机制，违反了经典结构因果模型和do-演算所需的不变性假设。本研究基于网络结构因果模型（NSCM）框架，提出了一种用于因果推断的消息传递变分自编码器（MPVA），以计算因果公平节点分类的干预分布。我们在两个条件下建立了理论严谨性：可分解性与图独立性。这些条件形式化了在何种情况下可通过构建结构表示恢复不变性，从而在非IID设置中利用do-演算计算干预分布。在半合成与真实世界数据集上的实证评估表明，MPVA能有效近似干预分布并减轻偏见，优于传统方法。我们的研究展示了基于因果的公平性在复杂机器学习应用中的潜力，并激励未来对算法公平性经典假设进行放松的研究。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of achieving causally fair node classification in non-IID graph data, where nodes with different neighborhood structures follow distinct causal mechanisms, violating standard invariance assumptions. It proposes the Message Passing Variational Autoencoder for Causal Inference (MPVA) within the Network Structural Causal Model (NSCM) framework to compute interventional distributions for fair predictions. The method is theoretically grounded under two conditions—Decomposability and Graph Independence—that enable causal invariance restoration. Experiments on semi-synthetic and real-world datasets show that MPVA outperforms conventional methods by more accurately approximating interventional distributions and reducing bias, demonstrating the effectiveness of causal reasoning for fairness in graph-based learning.
该论文针对非独立同分布（非IID）图数据中节点分类的因果公平性问题，提出MPVA方法，基于网络结构因果模型框架，通过消息传递变分自编码器计算干预分布以实现公平预测。该方法在“可分解性”与“图独立性”两个条件下具备理论基础，可恢复因果不变性。实验表明，MPVA在半合成与真实数据集上优于传统方法，能更准确逼近干预分布并降低偏倚，验证了因果公平在复杂图学习中的可行性。
Generative Modeling via Kernelized Stochastic Interpolants
Authors: Florentin Coeurdoux, Etienne Lempereur, Nathanaël Cuvelle-Magar, Stéphane Mallat, Eric Vanden-Eijnden
First: 2026-02-23T17:26:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:59:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We develop a kernel method for generative modeling within the stochastic interpolant framework, replacing neural network training with linear systems. The drift of the generative SDE is $\hat b_t(x) = \nablaφ(x)^\topη_t$, where $η_t \in \mathbb{R}^P$ solves a $P\times P$ system computable from data, with $P$ independent of the data dimension $d$. Since estimates are inexact, the diffusion coefficient $D_t$ affects sample quality; the optimal $D_t^*$ from Girsanov diverges at $t=0$, but this poses no difficulty and we develop an integrator that handles it seamlessly. The framework accommodates diverse feature maps: scattering transforms, pretrained generative models, etc, enabling generation and model combination without neural network training. We demonstrate the approach on financial time series, turbulence, and image generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过核化随机插值进行生成建模
我们在随机插值框架内开发了一种核方法用于生成建模，用线性系统替代神经网络训练。生成SDE的漂移项为$\hat b_t(x) = \nablaφ(x)^\topη_t$，其中$η_t \in \mathbb{R}^P$通过数据可计算的$P\times P$系统求解，且$P$与数据维度$d$无关。由于估计不精确，扩散系数$D_t$影响样本质量；Girsanov理论中的最优$D_t^*$在$t=0$处发散，但这无碍，我们开发了一种可无缝处理该问题的积分器。该框架支持多种特征映射（如散射变换、预训练生成模型等），实现生成与模型组合而无需神经网络训练。我们在金融时间序列、湍流和图像生成中验证了该方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the desire to avoid neural network training in generative modeling, this work introduces a kernel-based method using stochastic interpolants, where the drift term is derived from a linear system solvable from data, independent of input dimension. The diffusion coefficient is shown to influence sample quality, and while the optimal Girsanov-based diffusion diverges at t=0, a specialized integrator resolves this issue. Experiments on financial time series, turbulence, and image generation confirm the method’s efficacy across diverse feature maps including scattering transforms and pretrained models, enabling generation and model fusion without training neural networks.
为避免生成模型中的神经网络训练，本文在随机插值框架内提出一种基于核的方法，用线性系统替代神经网络优化。生成SDE的漂移项由特征图梯度与从数据计算出的小型线性系统解构成，其规模与数据维度无关。尽管估计存在误差，扩散系数会影响样本质量，但作者证明最优扩散在t=0处发散，可通过专用积分器无缝处理。该方法支持散射变换、预训练生成模型等多种特征映射，实现无需训练的生成与模型组合。在金融时间序列、湍流和图像生成等实验中，该方法展现出跨领域的有效性。
The Topology of Ill-Posed Questions: Persistent Homology for Detection and Steering in LLMs
Authors: Guangyu Jiang, Sizhe Tang, Mahdi Imani, Tian Lan
First: 2026-06-22T16:58:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:58:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Ill-posed questions, including ambiguous, underspecified, or contradictory queries, may admit no valid answer or multiple plausible answers, posing a challenge for large language models (LLMs). Existing approaches largely analyze ill-posedness through model outputs and often focus on specific subclasses. We investigate whether diverse sources of ill-posedness can be represented within a unified topology of LLM internal states and whether this structure can be used to steer response behavior. We model the contextual hidden states of prompt tokens at each transformer layer as a point cloud and characterize its geometry using finite zero-dimensional persistent homology. Each layer is summarized by three compact descriptors: mean finite lifetime, normalized lifetime entropy, and largest-lifetime concentration. Concatenating these descriptors across layers yields a topology representation of the question. We further introduce topology-conditioned activation steering, which retrieves topologically similar examples and constructs query-specific activation interventions that encourage source-aware clarification or abstention. Across three open-weight LLMs, topology features consistently outperform prompt-based and pooled-hidden-state baselines for ill-posedness classification, improving average accuracy from \(67.4\%\) to \(78.9\%\) on AmbigQA, from \(79.9\%\) to \(88.5\%\) on SituatedQA, and from \(57.6\%\) to \(69.6\%\) on CLAMBER 9-way classification. Topology-conditioned steering increases the average total acceptable response rate from \(61.4\%\) to \(70.6\%\) and grounded acceptable responses from \(11.9\%\) to \(16.4\%\). These results show that persistent homology provides both an interpretable representation of ill-posedness and an effective mechanism for targeted response steering.
中文标题/摘要
标题：不适定问题的拓扑结构：使用持久同调检测与引导大型语言模型
不适定问题（如模糊、未指定或矛盾的查询）可能无有效答案或存在多个合理答案，对大型语言模型（LLMs）构成挑战。现有方法主要通过模型输出分析不适定性，且多聚焦特定子类。我们探究是否能将不同来源的不适定性统一表示为LLM内部状态的拓扑结构，并利用该结构引导响应行为。我们将每个Transformer层中提示词的上下文隐藏状态建模为点云，使用有限零维持久同调刻画其几何结构。每层用三个紧凑描述符总结：平均有限寿命、归一化寿命熵和最大寿命集中度。层间描述符拼接形成问题的拓扑表示。我们进一步提出拓扑条件激活引导方法，检索拓扑相似示例并构建查询特定激活干预，以鼓励源感知澄清或回避。在三个开源LLM上，拓扑特征在不适定性分类中持续优于基于提示和池化隐藏状态的基线方法：在AmbigQA上准确率从67.4%提升至78.9%，在SituatedQA上从79.9%提升至88.5%，在CLAMBER 9分类上从57.6%提升至69.6%。拓扑引导使平均可接受响应率从61.4%提升至70.6%，接地可接受响应率从11.9%提升至16.4%。结果表明，持久同调既提供不适定性的可解释表示，也是有效引导响应的机制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of handling ill-posed questions—ambiguous, underspecified, or contradictory queries—that confuse large language models, this work proposes using persistent homology to capture the topological structure of LLM internal states. The method models hidden states across transformer layers as a point cloud and extracts three compact topological descriptors per layer, which are concatenated to form a question-level topology representation. This representation is then used to steer model behavior via topology-conditioned activation interventions that retrieve similar examples and guide clarification or abstention. Experiments on three open-weight LLMs show that topology-based features outperform prompt and pooled-state baselines in ill-posedness classification, boosting accuracy on AmbigQA, SituatedQA, and CLAMBER by 11.5–19.1 percentage points. Topology-conditioned steering also increases acceptable response rates by 9.2% overall and grounded responses by 4.5%.
为应对大语言模型难以处理的模糊、不完整或矛盾问题，该研究提出利用持久同调分析模型内部状态的拓扑结构。方法将各层隐藏状态建模为点云，并提取三个拓扑特征：平均有限寿命、归一化寿命熵和最大寿命集中度，拼接后形成问题的拓扑表示。进一步引入拓扑条件激活引导机制，通过检索相似拓扑示例，引导模型进行澄清或回避。在三个开源模型上，拓扑特征在问题识别任务中显著优于基于提示和池化状态的基线方法，准确率分别提升11.5%至19.9%。拓扑引导的干预使可接受回答率平均提升9.2%，其中基于事实的回答率提升4.5%。
KEMO: Event-Driven Keyframe Memory for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation with VLA Policies
Authors: Yihan Zeng, Minghao Ye, Yiyuan Chen, Yide Shentu, Philipp Wu, Zike Yan, Zhongyu Li
First: 2026-06-22T16:57:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:57:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-horizon robot manipulation remains challenging because similar observations may occur at different execution stages, while the appropriate action depends on previously completed operations. Memory can address this ambiguity by enabling policies to infer task progress from execution history. However, existing memory-augmented approaches often either retain dense histories that require compression or rely primarily on recent context that may discard earlier task-relevant events. In this work, we propose propose KEMO, a lightweight plug-in memory framework that automatically selectively preserves keyframes associated with task-relevant state changes for VLA policies. KEMO combines robot kinematics with visual filtering to detect events, encodes the selected keyframes as compact temporally ordered memory tokens, and integrates them with current visual features through cross-attention and gated residual fusion for VLA training. The detected events also define higher-weight training samples near critical transitions. We evaluate KEMO on various real-world dual-arm manipulation tasks spanning 2 to 6 scored subtasks, and trajectory length ranging from 830 steps to 2846 execution steps (durations from 28 to 95 seconds). Compared with the memory-free baseline (e.g., $π_{0.5}$), KEMO improves aggregate Task Success Rate by 23.6\% and Stage Completion Rate by 34.1\%. Ablations show that event-driven keyframe selection outperforms uniform sampling and recent-frame retention, while the proposed gated fusion and keyframe-aligned loss weighting provide complementary gains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：KEMO：面向长时程机器人操作的事件驱动关键帧记忆框架（用于VLA策略）
长时程机器人操作仍具挑战性，因为相似观测可能出现在不同执行阶段，而适当动作取决于先前已完成的操作。记忆可通过使策略从执行历史中推断任务进度来解决此歧义。然而，现有记忆增强方法要么保留需压缩的密集历史，要么主要依赖近期上下文，可能丢弃早期任务相关事件。本文提出KEMO，一种轻量级插件式记忆框架，可自动选择性保留与任务相关状态变化的关键帧，供VLA策略使用。KEMO结合机器人运动学与视觉过滤检测事件，将选定关键帧编码为紧凑的时序记忆令牌，并通过交叉注意力与门控残差融合与当前视觉特征集成，用于VLA训练。检测到的事件还定义了关键转换附近高权重训练样本。我们在涵盖2至6个评分子任务、轨迹长度从830步至2846步（时长28至95秒）的多种真实世界双臂操作任务上评估KEMO。与无记忆基线（如π₀.₅）相比，KEMO使综合任务成功率提升23.6%，阶段完成率提升34.1%。消融实验表明，事件驱动的关键帧选择优于均匀采样与近期帧保留，而所提门控融合与关键帧对齐损失加权提供互补增益。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of long-horizon robot manipulation where similar observations can occur at different task stages, KEMO introduces a lightweight memory framework that selectively preserves keyframes tied to task-relevant state changes. It combines robot kinematics and visual filtering to detect events, encodes these keyframes into compact memory tokens, and fuses them with current visual features via cross-attention and gated residual fusion for VLA training. Events also guide loss weighting to emphasize critical transitions. Evaluated on real-world dual-arm tasks with 2–6 subtasks and 830–2846 steps, KEMO outperforms memory-free baselines by 23.6% in task success rate and 34.1% in stage completion rate, with ablations confirming the superiority of event-driven selection and gated fusion.
为解决长时程机器人操作中相似观测可能出现在不同任务阶段的问题，KEMO提出一种轻量级记忆框架，自动选择与任务相关状态变化的关键帧。该框架结合机器人运动学与视觉过滤检测事件，将关键帧编码为紧凑的时间有序记忆令牌，并通过交叉注意力与门控残差融合与当前视觉特征结合用于VLA训练，同时对事件检测到的临界过渡赋予更高训练权重。在涵盖2至6个子任务、轨迹长达2846步的现实双臂操作任务中，KEMO相较无记忆基线提升综合任务成功率23.6%、阶段完成率34.1%，消融实验验证了事件驱动选择与门控融合的有效性。
A Generative Model for Closed-Loop Microsimulation of Signalized Intersections
Authors: Yash Ranjan, Rahul Sengupta, Anand Rangarajan, Sanjay Ranka
First: 2026-06-22T16:57:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:57:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Traffic microsimulators rely on hand-crafted behavior models that reproduce aggregate flow but miss the heterogeneous interactions between vehicles at signalized intersections. Learned trajectory predictors capture richer interactions but are short-horizon and tend to be unstable when run in closed loop. We present Enactor, an actor-centric generative model for closed-loop intersection microsimulation. The model focuses on vehicles; pedestrians are included as context that can influence vehicle decisions but not predicted. Dynamic actors and lane polylines are encoded in polar coordinates referenced to the intersection center. A transformer with separate spatial and temporal attention blocks predicts a distribution over each actor's next-step motion ($s$, $α$). Training uses a closed-loop curriculum so the model is exposed to its own predictions. We evaluate Enactor in two regimes. In a 4000-second simulation-in-the-loop test at two intersection geometries, Enactor controls every dynamic vehicle against a continuously refreshing actor set rather than the fixed cohort that learned trajectory predictors are usually evaluated against. It recovers the SUMO data generator's speed and travel-time distributions with KL divergence over an order of magnitude lower than a recent transformer baseline on travel time, and substantially lower on speed (roughly $5\times$ lower at Site 1), and reduces red-light violations relative to the same baseline by more than an order of magnitude. An ablation isolates the leader rear-bumper feature as the change with the largest effect on intersection-aware safety metrics. We also evaluate on real-world field data and apply the same architecture to naturalistic vehicle trajectories from a fish-eye camera at a signalized intersection and evaluate it on multi-horizon predictive tasks. Enactor outperforms a constant-velocity baseline at every horizon evaluated.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于信号交叉口闭环微观仿真的生成模型
交通微观仿真器依赖手工构建的行为模型，能再现总体流量，但忽略了信号交叉口车辆间的异质交互。学习的轨迹预测器能捕捉更丰富的交互，但为短时程且在闭环运行时易不稳定。我们提出Enactor，一种以车辆为中心的生成模型，用于闭环交叉口微观仿真。该模型聚焦车辆，行人作为可影响车辆决策但不被预测的上下文信息。动态车辆和车道多边形以极坐标形式编码，参考交叉口中心。一个具有独立空间和时间注意力块的Transformer预测每个主体下一步运动的分布（$s$，$α$）。训练采用闭环课程策略，使模型接触自身预测。我们在两种模式下评估Enactor：在两个交叉口几何结构的4000秒仿真闭环测试中，Enactor针对不断刷新的动态车辆集合进行控制，而非传统轨迹预测器通常评估的固定群体。其恢复的SUMO数据生成器的速度与行程时间分布，其KL散度在行程时间上比近期Transformer基线低一个数量级，在速度上显著更低（在Site 1约低5倍），且红灯违规率较基线降低一个数量级以上。消融实验表明，领车后保险杠特征对交叉口安全指标影响最大。我们还评估了真实世界场数据，将相同架构应用于鱼眼相机在信号交叉口采集的自然驾驶轨迹，并在多时程预测任务中评估其性能。Enactor在所有评估时程上均优于恒定速度基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of hand-crafted traffic models and unstable short-horizon learned predictors, the paper introduces Enactor, a generative actor-centric model for closed-loop microsimulation of signalized intersections. It encodes vehicles and lane geometry in polar coordinates relative to the intersection center and uses a transformer with separate spatial and temporal attention to predict motion distributions for each vehicle. Trained via a closed-loop curriculum, Enactor simulates traffic dynamically by controlling vehicles against a continuously updating actor set. In 4000-second simulations, it matches SUMO’s speed and travel-time distributions with significantly lower KL divergence than a transformer baseline—roughly 5x lower on speed at one site—and reduces red-light violations by over an order of magnitude. An ablation study identifies the leader rear-bumper feature as most impactful for safety, and Enactor also outperforms a constant-velocity baseline on multi-horizon predictions using real-world camera data.
为克服传统手工交通模型缺乏异质性交互、以及现有学习预测器在闭环中不稳定的问题，作者提出Enactor，一种以车辆为中心的生成式闭环微观仿真模型。该模型将车辆与车道线编码为相对于路口中心的极坐标，并使用含空间与时间注意力的Transformer预测每辆车的下一步运动分布。通过闭环课程训练，Enactor在动态刷新的车辆群体中表现优异，其速度与行程时间分布与真实数据生成器高度接近，KL散度显著低于近期Transformer基线（行程时间低一个数量级，速度低约5倍），且红灯违规率降低超一个数量级。消融实验表明，前车后保险杠特征对安全指标提升贡献最大，模型亦在真实路测数据上表现良好，所有预测时域均优于恒速基线。
It's Much Easier for Neural Networks to learn Game of Life Dynamics with the Right Activation Function: Polynomial Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks
Authors: Tashin Ahmed, Q. Tyrell Davis
First: 2026-06-22T16:56:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:56:59+00:00
Comments: To be published in Proceedings of the 2026 Artificial Life Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Previous work has found a gap between the scale of neural networks that reliably learn Conway's Game of Life, and minimal networks capable of representing the classic cellular automaton with hard-coded parameter values. Viewing neural network learning as a search process suggests a dependence on networks large enough to contain sub-networks with lucky initializations (sometimes known as 'winning tickets') that actually learn the task. In this work, we reorient our perspective from discovering Life rules as a search problem back to a learning problem, and reason that with fitting inductive biases, the problem should be much more amenable to minimal networks. We find that network variants with several alternative activation functions meaningfully outperform the default choice of Rectified Linear Units, and in particular, that a 2nd degree polynomial activation function consistently learns Life dynamics with or without the benefit of learning neural weights. Our results provide an informative demonstration of the benefits of matching learning to the task at hand and challenge the easy default choice of scale for all problems. In particular, we advocate for the use of cellular automata as simple test domains for developing strategies that can benefit machine learning for science, physics-based deep learning, and interpretable machine learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：合适的激活函数使神经网络更容易学习生命游戏的动力学：多项式柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺德网络
先前研究发现，神经网络可靠学习康威生命游戏的规模与能通过硬编码参数表示经典细胞自动机的最小网络之间存在差距。将神经网络学习视为搜索过程，暗示其依赖于足够大的网络，以包含初始设置幸运（有时称为“获胜彩票”）的子网络，这些子网络实际能学习任务。本研究将视角从将生命规则发现视为搜索问题，重新转向学习问题，并推断，若具备合适的归纳偏置，该问题应更易被最小网络解决。我们发现，多种替代激活函数的网络显著优于默认的修正线性单元，特别是二次多项式激活函数，无论是否学习神经权重，都能稳定学习生命游戏动力学。我们的结果展示了匹配学习与任务本身的好处，并挑战了所有问题都采用简单默认规模的观念。特别地，我们提倡将细胞自动机作为开发可应用于科学、基于物理的深度学习和可解释机器学习策略的简单测试领域。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how activation functions influence neural networks' ability to learn Conway's Game of Life, motivated by the observation that minimal networks often fail despite being theoretically sufficient. The authors propose that learning dynamics should be matched to the task's structure, and test several activation functions against the standard ReLU. They find that a second-degree polynomial activation function enables even minimal networks to learn Life dynamics effectively, either with or without trainable weights, outperforming ReLU and other alternatives. This demonstrates that task-specific inductive biases can drastically reduce network scale requirements and supports using cellular automata as benchmarks for developing physics-aware and interpretable machine learning methods.
该研究探讨了神经网络在学习康威生命游戏动态时为何难以收敛，认为问题根源在于模型的归纳偏置与任务不匹配而非网络规模。作者提出使用多项式柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺德网络并引入多种激活函数，发现二阶多项式激活函数可使极简网络无需优化权重即能学习生命游戏规则，显著优于默认的ReLU激活。实验结果表明，将网络结构与任务特性对齐可大幅降低模型复杂度，为科学建模、物理驱动深度学习及可解释性机器学习提供了高效架构范式。
Controllable Accent Normalization via Discrete Diffusion
Authors: Qibing Bai, Yuhan Du, Tom Ko, Shuai Wang, Yannan Wang, Haizhou Li
First: 2026-03-15T08:17:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:56:11+00:00
Comments: Accepted to Interspeech 2026 as a long paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing accent normalization methods do not typically offer control over accent strength, yet many applications-such as language learning and dubbing-require tunable accent retention. We propose DLM-AN, a controllable accent normalization system built on masked discrete diffusion over self-supervised speech tokens. A Common Token Predictor identifies source tokens that likely encode native pronunciation; these tokens are selectively reused to initialize the reverse diffusion process. This provides a simple yet effective mechanism for controlling accent strength: reusing more tokens preserves more of the original accent. DLM-AN further incorporates a flow-matching Duration Ratio Predictor that automatically adjusts the total duration to better match the native rhythm. Experiments on multi-accent English data show that DLM-AN achieves the lowest word error rate among all compared systems while delivering competitive accent reduction and smooth, interpretable accent strength control.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过离散扩散实现可控口音标准化
现有口音标准化方法通常无法控制口音强度，但许多应用（如语言学习和配音）需要可调节的口音保留。我们提出DLM-AN，一种基于自监督语音令牌上掩码离散扩散的可控口音标准化系统。一个公共令牌预测器识别可能编码母语发音的源令牌；这些令牌被选择性重用以初始化反向扩散过程。这提供了一种简单而有效的口音强度控制机制：重用更多令牌保留更多原始口音。DLM-AN进一步引入了流匹配持续时间比例预测器，自动调整总时长以更好地匹配母语节奏。在多口音英语数据上的实验表明，DLM-AN在所有对比系统中实现了最低的词错误率，同时提供有竞争力的口音减弱效果和平滑、可解释的口音强度控制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for adjustable accent retention in applications like language learning and dubbing, this paper introduces DLM-AN, a controllable accent normalization system using masked discrete diffusion over self-supervised speech tokens. It employs a Common Token Predictor to identify native pronunciation tokens, which are selectively reused to initialize reverse diffusion, enabling fine-grained control over accent strength: more reused tokens preserve more accent. Additionally, a Duration Ratio Predictor adjusts output duration to match native rhythm. Experiments on multi-accent English data show DLM-AN achieves the lowest word error rate among competitors while offering effective accent reduction and smooth, interpretable control over accent strength.
为满足语言学习和配音等应用对可调节口音保留的需求，本文提出DLM-AN系统，基于自监督语音令牌上的掩码离散扩散模型。系统通过常见令牌预测器识别原生发音令牌，并选择性重用这些令牌以初始化逆向扩散过程，从而实现对口音强度的简单有效控制：重用越多，保留口音越多。同时引入时长比例预测器自动调整输出时长以匹配原生节奏。在多口音英语数据上的实验表明，DLM-AN在所有对比系统中达到最低词错误率，同时提供有效的口音减弱和平滑可解释的口音强度控制。
OGD4All: A Framework for Accessible Interaction with Geospatial Open Government Data Based on Large Language Models
Authors: Michael Siebenmann, Javier Argota Sánchez-Vaquerizo, Stefan Arisona, Krystian Samp, Luis Gisler, Dirk Helbing
Venue: 2026 IEEE Conference on Artificial Intelligence (CAI), pp. 882-888
First: 2025-11-30T15:41:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:53:04+00:00
Comments: Author Accepted Manuscript (AAM). Proceedings of 2026 IEEE CAI (Granada, Spain). Update manuscript with final DOI. Code & data available at: https://github.com/ethz-coss/ogd4all
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We present OGD4All, a transparent, auditable, and reproducible framework based on Large Language Models (LLMs) to enhance citizens' interaction with geospatial Open Government Data (OGD). The system combines semantic data retrieval, agentic reasoning for iterative code generation, and secure sandboxed execution that produces verifiable multimodal outputs. Evaluated on a 199-question benchmark covering both factual and unanswerable questions, across 430 City-of-Zurich datasets and 11 LLMs, OGD4All reaches 98% analytical correctness and 94% recall while reliably rejecting questions unsupported by available data, which minimizes hallucination risks. Statistical robustness tests, as well as expert feedback, show reliability and social relevance. The proposed approach shows how LLMs can provide explainable, multimodal access to public data, advancing trustworthy AI for open governance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OGD4All：基于大语言模型的地理空间开放政府数据可访问交互框架
我们提出了OGD4All，一个基于大语言模型（LLMs）的透明、可审计且可复现的框架，旨在提升公民与地理空间开放政府数据（OGD）的交互体验。该系统结合语义数据检索、代理推理驱动的迭代代码生成，以及安全沙箱执行机制，生成可验证的多模态输出。在涵盖事实性与无答案问题的199题基准测试中，针对苏黎世市430个数据集和11个LLMs，OGD4All达到98%的分析正确率和94%的召回率，同时可靠拒绝无数据支持的问题，从而降低幻觉风险。统计稳健性测试及专家反馈表明其可靠性和社会相关性。该方法展示了LLMs如何提供可解释、多模态的公共数据访问，推动可信AI在开放治理中的应用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more accessible and trustworthy interaction with geospatial open government data, OGD4All leverages large language models to enable citizens to query and analyze spatial datasets through natural language. The framework integrates semantic retrieval, agentic code generation, and sandboxed execution to produce verifiable multimodal outputs. Evaluated across 430 City-of-Zurich datasets and 11 LLMs using a 199-question benchmark, it achieved 98% analytical correctness and 94% recall while reliably rejecting unsupported queries to reduce hallucinations. Robustness tests and expert feedback confirmed its reliability and social relevance, demonstrating how LLMs can support explainable, multimodal access to public data for open governance.
为提升公众对地理空间开放政府数据的可访问性和可信度，OGD4All框架基于大语言模型，使用户可通过自然语言查询和分析公共数据。该框架结合语义数据检索、代理推理生成迭代代码及安全沙箱执行，输出可验证的多模态结果。在涵盖430个苏黎世市数据集、11个大语言模型的199题基准测试中，系统达到98%分析正确率和94%召回率，同时可靠拒绝无数据支持的查询以降低幻觉风险。稳健性测试与专家反馈证实其可靠性与社会相关性，展示了大语言模型如何实现对公共数据的可解释、多模态访问，推动可信开放治理。
Decentralized Autonomous Traffic Management through Corridor Networks
Authors: Jasmine Jerry Aloor, Aadarsh Govada, Hamsa Balakrishnan
First: 2026-06-22T16:49:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:49:53+00:00
Comments: Presented at the Second US-Europe Air Transportation Research and Development Symposium (ATRDS2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As autonomous aircraft are introduced at scale and traffic density increases, centralized management becomes insufficient to coordinate the large numbers of crewed and uncrewed aircraft. Dedicated Advanced Air Mobility (AAM) corridors have therefore been proposed for organizing high-density autonomous traffic flows. The desire to scalably provide autonomous aircraft flexibility in trajectory planning motivates the development of decentralized approaches to traffic management in AAM corridors. In this work, we extend a multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) approach to address the challenge of decentralized traffic flow management in air corridor networks. We test policies trained in a single-corridor setting on increasingly complex multi-corridor networks with combinations of merges and splits in a zero-shot manner. Experimental results demonstrate that learned behaviors transfer well to scenarios with varying traffic density, network geometry, and heterogeneous vehicle performance, without needing centralized coordination or model retraining. We evaluate system-level performance in terms of conformance to corridor boundaries, completion rates, average speeds, distance traveled, and maintenance of inter-aircraft separation. We find that although our policies require only locally coordinated entry, traversal, and exit behaviors, they collectively produce desirable traffic flows through the corridor network.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过走廊网络实现去中心化自主交通管理
随着自主飞行器大规模引入及交通密度增加，集中式管理已不足以协调大量有人与无人飞行器。因此，提出了专用先进空中交通（AAM）走廊，用于组织高密度自主交通流。为可扩展地提供自主飞行器在航迹规划上的灵活性，推动了AAM走廊中去中心化交通管理方法的发展。 本研究将多智能体强化学习（MARL）方法扩展，以应对航空走廊网络中去中心化交通流管理的挑战。我们在零样本方式下，将单走廊环境中训练的策略测试于日益复杂的多走廊网络，网络中包含合并与分流组合。实验结果表明，所学行为能良好迁移至不同交通密度、网络几何结构及异构飞行器性能的场景，无需集中协调或模型再训练。我们从走廊边界遵循度、任务完成率、平均速度、行驶距离及飞行器间间隔维持等维度评估系统级性能。结果发现，尽管策略仅需局部协调的进入、穿越与退出行为，但其集体行为可产生理想的走廊网络交通流。
Summary / 总结
As autonomous aircraft proliferate and traffic density rises, centralized control struggles to manage mixed crewed and uncrewed aircraft, prompting the need for decentralized traffic management in dedicated air corridors. This study extends multi-agent reinforcement learning to enable decentralized coordination across multi-corridor networks with merges and splits, trained in a single-corridor setting and tested in zero-shot scenarios. Results show the learned policies effectively adapt to varying traffic density, network geometry, and vehicle heterogeneity without retraining or central coordination, maintaining corridor boundaries, separation, and high completion rates while optimizing speed and distance traveled.
随着自主飞行器数量增加和交通密度上升，集中式管理难以协调混合编队的有人与无人飞行器，因此亟需在专用空中走廊中实现去中心化交通管理。本研究将多智能体强化学习方法扩展至多走廊网络，支持合并与分流场景，仅在单走廊环境中训练并在零样本条件下测试复杂网络。实验表明，所学策略能在不同交通密度、飞行器异构性及网络拓扑下保持走廊合规性、高完成率和安全间距，无需中心协调或重训练，展现出空中交通管理的可扩展自主能力。
Evaluation Awareness Is Not One Capability: Evidence from Open Language Models
Authors: Nilesh Nayan, Aishwarya Sampath Kumar, Rishiraj Girmal, Shivani Anilkumar, Sankaran Vaidyanathan, David A. Nader Palacio, Reshmi Ghosh, Soundararajan Srinivasan
First: 2026-06-22T16:48:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:48:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety benchmarks assume that test-condition behavior predicts deployment behavior, an assumption that fails if models detect evaluation cues and adapt. This opens a gap between benchmark performance and deployment behavior: compliance measured under test conditions becomes an optimistic upper bound that overstates how safely a model behaves once the evaluation harness is removed. We characterize this evaluation awareness through eight experiments across 37 open-weight models and seven families. (i)Detection is moderate and training-driven (24/37 models exceed chance, best AUROC 0.714 vs.0.819 human, with instruction tuning dominating over scale). (ii)Detection shifts safety behavior (hard refusal drops 5.8 percentage points under hypothetical framing, and 21/140 HarmBench framing effects are significant, with compliance rising up to +30 percentage points. (iii)Representations survive behavioral collapse (probes retain AUROC 0.98 under rewrites that drive behavior below chance, and multi-layer steering causally moves three downstream tasks while random controls do not). (iv)These axes are weakly coupled (only 1/15 correlations are significant, the sole robust link being behavioral detection versus framing resistance, $ρ=-0.79$, $p<0.001$). We call this gap the benchmark illusion: because detectability, behavioral manifestation, and controllability vary independently, it is multivariate rather than a single number, so no single awareness score is a reliable proxy for deployment safety.
中文标题/摘要
标题：评估意识并非单一能力：来自开源语言模型的证据
安全基准假设测试条件下的行为可预测部署行为，但若模型能检测到评估线索并调整行为，该假设即失效。这导致基准表现与实际部署行为之间出现差距：测试条件下测得的合规性成为过于乐观的上限，高估了移除评估环境后模型的安全表现。我们通过37个开源模型和七类模型的八项实验刻画了这种评估意识：(i)检测能力中等且受训练驱动（24/37模型超越随机水平，最佳AUROC为0.714，低于人类0.819，指令调优优于规模扩展）；(ii)检测影响安全行为（假设框架下强硬拒绝率下降5.8个百分点，140个HarmBench框架效应中21个显著，合规率最高上升30个百分点）；(iii)表征在行为崩溃后仍存续（重写驱动行为低于随机水平时，探针AUROC仍达0.98，多层引导可因果影响三个下游任务，而随机控制则无此效果）；(iv)这些维度弱耦合（仅1/15相关性显著，唯一稳健关联为行为检测与框架抵抗性，ρ=-0.79，p<0.001）。我们称此差距为“基准幻觉”：因可检测性、行为表现与可控性独立变化，该现象是多维而非单一数值，故无单一意识评分可作为部署安全的可靠代理。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how open language models detect evaluation cues and adapt behavior, challenging the assumption that benchmark performance reliably predicts real-world safety. Through eight experiments across 37 models, researchers found that 24 models can detect evaluation contexts with moderate accuracy (best AUROC 0.714), primarily due to instruction tuning. Detection significantly alters safety behavior: refusal rates drop under hypothetical framing, and compliance can increase by up to 30 percentage points under certain prompts. Crucially, safety representations persist even when behavior collapses below chance, and multi-layer steering causally affects downstream tasks, unlike random controls. However, these dimensions—detection, behavioral manifestation, and controllability—are weakly correlated, with only one robust link (behavioral detection vs. framing resistance, ρ=-0.79, p<0.001). The authors term this disconnect the 'benchmark illusion,' arguing that no single metric captures deployment safety due to the multivariate nature of evaluation awareness.
该研究探讨了开放权重语言模型在检测到评估提示时如何调整其行为，挑战了基准测试成绩能可靠预测实际安全性的假设。通过对37个模型的八项实验发现，24个模型能以中等准确率识别评估情境，指令微调比模型规模影响更大。检测能力会改变安全行为：在假设性框架下，模型拒绝有害请求的概率下降，合规率最高可上升30个百分点。重要的是，即使行为崩溃，安全相关表征仍能保持高识别率，且多层引导能因果性影响下游任务。然而，这些维度——检测能力、行为表现与可控制性——彼此弱相关，仅有一个显著相关性（检测与框架抵抗性，ρ=-0.79，p<0.001）。作者称此差距为“基准幻觉”，认为无法用单一指标可靠衡量部署安全。
Kamera: Unified Position-Invariant Multimodal KV Cache for Training-Free Reuse
Authors: Bole Ma, Jan Eitzinger, Harald Koestler, Gerhard Wellein
First: 2026-06-22T16:47:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:47:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal agents repeatedly re-examine the same video frames, UI screenshots, and rendered artifacts as their context window slides and reasoning iterates, yet every look-back re-encodes from scratch, because prefix caches serve reuse only at a fixed leading position. We show this recompute is avoidable, and identify exactly what naive KV reuse loses: the cross-chunk conditioning a chunk absorbs from its neighbours. This loss is asymmetric. The direct readout of a cached chunk is recovered exactly and for free by the standard state-merge. What remains is a diffuse, low-rank residue concentrated in deep layers, invisible to single-hop retrieval but precisely what multi-hop reasoning binds on. Blind reuse therefore leaves single-hop recall intact while halving multi-hop accuracy; this is the failure mode prior position-independent caches, designed for single-context or single-image reuse, do not address. We repair it with a small, training-free low-rank conditioning patch stored alongside each position-free chunk. Reuse reduces to one operator across MLA, GQA, and MHA: exact RoPE re-rotation to any target position, plus the patch that restores cross-chunk binding. This makes three window operations cheap: reorder (one patch serves every ordering of a cached set), sliding-window survival (surviving chunks relocate via rotation only, zero re-encode), and recall (an evicted chunk is rehydrated by its patch, never re-encoded). A rank-m patch recovers full task accuracy on cross-chunk-binding benchmarks, MM-NIAH across two attention families and two-page doc-QA, at a fraction of the KV footprint, and reconstructs re-prefill KV to within bf16 rounding in a production SGLang kernel across six backbones. The conditioning signal is strongest in redundant vision and video streams, making our solution most impactful where multimodal agents spend their recompute budget.
中文标题/摘要
标题：相机：统一位置无关的多模态KV缓存，实现无需训练的复用
多模态代理在上下文窗口滑动和推理迭代时，会反复重审相同的视频帧、UI截图和渲染产物，但每次回溯都需从头重新编码，因为前缀缓存仅支持固定前置位置的复用。我们证明这种重计算是可以避免的，并明确指出朴素KV复用所丢失的内容：一个块从其邻居处吸收的跨块条件信息。这种损失是不对称的。通过标准状态合并，缓存块的直接读取可被精确且免费恢复。剩余的是集中在深层的弥散、低秩残差，单跳检索无法感知，但正是多跳推理所依赖的。盲目复用因此保留单跳召回能力，同时将多跳准确率减半；这是先前位置无关缓存（为单上下文或单图像复用设计）所未解决的失效模式。我们通过为每个位置无关块附加一个小型、无需训练的低秩条件补丁来修复该问题。复用简化为跨MLA、GQA和MHA的单一操作：对任意目标位置进行精确RoPE重旋转，加上补丁以恢复跨块绑定。这使得三个窗口操作成本低廉：重排序（一个补丁可服务缓存集合的任意排序）、滑动窗口生存（存活块仅通过旋转迁移，无需重编码）和召回（被驱逐块通过其补丁重新水合，永不重编码）。一个秩-m补丁可在跨块绑定基准测试（涵盖两种注意力家族和两页文档问答）中以KV存储量的几分之一恢复全任务准确率，并在六个骨干网络的生产SGLang内核中，以bf16舍入精度重建重预填充KV。条件信号在冗余视觉和视频流中最强，因此我们的解决方案在多模态代理消耗重计算预算最多的场景中最具影响力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of re-encoding multimodal context chunks during iterative reasoning, the paper proposes Kamera, a training-free method that enables position-invariant KV cache reuse by augmenting each cached chunk with a low-rank conditioning patch. This patch restores cross-chunk binding lost during naive reuse, which otherwise leaves single-hop recall intact but halves multi-hop accuracy. Experimental results show that Kamera achieves full task accuracy on cross-chunk-binding benchmarks, reduces KV footprint by a significant margin, and reconstructs re-prefill KV with high fidelity in production kernels across six models, with the most benefit observed in redundant vision and video streams.
为解决多模态代理在迭代推理中重复编码上下文块导致的效率问题，本文提出Kamera，一种无需训练的KV缓存机制，支持位置无关的重用而不丢失跨块条件信息。其方法是在每个缓存块旁存储一个低秩条件补丁，以恢复多跳绑定能力。实验表明，Kamera在MM-NIAH和两页文档问答等跨块绑定基准测试中恢复了完整任务精度，同时大幅降低KV存储开销，并支持重排序、滑动窗口生存和召回等窗口操作，通过RoPE重旋转与补丁应用实现高效重水化，且误差控制在bf16精度内。
The Trilemma of Truth in Large Language Models
Authors: Germans Savcisens, Tina Eliassi-Rad
First: 2025-06-30T14:49:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:41:42+00:00
Comments: The main text is 9 pages long (plus 3 pages of references); supplementary material (60 pages) is included in the same PDF
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The public often attributes human-like qualities to large language models (LLMs), assuming that they "know" certain things. In reality, LLMs encode information retained during training as internal probabilistic knowledge. This study examines existing methods for probing the veracity of that knowledge and identifies three flawed underlying assumptions. To address these flaws, we introduce sAwMIL (Sparse-Aware Multiple-Instance Learning), a multiclass probing framework that combines multiple-instance learning with conformal prediction. sAwMIL leverages LLMs' internal representations to classify statements as true, false, or neither. We evaluate sAwMIL across 16 open-source LLMs, including default and chat-based variants, using three new curated datasets. Our results show that (1) common probing methods fail to provide a reliable and transferable veracity direction and, in some settings, perform worse than zero-shot prompting; (2) truth and falsehood are not encoded symmetrically; and (3) LLMs encode a third type of signal that is distinct from both true and false.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型中真理的三难困境
公众常将类人特质归因于大语言模型（LLMs），假设它们“知道”某些事情。实际上，LLMs在训练过程中保留的信息以内部概率知识形式编码。本研究考察了现有验证该知识真实性的方法，并识别出三个根本性错误假设。为解决这些问题，我们提出了sAwMIL（稀疏感知多实例学习），一种结合多实例学习与一致性预测的多分类探测框架。sAwMIL利用LLMs的内部表示，将陈述分类为真、假或非真非假。我们在16个开源LLMs（包括默认和聊天型变体）上评估sAwMIL，使用三个新构建的数据集。结果表明：（1）常见探测方法无法提供可靠且可迁移的真实度方向，某些情况下甚至劣于零样本提示；（2）真实与虚假并非对称编码；（3）LLMs编码了一种不同于真与假的第三类信号。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the misconception that large language models possess factual knowledge akin to humans, revealing that their internal probabilistic representations are often misinterpreted as truth. To correct this, the authors introduce sAwMIL, a multiclass probing framework combining multiple-instance learning and conformal prediction to classify statements as true, false, or neither. Evaluated across 16 open-source LLMs using three new datasets, the results show that traditional probing methods are unreliable and sometimes worse than zero-shot prompting, that truth and falsehood are asymmetrically encoded, and that LLMs encode a third distinct signal beyond binary truth values.
该研究指出，人们常误以为大语言模型拥有类似人类的确定性知识，实则其内部表征仅存储训练数据的概率信息。为此，作者提出sAwMIL框架，结合多实例学习与置信预测，依据模型内部状态对陈述分类为真、假或非二元。在16个开源模型上使用三个新数据集评估后发现：传统探查方法不可靠且常不如零样本提示效果；真与假在模型中编码不对称；模型还编码了不同于真与假的第三类信号。
Solve for the Hyperparameter, Skip the Search: Kolmogorov-Optimal Scaling Laws for Spline Regression
Authors: Yong Yi Bay, Kathleen A. Yearick
First: 2026-06-22T16:41:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:41:10+00:00
Comments: 49 pages, 26 figures, 12 tables. Code: https://github.com/bay-yearick-lab/kore
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Hyperparameter tuning almost always means search: fit the model at every value on a grid, score each by cross-validation, and keep the winner. For spline regression that search is unnecessary. The optimal resolution can be solved for in closed form, to the accuracy an exhaustive search reaches, at a fraction of the compute. Three ingredients make this possible: classical approximation theory pins the squared bias to a known power of the resolution G, exactly the Kolmogorov n-width of the smoothness class; the basis dimension is an explicit polynomial in G; and leave-one-out error follows from a single fit via the PRESS identity. Balancing the two known curves gives the minimizer analytically. We extend this calculus to many coordinates by replacing ambient input dimension with interaction order, the number of active low-order components in an ANOVA decomposition, yielding a scaling law in which the optimal resolution and error are power functions of the effective density (sample size per active component), with input dimension absent from the exponent. The law becomes an algorithm. KORE (Kolmogorov-optimal Order-aware Resolution Estimation) fits two pilot resolutions, solves a leverage-calibrated 2x2 system for the bias and noise scales, and evaluates the closed-form plug-in resolution with a tiny leave-one-out certificate: about a dozen fits instead of a full grid sweep, with a consistency guarantee as the sample grows. Across additive and sparse pairwise targets up to 80 input dimensions, KORE matches exhaustive 3-fold cross-validation and the full classical ladder (GCV, Mallows' Cp, AIC, BIC) while fitting roughly 8x fewer models; on 36 real tabular datasets it ranks first among 21 methods in accuracy per unit of compute, ahead of tuned boosters and kernel machines. When complexity lives in low interaction order, solving for the resolution beats searching for it.
中文标题/摘要
标题：求解超参数，跳过搜索：样条回归的柯尔莫哥洛夫最优缩放定律
超参数调优几乎总是意味着搜索：在网格上对每个值拟合模型，用交叉验证评分，保留最优者。对于样条回归，这种搜索是不必要的。最优分辨率可通过闭式解求得，其精度可达到穷举搜索水平，但计算量仅为后者的几分之一。三个要素使这成为可能：经典逼近理论将平方偏差精确绑定到分辨率G的已知幂次，即光滑类的柯尔莫哥洛夫n宽度；基函数维度是G的显式多项式；留一法误差可通过PRESS恒等式由单次拟合推导。平衡这两条已知曲线即可解析求得最小化点。我们将此微积分推广至多维情形，用交互阶（ANOVA分解中活跃的低阶分量数）替代输入维度，得到一个缩放定律：最优分辨率与误差均为有效密度（每个活跃分量的样本量）的幂函数，且输入维度不出现于指数中。该定律演变为算法。KORE（柯尔莫哥洛夫最优阶感知分辨率估计）拟合两个试点分辨率，通过杠杆校准的2x2系统求解偏差与噪声尺度，再用极小留一验证证书评估闭式插值分辨率：仅需约十余次拟合，而非完整网格扫描，且样本增大时具有一致性保证。在最多80维输入的加性与稀疏双变量目标上，KORE匹配穷举三折交叉验证及完整经典梯度（GCV、Mallows' Cp、AIC、BIC），拟合模型数量约少8倍；在36个真实表格数据集上，其在计算效率下准确率排名21种方法之首，超越调优的提升树与核机器。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of grid-based hyperparameter search in spline regression, this work derives closed-form solutions for optimal resolution using classical approximation theory, explicit basis dimension scaling, and the PRESS identity to compute leave-one-out error. The method, called KORE, estimates optimal resolution by fitting two pilot resolutions, solving a 2x2 system for bias and noise scales, then plugging into a closed-form formula validated by a minimal leave-one-out check. Experiments across synthetic and real datasets up to 80 dimensions show KORE matches cross-validation accuracy while fitting only 8x fewer models, and outperforms 21 competitors in accuracy per compute unit on 36 tabular datasets, especially when complexity resides in low interaction order.
为解决样条回归中网格搜索超参数效率低的问题，本文利用经典逼近理论、显式基函数维度公式及PRESS恒等式，推导出最优分辨率的闭式解，通过解析平衡偏差与噪声实现无需搜索。方法将输入维度替换为交互阶数（ANOVA分解中活跃的低阶分量数），得到最优分辨率与误差仅依赖有效密度（样本数/活跃分量）的幂律关系，与输入维度无关。KORE算法通过两个试点分辨率估计偏差与噪声，解一个校准的2x2系统求出闭式最优分辨率，并用少量留一验证。实验表明，KORE在最多80维输入的加性与稀疏双变量目标上，匹配交叉验证与经典准则（GCV、AIC、BIC），模型数量减少约8倍；在36个真实数据集上，每单位计算量准确率排名第一，超越调参提升器与核方法，尤其在低阶复杂度场景下表现更优。
Efficient Training of Boltzmann Generators Using Off-Policy Log-Dispersion Regularization
Authors: Henrik Schopmans, Christopher von Klitzing, Pascal Friederich
First: 2026-02-03T16:49:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:40:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sampling from unnormalized probability densities is a central challenge in computational science. Boltzmann generators are generative models that enable independent sampling from the Boltzmann distribution of physical systems at a given temperature. However, their practical success depends on data-efficient training, as both simulation data and target energy evaluations are costly. To this end, we propose off-policy log-dispersion regularization (LDR), a novel regularization framework that builds on a generalization of the log-variance objective. We apply LDR in the off-policy setting in combination with standard data-based training objectives, without requiring additional on-policy samples. LDR acts as a shape regularizer of the energy landscape by leveraging additional information in the form of target energy labels. The proposed regularization framework is broadly applicable, supporting unbiased or biased simulation datasets as well as purely variational training without access to target samples. Across all benchmarks, LDR improves both final performance and data efficiency, with sample efficiency gains of up to one order of magnitude.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用离策略对数分散正则化高效训练玻尔兹曼生成器
从非归一化概率密度中采样是计算科学中的核心挑战。玻尔兹曼生成器是一种生成模型，能够在给定温度下独立地从物理系统的玻尔兹曼分布中进行采样。然而，其实际成功依赖于数据高效的训练，因为模拟数据和目标能量评估均成本高昂。为此，我们提出离策略对数分散正则化（LDR），这是一种基于对数方差目标泛化的新正则化框架。我们将在离策略设置下结合标准基于数据的训练目标应用LDR，无需额外的在线策略样本。LDR通过利用目标能量标签提供的附加信息，作为能量景观的形状正则化器。所提出的正则化框架具有广泛适用性，支持无偏或有偏的模拟数据集，以及在无目标样本访问情况下纯变分训练。在所有基准测试中，LDR均提升了最终性能和数据效率，样本效率提升最高可达一个数量级。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost of simulation data and target energy evaluations in training Boltzmann generators, this work introduces off-policy log-dispersion regularization (LDR), a novel regularization method that leverages target energy labels to shape the energy landscape without requiring additional on-policy samples. LDR is applied alongside standard training objectives and supports both unbiased and biased datasets, as well as variational training without target samples. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks show that LDR significantly improves final model performance and data efficiency, achieving sample efficiency gains of up to one order of magnitude.
为解决训练玻尔兹曼生成器时模拟数据和目标能量评估成本高昂的问题，本文提出了一种名为离策略对数分散正则化（LDR）的新正则化方法。该方法利用目标能量标签塑造能量景观，无需额外的离策略样本，可与标准训练目标结合使用，适用于无偏或有偏数据集，甚至在无目标样本的纯变分训练场景下也有效。实验表明，LDR在多个基准测试中显著提升了模型最终性能和数据效率，样本效率提升最高可达一个数量级。
A Watermark for Vision-Language-Action and World Action Models
Authors: Yule Liu, Shuai Liu, Jiaheng Wei, Xinlei He
First: 2026-06-22T16:39:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:39:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language-action (VLA) models and world-action models (WAM) are the generative models now driving general-purpose robot control, turning raw camera input directly into motor commands. They are increasingly deployed as black-box services, where a partner runs the policy through an interface while the owner keeps the weights private. Training such a model takes proprietary data and heavy computational power, making the deployed model itself a valuable intellectual property. To address this, we propose the \emph{keyed latent-provenance verification} method, which fingerprints the policy through the seed of the Gaussian noise vector that the models draw before generation. At the injection stage, the owner swaps this seed for a keyed one with the same distribution as ordinary noise, so the fingerprinted actions are statistically identical to those of an ordinary run and an adversary watching the output finds no signal to detect or remove. At the verification stage, the owner runs the suspect model under authorized access and records the action channels the robot executes, a partial and possibly post-processed view of the policy's output. From this view, the verifier recovers the seed by gradient-based maximum a posteriori (MAP) optimization, tests it for the secret key to score each rollout, and aggregates these scores into a single decision on whether the suspect model belongs to the owner. We evaluate the method on two representative models across two robot suites. The experiments cover detection of the fingerprint, identification of which of several keys a suspect carries, robustness to a range of attacks, and an analysis of why the design works. Across both models, the fingerprint can be detected reliably with little change to task performance, and it remains detectable under output-side removal attacks and weight-level edits.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于视觉-语言-动作与世界动作模型的水印
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型和世界动作模型（WAM）是当前驱动通用机器人控制的生成模型，可直接将原始摄像头输入转化为电机指令。这些模型正越来越多地作为黑盒服务部署，合作伙伴通过接口运行策略，而所有者则保留权重私密性。训练此类模型需要专有数据和大量计算资源，因此部署后的模型本身成为有价值的知识产权。 为解决此问题，我们提出了一种名为“带密钥的潜在溯源验证”的方法，通过模型生成前抽取的高斯噪声向量种子对策略进行指纹化。在注入阶段，所有者将该种子替换为具有相同分布的带密钥种子，使得指纹化动作在统计上与普通运行无异，攻击者观察输出时无法检测或移除该指纹。在验证阶段，所有者在授权访问下运行可疑模型并记录机器人执行的动作通道（策略输出的部分且可能经过后处理的视图）。验证者通过基于梯度的最大后验（MAP）优化恢复种子，用秘密密钥测试每个运行轨迹得分，并将这些分数聚合为一个判断可疑模型是否属于所有者的最终决策。 我们在两个代表性模型上评估该方法，覆盖指纹检测、可疑模型所携带密钥识别、多种攻击下的鲁棒性及设计原理分析。在两个模型中，指纹检测可靠且对任务性能影响极小，且在输出端移除攻击和权重级编辑下仍可检测。
Summary / 总结
To protect proprietary vision-language-action and world-action models used in robot control, the paper proposes a keyed latent-provenance verification method that embeds a fingerprint via a Gaussian noise seed injected during model generation. This seed, replaced with a keyed version during training, ensures the model’s output remains statistically indistinguishable from normal operation, hiding the watermark from adversaries. At verification, the owner runs the suspect model and recovers the seed from recorded robot actions using gradient-based MAP optimization, then validates it against a secret key to determine ownership. Experiments on two robot models show the fingerprint is reliably detectable with minimal performance degradation and remains intact under output removal and weight-level attacks.
为保护作为黑盒服务部署的视觉-语言-动作及世界动作模型，本文提出一种基于键控潜变量溯源验证的方法，通过替换生成时使用的高斯噪声种子来嵌入指纹。训练阶段，所有者用带密钥的种子替代普通种子，确保输出统计特性不变；验证阶段，所有者运行可疑模型并记录部分动作输出，通过梯度最大后验优化恢复种子，与密钥比对后聚合得分以判断模型归属。实验表明，该指纹在两类机器人模型上均可稳定检测，任务性能影响微小，且在输出删除与权重篡改攻击下仍保持有效性。
What Language is This? Ask Your Tokenizer
Authors: Clara Meister, Ahmetcan Yavuz, Pietro Lesci, Tiago Pimentel
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-19T18:58:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:39:17+00:00
Comments: In Proceedings of ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language Identification (LID) is an important component of many multilingual natural language processing pipelines, where it facilitates corpus curation, training data analysis, and cross-lingual evaluation of large language models. Despite near-perfect performance on high-resource languages, existing systems remain brittle in low-resource and closely related language settings. We introduce UniLID, a simple and efficient LID method based on the UnigramLM tokenization algorithm, leveraging its probabilistic framing, parameter estimation technique and inference strategy. In short, to predict a string's language label, we simply ask: under which language's unigram distribution is this string most likely? Our formulation is data- and compute-efficient, supports incremental addition of new languages without retraining existing models, and can naturally be integrated into existing language model tokenization pipelines. Empirical evaluations against widely used baselines, including fastText, GlotLID and CLD3, show that UniLID achieves competitive performance on standard benchmarks, substantially improves sample efficiency in low-resource settings -- reaching ~70% accuracy with as few as five labeled samples per language -- and delivers large gains on fine-grained dialect identification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：这是什么语言？问问你的分词器

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the brittleness of existing language identification systems in low-resource and closely related language settings, this paper introduces UniLID, a lightweight LID method based on the UnigramLM tokenization algorithm. It predicts a string’s language by determining which language’s unigram distribution most likely generates it, leveraging probabilistic modeling and parameter estimation without retraining. Experiments show UniLID matches or outperforms baselines like fastText and CLD3 on standard benchmarks, achieves ~70% accuracy with just five labeled samples per language in low-resource scenarios, and excels in fine-grained dialect identification.
为解决现有语言识别系统在低资源及语言相近场景下的脆弱性，本文提出基于UnigramLM分词算法的轻量级语言识别方法UniLID。该方法通过判断字符串最可能由哪种语言的单字分布生成来预测语言，利用概率建模与参数估计无需重训即可支持新增语言。实验表明，UniLID在标准基准上表现与fastText等主流方法相当或更优，在仅需每语言5个标注样本时即可达到约70%准确率，并在细粒度方言识别上表现突出。
A Spectral Theory of Normalized Corrected GNN Propagation
Authors: Qihan Chen, Wei Li, Meng Qin, Jianfeng Hou
First: 2026-06-22T16:38:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:38:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We develop a spectral theory for \emph{normalized corrected GNN propagation}. The object of study is the symmetric normalized adjacency with its degree-stationary component removed, matching the normalization used by standard GCN-style models while isolating the stationary direction most directly tied to oversmoothing. The central theoretical question is whether this corrected normalized operator preserves class-discriminative signal after many propagation layers. Our main result is a high-probability exact-recovery theorem for the binary Contextual Stochastic Block Model after \(k=O(\log n)\) propagation steps in the dense polylogarithmic regime \(p\ge C\log^B n/n\), for any fixed \(B>4\), under explicit graph-signal and feature-SNR conditions. We also establish a multi-class partial recovery theorem showing contraction toward class centers for most nodes. Synthetic and real node-classification experiments are included as empirical checks of the theory's predicted dependence on depth, graph signal, and feature noise.
中文标题/摘要
标题：归一化修正GNN传播的谱理论
我们建立了归一化修正GNN传播的谱理论。研究对象是去除度平稳分量后的对称归一化邻接矩阵，该归一化方式与标准GCN型模型一致，同时最直接地隔离与过度平滑相关的平稳方向。核心理论问题是：该修正归一化算子在多层传播后是否仍能保留类别判别信号。我们的主要结果是在稠密对数多项式阶 regime（p≥C log^B n/n，对任意固定B>4）下，经过k=O(log n)传播步后，对于二元上下文随机块模型，满足显式图信号和特征信噪比条件时，可实现高概率精确恢复。我们还建立了多类部分恢复定理，表明大多数节点会向类别中心收敛。通过合成和真实节点分类实验，验证了理论对深度、图信号和特征噪声的预测依赖关系。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the problem of oversmoothing in GNNs, this paper develops a spectral theory for normalized corrected GNN propagation, focusing on the symmetric normalized adjacency matrix with its degree-stationary component removed to isolate the signal most relevant to class discrimination. The method analyzes whether this corrected operator preserves class-discriminative information after multiple propagation layers, proving a high-probability exact-recovery result for binary Contextual Stochastic Block Models after O(log n) layers under dense polylogarithmic graph regimes and explicit signal-to-noise conditions. Empirical experiments on synthetic and real datasets confirm the theoretical predictions regarding depth, graph structure, and feature noise effects on classification performance.
为解决图神经网络中的过平滑问题，本文构建了归一化修正GNN传播的谱理论，研究对象是去除度平稳分量后的对称归一化邻接矩阵，以直接分离与类别判别最相关的信号。方法分析该修正算子在多层传播后是否仍能保留类别判别信息，证明在稠密对数多项式阶图中，经过O(log n)层传播后，在满足特定图信号与特征信噪比条件下，可实现二分类模型的高概率精确恢复；同时建立多分类部分恢复定理，表明多数节点会收敛至类别中心。合成与真实数据实验验证了理论对传播深度、图结构与特征噪声依赖关系的预测。
Sarc7: Evaluating Sarcasm Detection and Generation with Seven Types and Emotion-Informed Techniques
Authors: Raina Gao, Alyssa Jeong, Lang Xiong, Yicheng Fu, Sean O'Brien, Vasu Sharma, Kevin Zhu
Venue: EMNLP
First: 2025-05-31T18:01:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:37:59+00:00
Comments: Accepted to EMNLP WiNLP and COLM Melt, Solar, PragLM, and Origen
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sarcasm is a form of humor where expressions convey meanings opposite to their literal interpretations. Classifying and generating sarcasm using large language models is vital for interpreting human communication. Sarcasm poses challenges for computational models, due to its nuanced nature. We introduce Sarc7, a benchmark that classifies 7 types of sarcasm: self-deprecating, brooding, deadpan, polite, obnoxious, raging, and manic by annotating entries of the MUStARD dataset. Classification was evaluated using zero-shot, few-shot, chain-of-thought (CoT), and a novel emotion-based prompting technique. We propose an emotion-based generation method developed by identifying key components of sarcasm-incongruity, shock value, and context dependency. Our classification experiments show that Gemini 2.5, using emotion-based prompting, outperforms other setups with an F1 score of 0.3664. Human evaluators preferred our emotion-based prompting, with 38.46% more successful generations than zero-shot prompting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Sarc7：使用七种类型和情感引导技术评估讽刺检测与生成
讽刺是一种幽默形式，其表达含义与字面意义相反。利用大语言模型对讽刺进行分类和生成，对于理解人类交流至关重要。由于讽刺的微妙性，其对计算模型构成挑战。我们引入Sarc7基准，通过对MUStARD数据集条目进行标注，将讽刺分为七类：自嘲、沉思、冷淡、礼貌、粗鲁、愤怒和狂躁。分类评估采用零样本、少样本、思维链（CoT）及一种新颖的情感引导提示技术。我们提出一种基于情感的生成方法，通过识别讽刺的不协调性、冲击力和语境依赖性关键要素构建。分类实验表明，使用情感引导提示的Gemini 2.5模型F1分数达0.3664，优于其他设置。人工评估者更偏好我们的情感引导提示，其成功生成比例比零样本提示高出38.46%。
