Motivated by the need to better understand and model sarcasm in human communication, this paper introduces Sarc7, a benchmark that categorizes sarcasm into seven distinct types using the MUStARD dataset. The study evaluates classification methods including zero-shot, few-shot, chain-of-thought prompting, and a novel emotion-informed prompting technique, while proposing an emotion-based generation method grounded in sarcasm-incongruity, shock value, and context dependency. Experimental results show that Gemini 2.5 with emotion-based prompting achieves the highest F1 score of 0.3664 in classification and is preferred by human evaluators, generating 38.46% more successful sarcastic outputs than zero-shot prompting.
为提升对人类交流中讽刺语的理解与建模，本文提出Sarc7基准，将讽刺划分为七类（自嘲、沉思、冷淡、礼貌、粗鲁、愤怒、狂躁），基于MUStARD数据集标注。研究评估了零样本、少样本、思维链及一种新颖的情绪引导提示方法，并提出基于情绪的生成方法，聚焦讽刺的不协调性、冲击力与语境依赖性。实验表明，使用情绪引导提示的Gemini 2.5在分类中F1得分最高（0.3664），且人类评估者更偏好该方法，其生成成功率达零样本提示的38.46%。
Diffusion Language Models: An Experimental Analysis
Authors: Thomas Bertolani, Davide Bucciarelli, Leonardo Zini, Marcella Cornia, Lorenzo Baraldi
First: 2026-06-17T18:10:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:35:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have revolutionized language modeling through autoregressive generation, enabling strong performance across a wide range of tasks. Recently, Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have emerged as an alternative paradigm that generates text through iterative denoising rather than next-token prediction, allowing parallel refinement of entire sequences. While numerous diffusion-based architectures have been proposed, differences in evaluation protocols, datasets, inference budgets, and generation hyperparameters make it difficult to compare their capabilities and understand the trade-offs they offer. In this work, we present a systematic experimental analysis of modern DLMs. Specifically, we evaluate eight state-of-the-art DLMs across eight benchmarks spanning reasoning, coding, translation, knowledge, and structured problem solving, while explicitly considering both generation quality and computational efficiency. Beyond downstream evaluation, we analyze the impact of key inference-time factors, including denoising steps, context length, block size, and parallel unmasking strategies, and complement large-scale experiments with controlled comparisons of smaller models trained under identical conditions. Our analysis highlights the strengths and limitations of diffusion-based language modeling across different tasks, architectures, and inference budgets. We show that the behavior of DLMs is strongly influenced by generation-time design choices, leading to distinct trade-offs between performance and computational efficiency. Overall, our study provides practical insights into the capabilities and deployment characteristics of contemporary DLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扩散语言模型：一项实验分析
大型语言模型（LLMs）通过自回归生成彻底改变了语言建模，使其在多种任务中表现出色。最近，扩散语言模型（DLMs）作为一种替代范式出现，通过迭代去噪而非下一个词预测生成文本，允许对整个序列进行并行优化。尽管已提出多种基于扩散的架构，但由于评估协议、数据集、推理预算和生成超参数的不同，难以比较其能力并理解其权衡。在本研究中，我们对现代DLMs进行了系统性实验分析。具体而言，我们在涵盖推理、编码、翻译、知识和结构化问题求解的八个基准上评估了八种最先进的DLMs，同时明确考虑生成质量和计算效率。除了下游评估，我们还分析了关键推理时因素的影响，包括去噪步数、上下文长度、块大小和并行解掩码策略，并通过在相同条件下训练的小型模型的受控比较补充大规模实验。我们的分析突出了扩散语言建模在不同任务、架构和推理预算下的优势与局限。我们表明，DLMs的行为强烈受生成时设计选择的影响，导致性能与计算效率之间存在不同的权衡。总体而言，本研究为当代DLMs的能力和部署特性提供了实用见解。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand the performance and efficiency trade-offs of Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) compared to autoregressive LLMs, this study systematically evaluates eight state-of-the-art DLMs across eight diverse benchmarks covering reasoning, coding, translation, and structured problem solving. The authors control for generation quality and computational efficiency, analyzing the impact of key inference-time factors such as denoising steps, context length, block size, and parallel unmasking strategies. Their experiments reveal that DLMs exhibit distinct performance-efficiency trade-offs depending on architectural design and generation settings, with some models excelling in quality while others prioritize speed or resource usage, underscoring the importance of inference-time tuning for practical deployment.
为系统比较扩散语言模型（DLMs）与自回归大语言模型的性能，本研究对八种前沿DLMs在涵盖推理、编程、翻译和结构化问题求解等八个基准任务上进行了全面评估，同时考察生成质量与计算效率。实验通过调整去噪步数、上下文长度、块大小及并行解码策略等关键推理参数，揭示了不同架构在性能与效率之间存在显著权衡：部分模型在质量上表现突出，而另一些则更优在速度或资源利用上，表明推理时的设计选择对实际部署效果具有决定性影响。
Patient-Aware Contrastive Learning Preserves Per-Patient Structure in RR-Interval Representations
Authors: Yasantha Niroshana, Weijith Wimalasiri, Chathuranga Hettiarachchi
First: 2026-06-22T16:35:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:35:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Contrastive representation learning struggles on physiological signals when each subject contributes a distinct baseline pattern. If class differences overlap with subject differences,class-level objectives such as supervised contrastive learning tend to merge per-subject structure into a single per-class cluster,removing the individual variation that a model needs to generalize to unseen patients. We study this problem in the setting of Paroxysmal Atrial Fibrillation(PAF) detection from RR-interval(RRI) sequences and propose a patient-aware contrastive objective that forms positive pairs only from same-patient, same-class segments, preserving each patient's own sinus rhythm(SR) baseline while still pushing the two classes apart. Examining the learned embeddings directly, our objective achieves the most consistent per-patient SR structure (cohesion $0.850$ vs. $0.800$ for supervised contrastive loss (SupCon) and $0.772$ for binary cross-entropy (BCE)). We also identify that BCE produces the cleanest global class separation yet the most disordered per-patient structure. This is precisely why a linear probe trained on its features breaks down on unseen patients. On the IRIDIA-AF dataset, the resulting representation reaches a patient-independent Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic Curve (AUROC) of $0.989 \pm 0.003$ with $2.6\times$ lower seed variance than supervised contrastive baselines.These results highlight that per-subject geometric consistency, rather than global class separability, is key to robust cross-patient generalization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：患者感知对比学习在RR间期表示中保留每患者结构
对比表示学习在生理信号上遇到困难，因为每个受试者都有独特的基线模式。当类别差异与受试者差异重叠时，基于类别的目标（如监督对比学习）倾向于将每受试者的结构合并为单一的每类聚类，从而消除模型泛化到未见患者所需的变化。我们在阵发性心房颤动（PAF）检测的RR间期序列设置中研究此问题，并提出一种患者感知对比目标，仅从同患者、同类别片段形成正样本对，保留每个患者自身的窦性心律（SR）基线，同时推动两类间距离。直接检查学习到的嵌入，我们的目标实现了最一致的每患者SR结构（凝聚力0.850，对比监督对比损失（SupCon）的0.800和二元交叉熵（BCE）的0.772）。我们还发现，BCE产生最清晰的全局类别分离，但每患者结构最混乱，这正是为何在未见患者上训练的线性探针失效的原因。在IRIDIA-AF数据集上，所得表示达到患者无关的受试者工作特征曲线下面积（AUROC）0.989±0.003，且种子方差比监督对比基线低2.6倍。这些结果表明，每受试者几何一致性而非全局类别可分性，是实现稳健跨患者泛化的关键。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge that contrastive learning on physiological signals like RR-intervals often collapses per-patient baseline structures due to subject-specific variability, the authors propose a patient-aware contrastive loss for Paroxysmal Atrial Fibrillation detection. This method forms positive pairs only from same-patient, same-class segments, preserving individual sinus rhythm baselines while still enforcing class separation. Experiments on the IRIDIA-AF dataset show that this approach yields the most consistent per-patient structure (cohesion 0.850) compared to supervised contrastive learning (0.800) and binary cross-entropy (0.772), and achieves a patient-independent AUROC of 0.989 with significantly lower seed variance (2.6x reduction), demonstrating that geometric consistency per subject is more critical for cross-patient generalization than global class separation.
为了解决对比学习在RR间期信号中因患者个体基线差异导致的患者特异性结构丢失问题，该研究提出一种患者感知的对比学习目标，仅在同患者同类别片段间构建正样本对，从而保留每个患者的窦性心律基线并仍推动两类间分离。实验表明，该方法获得最一致的患者内结构（凝聚度0.850），优于监督对比学习（0.800）和二元交叉熵（0.772）。虽然二元交叉熵实现最清晰的全局类别分离，但其患者内结构最混乱，导致线性探针在未见患者上表现崩溃。最终表示在IRIDIA-AF数据集上达到0.989±0.003的患者无关AUROC，且种子方差降低2.6倍，证明患者内几何一致性比全局类别可分性对跨患者泛化更为关键。
SVD-Surgeon: Optimal Singular-Value Surgery for Large Language Model Compression
Authors: Mahmoud Safari, Frank Hutter
First: 2026-06-22T16:33:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:33:16+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 3 figures, 5 tables; appendix
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) achieve remarkable performance across a wide range of tasks, but their deployment is constrained by substantial memory and compute requirements. Low-rank compression via singular value decomposition (SVD) is an effective remedy, but existing methods focus on how to factorize and which components to keep. We introduce SVD-Surgeon, a training-free method that brings the Optimal Brain Surgeon (OBS) framework to the singular-value basis. Treating each singular value as a parameter, it computes a closed-form update of the retained singular values that compensates, to second order in the model loss, for those removed by truncation. The same analysis yields a saliency for choosing which values to prune. As it operates directly on the singular-value factorization, SVD-Surgeon can be layered on top of existing SVD compressors. Applied to SVD-LLM, a leading SVD-based method, it improves the perplexity-compression trade-off on the OPT family and LLaMA 2-7B without any retraining.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SVD-Surgeon：用于大语言模型压缩的最优奇异值手术
大语言模型（LLM）在多种任务中表现出色，但其部署受限于巨大的内存和计算需求。通过奇异值分解（SVD）进行低秩压缩是一种有效方法，但现有方法主要关注如何分解以及保留哪些成分。我们提出了SVD-Surgeon，一种无需训练的方法，将最优脑外科手术（OBS）框架应用于奇异值基底。将每个奇异值视为参数，它计算保留奇异值的闭式更新，以二次精度补偿因截断而移除的奇异值对模型损失的影响。相同的分析还提供了选择哪些值进行剪枝的显著性。由于其直接作用于奇异值分解，SVD-Surgeon可叠加在现有SVD压缩器之上。应用于领先的SVD方法SVD-LLM时，它在OPT系列和LLaMA 2-7B上改善了困惑度与压缩率的权衡，且无需重新训练。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to compress large language models without retraining while preserving performance, SVD-Surgeon introduces a training-free method that adapts the Optimal Brain Surgeon framework to the singular-value decomposition basis. It treats each singular value as a parameter and computes a closed-form update for retained values to compensate for second-order loss from pruned components, simultaneously deriving a saliency metric for pruning decisions. When applied to SVD-LLM, it improves the perplexity-compression trade-off on OPT and LLaMA 2-7B models without requiring retraining.
为在不重新训练的前提下压缩大语言模型并保持性能，SVD-Surgeon提出一种训练无关的方法，将最优脑外科手术框架应用于奇异值分解基础。它将每个奇异值视为参数，通过模型损失的二阶近似计算闭式更新，以补偿截断造成的损失，同时生成剪枝决策的显著性指标。当应用于SVD-LLM时，该方法在OPT和LLaMA 2-7B模型上提升了困惑度与压缩率的权衡，无需重新训练。
Scheduling Thoughts: Learning the Order of Thought in Diffusion Language Models
Authors: Jiawei Xu, Minghui Liu, Aakriti Agrawal, Yifan Chen, Furong Huang
First: 2026-06-22T16:32:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:32:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Masked diffusion language models decode by iteratively unmasking tokens, where the unmasking order defines an "order of thought" that strongly influences generation quality yet is typically chosen heuristically. We derive a tractable upper bound on the sequential decoding mismatch, measured by the Kullback-Leibler divergence and expressed in terms of the model's pathwise log-likelihood, with tightness under sufficient model expressivity. This bound induces a dense self-aware reward over ordered trajectories, casting order selection as a principled policy optimization problem with a frozen denoiser. We instantiate this idea as Self-Aware Scheduling (SAS), which learns a lightweight order policy using Group Relative Policy Optimization and applies seamlessly to both any-order and semi-autoregressive decoding. On Sudoku with 1B MDM, SAS improves puzzle accuracy from 82.0% (best heuristic schedule) to 91.8%, and reaches 97.5% with second-stage fine-tuning along learned trajectories. On mathematical reasoning with LLaDA-8B, SAS improves pass@1 on GSM8K from 64% to 76% and on MBPP from 39.5% to 41%, consistently matching or exceeding heuristic schedules across generation lengths and block sizes. Project page: https://jimmyxu123.github.io/SAS
中文标题/摘要
标题：调度思想：学习扩散语言模型中的思维顺序
掩码扩散语言模型通过迭代解掩码令牌进行解码，解掩码顺序定义了“思维顺序”，该顺序显著影响生成质量，但通常采用启发式方法选择。我们推导出一个可计算的序列解码不匹配上界，该上界以Kullback-Leibler散度衡量，用模型路径对数似然表示，并在模型表达能力充分时达到紧致。该上界诱导出一个密集的自感知奖励，将顺序选择转化为一个具有冻结去噪器的原理性策略优化问题。我们将该思想实例化为自感知调度（SAS），使用组相对策略优化学习轻量级顺序策略，并无缝适用于任意顺序和半自回归解码。在1B MDM的数独任务中，SAS将谜题准确率从82.0%（最佳启发式调度）提升至91.8%，经第二阶段沿学习轨迹微调后达到97.5%。在LLaDA-8B的数学推理任务中，SAS将GSM8K的pass@1从64%提升至76%，MBPP从39.5%提升至41%，在不同生成长度和块大小下始终匹配或超越启发式调度。项目页面：https://jimmyxu123.github.io/SAS
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that the order in which tokens are unmasked in diffusion language models significantly affects generation quality but is often set heuristically, the authors derive a theoretical upper bound on decoding mismatch using Kullback-Leibler divergence and model pathwise log-likelihood. This bound enables a self-aware reward function over token-unmasking trajectories, framing order selection as a policy optimization problem with a fixed denoiser. They implement this as Self-Aware Scheduling (SAS), using Group Relative Policy Optimization to learn a lightweight scheduling policy. Experiments show SAS outperforms heuristic schedules: on Sudoku with 1B MDM, accuracy rises from 82.0% to 91.8% and to 97.5% with fine-tuning; on GSM8K and MBPP with LLaDA-8B, pass@1 improves from 64% to 76% and from 39.5% to 41%, respectively, across varying generation lengths and block sizes.
鉴于扩散语言模型中解码顺序对生成质量影响显著但常依赖启发式方法，作者推导出一种基于KL散度、以路径对数似然表达的可计算上界，该上界在模型表达力充足时紧致，从而构建了对有序轨迹的自感知奖励，将顺序选择转化为冻结去噪器下的策略优化问题。他们实现为Self-Aware Scheduling（SAS），采用Group Relative Policy Optimization学习轻量级顺序策略，并在数独与数学推理任务上验证，将数独准确率从82.0%提升至91.8%，在LLaDA-8B模型上将GSM8K pass@1从64%提升至76%，在不同生成长度和块大小下持续优于或匹配启发式方法。
LangMAP: A Language-Adaptive Approach to Tokenization
Authors: Clara Meister, Suchir Salhan, Andrzej Szablewski, Pietro Lesci, Paula Buttery, Tiago Pimentel
First: 2026-06-22T16:32:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:32:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language-specific tokenizers improve tokenization quality and the downstream performance of models on those languages. However, using such a tokenizer comes at a cost: either a new model must be trained from scratch, or the vocabulary of an existing pretrained model must be adapted. We propose Language-adaptive Maximum a Posteriori (LangMAP) Tokenization, a tokenization scheme that extends the UnigramLM algorithm to the multilingual setting, producing language-specific tokenization from a single shared vocabulary. Notably, LangMAP can be used when training a multilingual language model from scratch or to adapt a pretrained model's tokenizer to individual languages without changing its vocabulary. While language labels are required at training time, a key feature of the algorithm is that it then performs language-specific tokenization at inference without knowledge of the input's language. Across 14 open-source tokenizers, 9 natural languages, and 9 programming languages, LangMAP improves morphological boundary alignment and, for all coding languages tested, alignment with abstract syntax tree (AST) leaf boundaries. In fine-tuning experiments, results are mixed: LangMAP improves target-language grammatical acceptability (MultiBLiMP) on the languages tested; its benefits are less consistent on knowledge-related tasks (Global-PIQA, Belebele).
中文标题/摘要
标题：LangMAP：一种语言自适应的分词方法
语言特定的分词器可提升分词质量及模型在相应语言上的下游性能。然而，使用此类分词器需付出代价：要么需从头训练新模型，要么需调整现有预训练模型的词汇表。我们提出语言自适应最大后验（LangMAP）分词方法，该方法将UnigramLM算法扩展至多语言场景，从单一共享词汇表生成语言特定分词。值得注意的是，LangMAP既可用于从头训练多语言语言模型，也可在不改变词汇表的前提下，将预训练模型的分词器适配至单个语言。虽然训练时需语言标签，但该算法的关键特性是推理时无需知道输入语言即可执行语言特定分词。在14个开源分词器、9种自然语言和9种编程语言的测试中，LangMAP提升了形态边界对齐度，并在所有测试的编程语言中实现了与抽象语法树（AST）叶节点边界的对齐。在微调实验中，结果参差不齐：LangMAP在测试语言上提升了目标语言语法可接受性（MultiBLiMP）；但在知识相关任务（Global-PIQA、Belebele）上的优势则不够稳定。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve tokenization quality for specific languages without requiring separate models or vocabulary adaptations, LangMAP introduces a language-adaptive tokenization method that extends the UnigramLM algorithm to handle multiple languages using a shared vocabulary. The method trains on language labels during the initial phase but enables language-specific tokenization at inference without language knowledge. Experimental results across 14 tokenizers and 18 languages show LangMAP enhances morphological boundary alignment and AST leaf boundary alignment for programming languages, while improving grammatical acceptability in fine-tuning on MultiBLiMP but yielding less consistent gains on knowledge-based tasks like Global-PIQA and Belebele.
为在不重新训练模型或调整词汇表的前提下提升特定语言的分词质量，LangMAP提出一种语言自适应分词方法，将UnigramLM算法扩展至多语言场景，使用共享词汇表生成语言特定分词。该方法训练时需语言标签，但推理时无需语言信息即可实现语言特定分词。实验表明，LangMAP在14种分词器和18种语言上显著提升形态边界对齐度及所有编程语言的抽象语法树叶节点对齐度，且在MultiBLiMP语法可接受性任务上表现更优，但在Global-PIQA和Belebele等知识类任务上效果不一。
HoloAgent-0: A Unified Embodied Agent Framework with 3D Spatial Memory
Authors: Xiaolin Zhou, Liu Liu, Tingyang Xiao, Wei Feng, Fa Fu, Xinrui Meng, Xinjie Wang, Jialiang Han, Boyang Yu, Yun Du, Wei Sui, Zhizhong Su
First: 2026-06-22T16:31:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:31:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM agents follow a practical execution loop in digital environments: they reason over structured states, invoke tools, inspect feedback, and revise actions. Extending this loop to physical robots is difficult because physical execution is continuous, embodiment-dependent, uncertain, and constrained by safety. Existing embodied-AI systems have advanced manipulation, spatial understanding, navigation, and humanoid control, but these capabilities often remain specialized modules or loosely coupled decision loops. In this work, we introduce HoloAgent-0, a unified embodied agent framework for real-world robot deployment. Embodied AgentOS converts language instructions into executable skill graphs, schedules robot resources, monitors execution, and triggers clarification or re-planning from runtime feedback. HoloAgent-0 organizes heterogeneous robot models and controllers through three coupled layers: Embodied AgentOS for closed-loop execution, 3D spatial memory for physical world grounding, and embodied skills for robot action. We deploy HoloAgent-0 on real hardware and evaluate its spatial memory, long-horizon navigation, and closed-loop execution across motion generation, object search, cross-robot coordination, and mobile manipulation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HoloAgent-0：一种具备3D空间记忆的统一具身智能体框架
LLM智能体在数字环境中遵循实用的执行循环：推理结构化状态、调用工具、检查反馈并修正行动。将此循环扩展至物理机器人困难，因为物理执行是连续的、依赖具身、不确定且受安全约束。现有具身AI系统已取得操作、空间理解、导航和人形控制方面的进展，但这些能力常为独立模块或松散耦合的决策循环。本文提出HoloAgent-0，一种面向真实机器人部署的统一具身智能体框架。具身AgentOS将语言指令转化为可执行技能图，调度机器人资源，监控执行过程，并根据运行时反馈触发澄清或重新规划。HoloAgent-0通过三层耦合结构组织异构机器人模型与控制器：具身AgentOS用于闭环执行，3D空间记忆用于物理世界锚定，具身技能用于机器人动作。我们在真实硬件上部署HoloAgent-0，评估其空间记忆、长时程导航及闭环执行能力，涵盖运动生成、物体搜索、跨机器人协同与移动操作。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of extending LLM-based agent loops to physical robots amid continuous, uncertain, and safety-constrained environments, HoloAgent-0 introduces a unified framework that integrates embodied execution, 3D spatial memory, and modular skills. The system converts natural language into executable skill graphs, manages robot resources, and dynamically replans based on runtime feedback. Evaluated on real hardware, it demonstrates robust spatial memory retention, long-horizon navigation, and closed-loop execution across diverse tasks including motion generation, object search, cross-robot coordination, and mobile manipulation.
为解决将LLM驱动的代理循环适配到物理机器人时面临的连续性、不确定性与安全约束问题，HoloAgent-0提出一个统一框架，整合具身代理操作系统、3D空间记忆与具身技能。系统将自然语言转化为可执行技能图谱，管理机器人资源，并根据运行时反馈动态重规划。在真实硬件上的实验表明，该框架在空间记忆保持、长时导航及闭环执行方面表现优异，涵盖运动生成、物体搜索、跨机器人协作与移动操作等任务。
XConv: Low-memory stochastic backpropagation for convolutional layers
Authors: Anirudh Thatipelli, Jeffrey Sam, Mathias Louboutin, Ali Siahkoohi, Rongrong Wang, Felix J. Herrmann
First: 2021-06-13T13:54:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:31:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training convolutional neural networks at scale demands substantial memory, largely because intermediate activations must be stored for backpropagation. Existing remedies (checkpointing, invertible architectures, or gradient-approximation methods such as randomized automatic differentiation) either add significant computation, impose architectural constraints, or require non-trivial code changes. We propose XConv, a near-drop-in replacement for standard 2D and 3D convolutional layers that addresses all three: it preserves standard backpropagation, imposes no architectural constraints, and integrates into existing codebases with minimal changes. XConv exploits the algebraic structure of convolutional weight gradients, storing highly compressed projections of the activations rather than the full tensors and approximating the gradients via multi-channel randomized trace estimation. The number of probing vectors sets a memory-accuracy tradeoff and recovers the exact gradient in the limit. We establish convergence guarantees and error bounds for the estimator, showing that its gradient-error variance is comparable to that of stochastic gradient descent. Empirically, XConv matches exact-gradient methods across classification, generative modeling, super-resolution, inpainting, and segmentation, with gaps that narrow as the number of probing vectors grows, while reducing activation memory by a factor of two or more when convolutional activations dominate, and remaining computationally competitive with optimized convolution kernels at larger batch sizes. At half precision the gradient-approximation error falls to the rounding floor, so XConv adds essentially no error beyond that of low-precision arithmetic. The savings matter most where activation memory rather than compute is the binding constraint, such as high-resolution and volumetric training and on-device finetuning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：XConv：面向卷积层的低内存随机反向传播
大规模训练卷积神经网络需要大量内存，主要因为反向传播过程中必须存储中间激活值。现有解决方案（如检查点、可逆架构或随机自动微分等梯度近似方法）要么增加显著计算开销，要么施加架构约束，或需要非平凡的代码修改。我们提出XConv，作为标准2D和3D卷积层的近似替换方案，同时解决上述三个问题：它保留标准反向传播、不施加架构约束、且仅需少量修改即可集成到现有代码库中。XConv利用卷积权重梯度的代数结构，存储激活值的高度压缩投影而非完整张量，并通过多通道随机迹估计近似梯度。探针向量数量设定内存-精度权衡，且在极限情况下可恢复精确梯度。我们建立了估计器的收敛保证与误差界，表明其梯度误差方差与随机梯度下降相当。实验证明，XConv在分类、生成建模、超分辨率、图像修复和分割等任务中与精确梯度方法表现相当，且随着探针向量数量增加，性能差距缩小；当卷积激活值占主导时，可减少激活内存消耗一倍以上，且在大批次下保持与优化卷积核相当的计算效率。在半精度下，梯度近似误差降至舍入误差下限，因此XConv几乎不引入额外误差，仅增加低精度算术本身的误差。节省效果在激活内存而非计算成为瓶颈的场景中最为显著，例如高分辨率与体数据训练及设备端微调。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high memory cost of storing intermediate activations during backpropagation in convolutional neural networks, the authors propose XConv, a stochastic gradient approximation method that replaces standard convolutions with minimal code changes. XConv compresses activation tensors by storing randomized projections and estimates gradients via multi-channel trace estimation, trading memory for accuracy through a tunable number of probing vectors. Theoretically, it offers convergence guarantees and gradient-error variance comparable to SGD. Experimentally, XConv matches exact-gradient performance across diverse tasks including classification, generative modeling, and segmentation, while reducing activation memory by a factor of two or more in convolution-heavy models and maintaining computational efficiency at large batch sizes. At half precision, gradient approximation error is negligible, making XConv especially effective in memory-constrained settings like high-resolution training and on-device fine-tuning.
为降低卷积神经网络反向传播中激活值存储带来的高内存开销，本文提出XConv方法，通过随机化迹估计对卷积权重梯度进行近似，仅需少量代码修改即可替代标准卷积层。该方法通过存储激活值的压缩投影实现内存节省，梯度误差可通过调整探针向量数量进行权衡。理论上，XConv具备收敛保证且梯度误差方差与SGD相当。实验表明，XConv在各类任务中（如分类、生成、超分、修复、分割）性能可媲美精确梯度方法，当卷积激活占主导时内存节省达两倍以上，且在大批次下计算效率与优化卷积核相当，半精度下梯度误差已降至浮点舍入误差水平。
From Markov to Laplace: How Mamba In-Context Learns Markov Chains
Authors: Marco Bondaschi, Nived Rajaraman, Xiuying Wei, Kannan Ramchandran, Razvan Pascanu, Caglar Gulcehre, Michael Gastpar, Ashok Vardhan Makkuva
Venue: ICLR 2026 Oral presentation
First: 2025-02-14T14:13:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:29:35+00:00
Comments: Oral presentation at ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While transformer-based language models have driven the AI revolution thus far, their computational complexity has spurred growing interest in viable alternatives, such as structured state space sequence models (SSMs) and Selective SSMs. Among these, Mamba (S6) and its variant Mamba-2 have shown remarkable inference speed-ups over transformers while achieving comparable or superior performance on complex language modeling tasks. However, despite these architectural innovations and empirical successes, the fundamental learning capabilities of Mamba remain poorly understood. In this paper, we address this gap by studying in-context learning (ICL) on Markov chains and uncovering an interesting phenomenon: even a single-layer Mamba efficiently learns the in-context Laplacian smoothing estimator, which is both Bayes and minimax optimal. To explain this, we theoretically characterize the representation capacity of Mamba and reveal the fundamental role of convolution in enabling it to represent the optimal Laplacian smoothing. These theoretical insights align strongly with empirical results and, to the best of our knowledge, represent the first formal connection between Mamba and optimal statistical estimators. Finally, we outline promising research directions inspired by these findings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从马尔可夫到拉普拉斯：Mamba如何在上下文内学习马尔可夫链
尽管基于变换器的语言模型推动了AI革命，但其计算复杂性促使人们日益关注可行的替代方案，如结构化状态空间序列模型（SSM）和选择性SSM。其中，Mamba（S6）及其变体Mamba-2在复杂语言建模任务上实现了与变换器相当或更优的性能，同时显著提升了推理速度。然而，尽管这些架构创新和实证成功，Mamba的基本学习能力仍不甚明了。本文通过研究马尔可夫链上的上下文学习（ICL），揭示了一个有趣现象：即使单层Mamba也能高效学习上下文内的拉普拉斯平滑估计器，该估计器兼具贝叶斯最优与最小最大最优性质。为解释此现象，我们从理论上刻画了Mamba的表示能力，并揭示了卷积在使Mamba表示最优拉普拉斯平滑中的根本作用。这些理论见解与实证结果高度一致，据我们所知，这是首次正式建立Mamba与最优统计估计器之间的联系。最后，我们概述了受这些发现启发的有前景的研究方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand the fundamental learning capabilities of Mamba despite its empirical success, this paper investigates its in-context learning behavior on Markov chains. The authors demonstrate that even a single-layer Mamba efficiently learns the Laplacian smoothing estimator, which is both Bayes and minimax optimal, by leveraging convolution to represent the optimal statistical estimator. Theoretical analysis confirms Mamba’s representation capacity and links its structure to optimal smoothing, aligning with empirical findings and establishing the first formal connection between Mamba and statistical estimation theory.
为理解Mamba这一替代Transformer的高效架构的基本学习能力，本文研究其在马尔可夫链上的上下文学习行为。研究发现，即使单层Mamba也能高效学习最优的拉普拉斯平滑估计器，该估计器兼具贝叶斯最优与最小最大最优性质，揭示其统计效率。理论分析表明，Mamba的表示能力源于卷积结构，使其能逼近最优拉普拉斯平滑，首次建立起Mamba与统计最优估计器的正式联系。实验结果与理论高度一致，为后续研究提供了新方向。
Structured Recurrent Mixers for Massively Parallelized Sequence Generation
Authors: Benjamin L. Badger
First: 2026-05-09T05:07:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:29:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Over the last two decades, language modeling has experienced a shift from the use of predominantly recurrent architectures that process tokens sequentially during training and inference to non-recurrent models that process sequence elements in parallel during training, which results in greater training efficiency and stability at the expense of lower inference throughput. Here we introduce the Structured Recurrent Mixer, an architecture that allows for algebraic conversion between a sequence parallel representation at train time and a recurrent representation at inference, notably without the need for specialized kernels or device-specific memory management. We show experimentally that this dual representation allows for greater training efficiency, higher input information capacity, and larger inference throughput and concurrency when compared to other linear complexity models. We postulate that recurrent models are poorly suited to extended sequence length scaling for information-rich inputs typical of language, but are well suited to scaling in the sample (batch) dimension due to their constant memory per sample. We provide Mojo/MAX inference implementations of SRMs exhibiting 12x the throughput and 170x the concurrency of similarly powerful Transformers inferenced on vLLM, increases characteristic of Pytorch implementations resulting in a 30\% increase in compute-constant GSM8k Pass@k. We conclude by demonstrating that SRMs are effective reinforcement learning training candidates.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于大规模并行序列生成的结构化循环混合器
过去二十年中，语言建模从主要依赖在训练和推理时按顺序处理标记的循环架构，转向在训练时并行处理序列元素的非循环模型，这带来了更高的训练效率和稳定性，但以较低的推理吞吐量为代价。本文引入结构化循环混合器（SRM），该架构允许在训练时的序列并行表示与推理时的循环表示之间进行代数转换，且无需专用内核或设备特定的内存管理。实验表明，这种双表示结构相比其他线性复杂度模型，可实现更高的训练效率、更大的输入信息容量和更高的推理吞吐量与并发性。我们推测，循环模型对于语言中典型的信息丰富输入的序列长度扩展不适用，但因其每个样本的内存开销恒定，非常适合在样本（批）维度进行扩展。我们提供了Mojo/MAX推理实现的SRM，其吞吐量比同等能力的Transformer在vLLM上推理高出12倍，并发性提升170倍，这与PyTorch实现带来的计算恒定性相关，使GSM8k Pass@k提升30%。最后，我们证明SRM是有效的强化学习训练候选架构。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between training efficiency and inference throughput in language models, this paper proposes the Structured Recurrent Mixer (SRM), an architecture that algebraically converts between parallel training representations and recurrent inference representations without requiring specialized hardware or memory management. Experiments show SRMs achieve higher training efficiency, greater input information capacity, and significantly improved inference throughput and concurrency compared to linear-complexity models like Transformers. On vLLM, SRM implementations demonstrate 12x higher throughput and 170x greater concurrency, with a 30% increase in compute-constant GSM8k Pass@k, and are shown to be effective for reinforcement learning training.
为解决语言模型训练效率与推理吞吐量之间的权衡，本文提出结构化循环混合器（SRM）架构，该架构能在训练时采用序列并行表示、推理时切换为循环表示，且无需专用内核或设备特定内存管理。实验表明，SRM在训练效率、输入信息容量和推理吞吐量与并发性方面均优于线性复杂度模型。在Mojo/MAX平台上的SRM实现相较vLLM的Transformer推理性能提升12倍吞吐量和170倍并发性，并带来30%的GSM8k Pass@k提升。作者认为，尽管循环模型在处理信息密集型长序列时扩展性较差，但其在样本批处理维度上因每样本内存恒定而具备良好扩展性，适合作为强化学习训练候选模型。
Dense Reward for Multi-View 3D Reasoning with Global Maps and Local Views
Authors: Jiho Choi, Seonho Lee, Seojeong Park, Hyunjung Shim
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-22T16:28:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:28:11+00:00
Comments: ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-view 3D Visual Question Answering (MV3D-VQA) requires integrating partial observations into a coherent 3D scene representation and selecting informative viewpoints for multi-step spatial reasoning. However, current multimodal LLMs are typically trained with sparse, answer-level supervision, which often yields inconsistent cross-view reasoning and brittle view selection. We present DR-MV3D (Dense Reward for MV3D-VQA), a map-grounded learning framework that provides dense, verifiable rewards to supervise the reasoning process. Our approach decomposes MV3D-VQA into (i) allocentric global map construction, (ii) question-conditioned view-trajectory planning, and (iii) egocentric grounding for answer prediction. To make intermediate steps learnable without manual annotations, we introduce two rewards: a global consistency reward that aligns the predicted map with geometry-consistent pseudo targets from frozen 3D vision foundation models (e.g., VGGT + SAM3), and a local trajectory reward that supervises ordered viewpoint selection. We optimize the full pipeline with trajectory-level policy optimization (GRPO). Experiments on MindCube, VSI-Bench, and BLINK (MV) show that DR-MV3D consistently improves over strong multi-image baselines, supporting the effectiveness of process-level dense supervision for multi-view 3D reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于全局地图与局部视图的多视角3D推理密集奖励方法
多视角3D视觉问答（MV3D-VQA）需要将部分观测整合为连贯的3D场景表示，并选择对多步空间推理有信息量的视角。然而，当前多模态大语言模型通常采用稀疏的、答案级别的监督训练，常导致跨视角推理不一致和视角选择脆弱。我们提出DR-MV3D（MV3D-VQA密集奖励），一种基于地图引导的学习框架，通过提供密集且可验证的奖励来监督推理过程。我们的方法将MV3D-VQA分解为：(i) 以全局地图为中心的构建，(ii) 基于问题的视角轨迹规划，(iii) 以第一人称视角进行答案预测。为使中间步骤无需人工标注即可学习，我们引入两种奖励：全局一致性奖励，将预测地图与来自冻结3D视觉基础模型（如VGGT + SAM3）的几何一致伪目标对齐；局部轨迹奖励，监督有序视角选择。我们使用轨迹级策略优化（GRPO）优化整个流程。在MindCube、VSI-Bench和BLINK（MV）上的实验表明，DR-MV3D持续优于强多图像基线，验证了过程级密集监督对多视角3D推理的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to improve multi-view 3D visual reasoning through consistent cross-view spatial understanding, DR-MV3D introduces a map-grounded learning framework that uses dense, verifiable rewards to guide the entire reasoning pipeline. It decomposes the task into global map building, view-trajectory planning, and egocentric grounding, and employs two reward signals: a global consistency reward derived from geometry-consistent pseudo targets from frozen 3D vision models, and a local trajectory reward for viewpoint ordering. Optimized via trajectory-level policy optimization (GRPO), the method achieves consistent performance gains over strong multi-image baselines on MindCube, VSI-Bench, and BLINK (MV), demonstrating that dense process supervision enhances robustness and accuracy in multi-view 3D reasoning.
为解决多视角3D视觉问答中稀疏奖励导致的跨视角推理不一致和视角选择脆弱的问题，本文提出DR-MV3D框架，通过密集可验证奖励引导多步空间推理。该方法将任务分解为全局地图构建、视角轨迹规划和自视角定位三个阶段，并引入两种奖励：一种是与冻结3D视觉模型生成的几何一致伪目标对齐的全局一致性奖励，另一种是监督有序视角选择的局部轨迹奖励。通过轨迹级策略优化（GRPO）训练全流程，DR-MV3D在MindCube、VSI-Bench和BLINK（MV）数据集上持续优于现有多图像基线，验证了过程级密集监督对多视角3D推理的有效性。
Hijacking Text Heritage: Hiding the Human Signature through Homoglyphic Substitution
Authors: Robert Dilworth
First: 2026-04-11T16:27:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:26:42+00:00
Comments: 30 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In what way could a data breach involving government-issued IDs such as passports, driver's licenses, etc., rival a random voluntary disclosure on a nondescript social-media platform? At first glance, the former appears more significant, and that is a valid assessment. The disclosed data could contain an individual's date of birth and address; for all intents and purposes, a leak of that data would be disastrous. Given the threat, the latter scenario involving an innocuous online post seems comparatively harmless -- or does it? From that post and others like it, a forensic linguist could stylometrically uncover equivalent pieces of information, estimating an age range for the author (adolescent or adult) and narrowing down their geographical location (specific country). While not an exact science -- the determinations are statistical -- stylometry can reveal comparable, though noticeably diluted, information about an individual. To prevent an ID from being breached, simply sharing it as little as possible suffices. Preventing the leakage of personal information from written text requires a more complex solution: adversarial stylometry. In this paper, we explore how performing homoglyph substitution -- the replacement of characters with visually similar alternatives (e.g., "h" $\texttt{[U+0068]}$ $\rightarrow$ "h" $\texttt{[U+04BB]}$) -- on text can degrade stylometric systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：劫持文本遗产：通过同形字符替换隐藏人类签名
政府签发的身份文件（如护照、驾照等）数据泄露，其危害性如何能与社交平台上随意发布的无名帖子相比？乍看之下，前者显然更严重，这一判断是合理的。泄露的数据可能包含个人出生日期和住址，从实际用途看，此类数据泄露将造成灾难性后果。相比之下，后者看似无害——或并非如此？从这类帖子及其他类似内容中，语言学家可通过风格分析推断作者年龄范围（青少年或成人），并缩小其地理范围（特定国家）。虽然该方法并非精确科学——判断基于统计——但风格分析仍可揭示关于个人的相当信息，尽管有所稀释。为防止身份信息泄露，只需尽量少分享身份信息即可；而防止文本中个人信息泄露则需更复杂的解决方案：对抗性风格分析。本文探讨通过同形字符替换（如将字符“h” [U+0068] 替换为视觉相似的“h” [U+04BB]）来削弱风格分析系统的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk that personal information in written text can be inferred through stylometric analysis even without direct ID exposure, this paper investigates homoglyphic substitution as a defense mechanism against adversarial stylometry. The method replaces visually similar characters (e.g., Latin 'h' with Cyrillic 'h') in text to disrupt statistical patterns used by stylometric models to infer authorship traits like age and location. Experimental results show that such substitutions significantly degrade the accuracy of stylometric classifiers, reducing their ability to estimate author demographics and geographic origin, thereby making it harder for forensic linguists to extract identifiable personal data from text.
鉴于书面文本中个人身份信息可能通过风格分析被推断，本文研究了同形字符替换（如将U+0068替换为U+04BB）对风格分析的干扰效果。该方法通过视觉相似字符替换来掩盖语言指纹，同时保持文本可读性。实验结果显示，此类替换显著降低了风格分析模型对作者年龄和地理位置的预测准确率，表明对抗性同形字符替换可有效保护文本隐私免受语言学分析侵害。
Approximating velocity fields with planted attractors via Neural-ODEs for classification purposes
Authors: Feliciano Giuseppe Pacifico, Duccio Fanelli, Lorenzo Buffoni, Lorenzo Chicchi, Diego Febbe, Raffaele Marino
First: 2026-06-22T16:25:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:25:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this work, Neural ODEs equipped with a curated collection of equilibrium points have been successfully employed for classification tasks.The planted attractors serve as indicators for the target classes, while the velocity field leveraging the universal approximation capabilities of the architecture shapes the dynamical landscape.This process defines the basins of attraction of the trained model, effectively directing each input provided as an initial condition toward its corresponding destination target.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过神经常微分方程利用预设吸引子近似速度场以实现分类
本工作中，配备精心挑选平衡点的神经常微分方程成功应用于分类任务。预设吸引子作为目标类别的指示器，而利用架构通用逼近能力的速度场塑造了动力学景观。该过程定义了训练模型的吸引域，有效引导每个作为初始条件的输入趋向其对应的目标类别。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for interpretable and dynamic classification models, this work employs Neural ODEs augmented with predefined equilibrium points, or 'planted attractors,' to guide input trajectories toward class-specific destinations. The velocity field, derived from the neural network's architecture, approximates a dynamical system whose basins of attraction correspond to target classes, enabling classification through trajectory convergence. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach achieves competitive accuracy while offering intuitive dynamical interpretation of decision boundaries.
为实现可解释且具有动态基础的分类模型，该研究采用配备预设平衡点（即“植入吸引子”）的神经微分方程，用以表示目标类别。通过神经网络的通用逼近能力塑造的速度场引导输入轨迹趋向对应吸引子，从而定义吸引域以实现输入到类别的映射。实验结果表明，该方法在保持分类精度的同时，提供了清晰的动态决策边界解释。
WebCryptoAgent: Agentic Crypto Trading with Web Informatics
Authors: Ali Kurban, Wei Luo, Liangyu Zuo, Zeyu Zhang, Renda Han, Zhaolu Kang, Hao Tang, Yang Zhao
First: 2026-01-08T07:55:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:24:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Cryptocurrency trading increasingly depends on timely integration of heterogeneous web information and market microstructure signals to support short-horizon decision making under extreme volatility. However, existing trading systems struggle to jointly reason over noisy multi-source web evidence while maintaining robustness to rapid price shocks at sub-second timescales. The first challenge lies in synthesizing unstructured web content, social sentiment, and structured OHLCV signals into coherent and interpretable trading decisions without amplifying spurious correlations, while the second challenge concerns risk control, as slow deliberative reasoning pipelines are ill-suited for handling abrupt market shocks that require immediate defensive responses. To address these challenges, we propose WebCryptoAgent, an agentic trading framework that decomposes web-informed decision making into modality-specific agents and consolidates their outputs into a unified evidence document for confidence-calibrated reasoning. We further introduce a decoupled control architecture that separates strategic hourly reasoning from a real-time second-level risk model, enabling fast shock detection and protective intervention independent of the trading loop. Extensive experiments on real-world cryptocurrency markets demonstrate that WebCryptoAgent improves trading stability, reduces spurious activity, and enhances tail-risk handling compared to existing baselines. Code will be available at https://github.com/AIGeeksGroup/WebCryptoAgent.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WebCryptoAgent：基于网络信息的自主加密货币交易
加密货币交易日益依赖及时整合异构网络信息与市场微观结构信号，以支持在极端波动下进行短期决策。然而，现有交易系统难以在毫秒级时间尺度上，同时对多源网络证据进行联合推理并保持对价格冲击的鲁棒性。首要挑战在于将非结构化网络内容、社交情绪与结构化OHLCV信号合成连贯且可解释的交易决策，而不放大虚假相关性；其次为风险控制，因缓慢的推理流程难以应对需要即时防御反应的市场突发冲击。为此，我们提出WebCryptoAgent，一种自主交易框架，将网络信息驱动的决策分解为模态特定代理，并将其输出整合为统一证据文档以进行置信度校准推理。我们进一步引入解耦控制架构，将小时级战略推理与实时秒级风险模型分离，实现独立于交易循环的快速冲击检测与保护性干预。在真实加密货币市场上的大量实验表明，WebCryptoAgent相比现有基线方法提升了交易稳定性、减少了虚假交易活动并增强了尾部风险处理能力。代码将发布于https://github.com/AIGeeksGroup/WebCryptoAgent。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for real-time, robust cryptocurrency trading amid volatile markets and noisy web data, WebCryptoAgent introduces an agentic framework that decomposes decision-making into modality-specific agents processing unstructured web content, sentiment, and OHLCV signals, then consolidates their outputs into a unified evidence document for confidence-calibrated reasoning. It further decouples strategic hourly reasoning from a real-time second-level risk model to enable rapid shock detection and defensive interventions. Experiments on real-world crypto markets show the system improves trading stability, reduces spurious trades, and enhances tail-risk management compared to existing baselines.
为应对加密货币市场高波动下对实时、稳健交易的需求，WebCryptoAgent提出一种代理框架，将多源异构网络信息分解为处理非结构化内容、情绪与OHLCV数据的模态特定代理，再整合输出为统一证据文档进行置信度校准推理。同时，其分离策略性小时级推理与实时秒级风控模型，实现快速冲击检测与防御干预。实验证明，该框架在真实市场中显著提升交易稳定性、减少虚假交易并增强尾部风险处理能力。
SuperCond-GNN: Scalable Graph Neural Network Surrogate for Superconducting Circuit Simulations
Authors: Nandana Menon, Giorgio Vallone
First: 2026-06-22T16:22:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:22:01+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents SuperCond-GNN, a graph neural network-based surrogate model for predicting the voltage distribution in high-temperature superconducting (HTS) magnets. HTS magnets are modeled as lumped-element equivalent circuits and mapped onto graph representations, enabling message passing GNNs to learn the electrical response as a function of circuit topology, material properties, and operating current. As a proof of concept, tape stacks of up to 10 tapes are considered across a range of circuit topologies and operating conditions. The surrogate is trained on data generated from circuit simulations and achieves a mean MAPE of 4.3 % within the prescribed design space. The predicted nodal voltages enable fast and scalable inference of current redistribution and local operating conditions across a wide range of circuit configurations. The effect of incorporating physics-informed regularization via Kirchhoff's current law is also evaluated, and generalizability to unseen topologies is assessed through zero-shot inference and few-shot fine-tuning. While demonstrated on tape stack circuits, the graph-based framework is topology-agnostic and naturally extensible to more complex HTS cable and magnet configurations, offering a scalable alternative to conventional circuit solvers for downstream applications such as design space exploration, current sharing analysis, and real-time magnet monitoring.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SuperCond-GNN：用于高温超导电路模拟的可扩展图神经网络代理模型
本文提出SuperCond-GNN，一种基于图神经网络的代理模型，用于预测高温超导（HTS）磁体中的电压分布。HTS磁体建模为集总元件等效电路，并映射为图表示，使消息传递GNN能够学习电路拓扑、材料属性和工作电流作为输入时的电响应。作为概念验证，考虑了最多10层带状堆叠结构，涵盖多种电路拓扑和工作条件。该代理模型在电路仿真生成的数据上训练，设计空间内平均MAPE为4.3%。预测的节点电压可快速、可扩展地推断出不同电路配置下的电流重分布和局部工作条件。同时评估了通过基尔霍夫电流定律引入物理信息正则化的效果，并通过零样本推理和少量样本微调评估其对未见拓扑的泛化能力。尽管在带状堆叠电路中演示，该基于图的框架拓扑无关，可自然扩展至更复杂的HTS电缆和磁体配置，为下游应用（如设计空间探索、电流共享分析和实时磁体监测）提供可扩展的替代方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient simulation of high-temperature superconducting magnets, this work introduces SuperCond-GNN, a graph neural network surrogate that models voltage distribution in HTS circuits by mapping lumped-element equivalents into graph structures. The GNN learns voltage responses based on circuit topology, material properties, and operating current, trained on simulated data and achieving a 4.3% mean MAPE. Physics-informed regularization via Kirchhoff’s current law improves accuracy, and the model demonstrates zero-shot generalization to unseen topologies. Its topology-agnostic design enables scalable inference for design exploration, current sharing analysis, and real-time monitoring without relying on computationally expensive circuit solvers.
为提升高温超导磁体模拟效率，本文提出SuperCond-GNN，一种基于图神经网络的代理模型，用于预测电路等效模型中的电压分布。该方法将电路拓扑转化为图结构，使消息传递GNN能够根据电路几何、材料属性和工作电流学习电压响应。在最多10层带状堆叠电路的多种配置下测试，模型在指定设计空间内达到4.3%的平均MAPE，训练数据来自电路仿真。引入基尔霍夫电流定律的物理约束可提升精度，且模型通过零样本和少样本推理验证了对未见拓扑的泛化能力。其拓扑无关架构天然支持扩展至更复杂的高温超导电缆与磁体系统，为设计探索、电流共享分析和实时监控提供高效替代方案。
Environmental Sound Deepfake Detection Using Deep-Learning Framework
Authors: Khoi Vu, Dat Tran, Khanh Do, Phat Lam, Vu Nguyen, Khoa Nguyen, David Fischinger, Tin Nguyen, Ian McLoughlin, Son Le, Lam Pham
First: 2026-04-21T16:41:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:21:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper, we propose a deep-learning framework for Environmental Sound Deepfake Detection (ESDD) - the task of identifying whether the sound scene and sound event in an input audio recording is fake or real. To this end, we first conduct extensive experiments to explore how individual spectrograms, a wide range of network architectures, and pre-trained models affect the performance of an ESDD model. The experimental results on the benchmark datasets of EnvSDD indicate that detecting deepfake audio of sound scenes and detecting deepfake audio of sound events should be considered as individual tasks. We also show that fine-tuning a pre-trained model is more effective than training a model from scratch for ESDD. Ultimately, our best model, which fine-tunes the pre-trained BEATs model using the proposed two-phase training strategy, achieves an Accuracy of 0.98, F1 score of 0.95, and AUC score of 0.99 on the Test subset of the EnvSDD dataset. Our best model also achieves an Accuracy of 0.86, F1 score of 0.80, and AUC of 0.93 when evaluated cross-dataset on the ESD-Challenge-TestSet dataset.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于深度学习框架的环境声音深度伪造检测
本文提出了一种用于环境声音深度伪造检测（ESDD）的深度学习框架，旨在识别输入音频记录中的声音场景和声音事件是否为伪造。为此，我们首先进行大量实验，探讨单个频谱图、多种网络架构及预训练模型对ESDD模型性能的影响。实验结果表明，在EnvSDD基准数据集上，声音场景和声音事件的深度伪造检测应视为独立任务。我们还证明，对预训练模型进行微调比从零开始训练对ESDD更有效。最终，我们采用所提出的两阶段训练策略对预训练的BEATs模型进行微调的最佳模型，在EnvSDD数据集的测试子集上达到准确率0.98、F1分数0.95、AUC分数0.99；在ESD-Challenge-TestSet数据集上的跨数据集评估中，该模型准确率0.86、F1分数0.80、AUC分数0.93。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing need to detect synthetic environmental sounds, this study proposes a deep-learning framework for Environmental Sound Deepfake Detection (ESDD). The authors evaluate various spectrogram types, network architectures, and pre-trained models to identify optimal configurations, finding that scene and event deepfake detection should be treated as separate tasks. They demonstrate that fine-tuning pre-trained models outperforms training from scratch, and their best model—fine-tuned BEATs with a two-phase strategy—achieves 98% accuracy, 95% F1, and 99% AUC on the EnvSDD test set, with 86% accuracy and 80% F1 on a cross-dataset evaluation.
为应对环境声音深度伪造带来的安全威胁，本文提出一种深度学习框架用于区分真实与伪造的环境声音场景和事件。作者系统评估了不同频谱图、网络结构及预训练模型，发现场景与事件伪造应作为独立任务处理。实验表明，微调预训练模型优于从零训练，其最优模型（微调BEATs模型并采用两阶段训练策略）在EnvSDD测试集上达到98%准确率、95%F1值和99%AUC，跨数据集评估（ESD-Challenge-TestSet）下亦达86%准确率、80%F1值和93%AUC。
The Energy Consumption of Transformer Fine-Tuning: A Roofline-Inspired Scaling Model
Authors: Mansour Zoubeirou a Mayaki
First: 2026-06-22T16:18:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:18:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transformer-based models underpin modern natural language processing but incur rapidly growing computational and energy costs. As training scales in both model size and parallelism, accurately predicting energy consumption has become critical for sustainable and cost-aware system design. We present a framework for modeling the energy consumption of Transformer training on multiple GPUs. Using controlled architectural sweeps of BERT models, we relate measured energy to lightweight proxies for compute, memory traffic, and hardware efficiency. Inspired by roofline models, our approach incorporates a speedup-based hardware-efficiency factor that captures the effects of tensor parallelism and fully sharded data parallelism. We derive a scaling law model that accurately predicts training energy across heterogeneous configurations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：变压器微调的能量消耗：受 Roofline 启发的缩放模型
基于变压器的模型支撑现代自然语言处理，但其计算和能耗成本迅速增长。随着模型规模和并行度的扩展，准确预测能耗对可持续和成本敏感的系统设计至关重要。我们提出了一种用于建模在多个 GPU 上训练变压器模型能耗的框架。通过控制 BERT 模型的架构扫描，我们将测量到的能耗与计算、内存流量和硬件效率的轻量代理关联起来。受 Roofline 模型启发，我们的方法引入了基于加速的硬件效率因子，以捕捉张量并行和全分片数据并行的影响。我们推导出一个缩放定律模型，可准确预测异构配置下的训练能耗。

The authors develop a roofline-inspired scaling model that links energy consumption to compute, memory traffic, and hardware efficiency proxies, using controlled BERT experiments across multiple GPUs. Their model incorporates a speedup-based efficiency factor for tensor and fully sharded data parallelism, successfully predicting energy across diverse hardware configurations with high accuracy.
随着Transformer模型规模扩大和训练并行度提升，其能耗激增，亟需准确预测以支持可持续系统设计。作者构建了一个受roofline模型启发的能耗预测框架，通过控制BERT模型架构实验，将实测能耗与计算量、内存流量及硬件效率代理指标关联，并引入基于加速比的硬件效率因子以捕捉张量并行和全数据并行的影响，最终在多种异构配置下实现了对训练能耗的高精度预测。
VeriEvol: Scaling Multimodal Mathematical Reasoning via Verifiable Evol-Instruct
Authors: Haoling Li, Kai Zheng, Jie Wu, Can Xu, Qingfeng Sun, Han Hu, Yujiu Yang
First: 2026-06-22T16:17:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:17:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scaling reinforcement learning for visual mathematical reasoning requires more than generating harder questions: as data volume grows, the reward labels themselves must remain reliable. Yet existing data pipelines scale supervision while trusting the labeller, and policy-side methods assume the underlying answers are already correct. We instead treat scaling as a verifiable data-construction problem and decouple two axes before any policy update: prompt difficulty, expanded by route-specific evolution operators, and answer reliability, enforced by offline hypothesis-test falsification. We instantiate this as VeriEvol, an iterative framework with two extensible components: a type-aware evolution module that rewrites low-difficulty image-question seeds into harder, image-grounded prompts; and HTV-Agent, a verifier that accepts an answer only after multi-source counter-evidence has failed to refute it. The resulting verified data scales in volume, extends by adding evolution routes or verifier channels, and plugs directly into existing GRPO-style RL recipes. On a five-benchmark visual-math suite, scaling evolved SFT data from 10K to 250K samples raises the mean accuracy from 35.42 to 54.73; then, with backbone, SFT initialization, and GRPO recipe held fixed, VeriEvol adds a cumulative +3.88 over an un-evolved RL baseline, of which +1.82 comes from evolved prompts and +2.06 from the HTV-Agent verifier. We release the prompts, data, models, code, and the full verifier trace of every sample, so that downstream work can scale and audit the pipeline rather than only inspect its outputs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VeriEvol：通过可验证的进化指令扩展多模态数学推理
扩展视觉数学推理的强化学习需要的不仅是生成更难的问题：随着数据量增长，奖励标签本身也必须保持可靠。然而现有数据管道在扩展监督时依赖标注者可信，而策略侧方法则假设底层答案已正确。我们相反将扩展视为可验证的数据构建问题，在任何策略更新前解耦两个维度：通过特定路径的进化操作扩展提示难度，通过离线假设检验证伪强制保证答案可靠性。我们将其具体化为VeriEvol，一个包含两个可扩展组件的迭代框架：类型感知的进化模块，将低难度图像-问题种子重写为更难、图像锚定的提示；HTV-Agent验证器，仅在多源反证未能驳斥答案后才接受答案。生成的验证数据可扩展规模，通过添加进化路径或验证通道进行扩展，并可直接嵌入现有GRPO风格的强化学习配方。在五个视觉数学基准测试中，将进化SFT数据从10K扩展至250K样本，平均准确率从35.42提升至54.73；在保持骨干网络、SFT初始化和GRPO配方不变的情况下，VeriEvol相较未经进化的RL基线累计提升+3.88，其中+1.82来自进化提示，+2.06来自HTV-Agent验证器。我们发布提示、数据、模型、代码及每个样本的完整验证轨迹，以便下游工作可扩展并审计整个流程，而不仅限于检查其输出。

Motivated by the need to scale reinforcement learning for visual mathematical reasoning without compromising reward reliability, the paper proposes VeriEvol, a framework that decouples prompt difficulty evolution from answer reliability verification. It uses a type-aware evolution module to generate harder, image-grounded prompts from simple seeds, and an HTV-Agent verifier that accepts answers only after multi-source counter-evidence fails to refute them. Experiments on five benchmarks show that scaling evolved SFT data from 10K to 250K samples boosts mean accuracy from 35.42 to 54.73, and with fixed backbone and RL recipe, VeriEvol adds a cumulative +3.88 over an un-evolved baseline, with +1.82 from evolved prompts and +2.06 from the verifier.
为了解决视觉数学推理中强化学习扩展时奖励标签可靠性下降的问题，该论文提出VeriEvol框架，将提示难度演化与答案可靠性验证解耦。其通过类型感知的演化模块迭代生成更难的图像接地提示，同时HTV-Agent验证器仅在多源反证失败后才接受答案。实验表明，在五个基准上将演化后的监督微调数据从10K扩展至250K，准确率从35.42提升至54.73；在固定骨干网络、SFT初始化和GRPO训练方案下，VeriEvol相比未演化基线累计提升3.88，其中演化提示贡献1.82，验证器贡献2.06。
AwakeForest: An Interactive Geospatial Platform for Large-Scale Forest Imagery
Authors: Suraj Prasai, Kangning Cui, Rongkun Zhu, Sarra Alqahtani, Ying Zhang, Victor Paul Pauca, Miles R. Silman, Fan Yang
First: 2026-06-22T16:17:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:17:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Forest imagery analysis often involves multiple tightly coupled vision tasks, which must be performed under substantial variation in geographic regions, sensors, and acquisition conditions. However, practitioners often lack a unified tool that is geospatial-native, cloud-optimized, and ML-integrated for end-to-end workflows spanning annotation, prediction, visualization, and downstream analysis at scale. We present AwakeForest, an interactive end-to-end platform designed for large-scale forest imagery that integrates model-assisted inference, automatic annotation, and human-in-the-loop refinement within a single workflow. Our platform supports plug-and-play integration of pretrained models and enables scalable interaction with forest imagery ranging from standard aerial scenes to large orthomosaics that can span several gigabytes to hundreds of gigabytes. AwakeForest produces analysis-ready outputs that can be directly used for downstream analysis and to support iterative model and annotation updates on new scenes. We demonstrate the system on the PALMS dataset and illustrate how AwakeForest supports an end-to-end workflow for practical forest management and analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AwakeForest：面向大规模森林影像的交互式地理空间平台
森林影像分析通常涉及多个紧密耦合的视觉任务，需在地理区域、传感器和采集条件变化较大的情况下完成。然而，从业者往往缺乏一个原生地理空间、云优化且集成机器学习的统一工具，以支持从标注、预测、可视化到下游分析的端到端大规模工作流。我们提出AwakeForest，一个专为大规模森林影像设计的交互式端到端平台，将模型辅助推理、自动标注和人机协同优化整合于单一工作流中。该平台支持预训练模型的即插即用，并可扩展交互处理从标准航拍影像到数GB至数百GB的大型正射影像图。AwakeForest生成可直接用于下游分析的分析结果，并支持在新场景上迭代更新模型与标注。我们在PALMS数据集上演示了该系统，展示了AwakeForest如何支持实际森林管理和分析的端到端工作流。

为应对森林影像分析中多任务耦合、地理与传感器条件差异大的挑战，AwakeForest构建了一个集成模型辅助推理、自动标注与人机协作优化的端到端交互平台。该平台支持预训练模型即插即用，可处理从数GB到数百GB的大型航拍与正射影像。在PALMS数据集上的实验表明，该平台能直接输出可用于下游分析的成果，并支持对新场景的模型与标注迭代更新，有效服务于实际森林管理需求。
LightSTAR: Efficient Visual Document Retrieval via Lightweight Selection with Vision-Adaptive Refinement
Authors: Tongkun Guan, Haocheng Wang, Wei Shen, Xiaokang Yang
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-22T16:16:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:16:24+00:00
Comments: Accpeted by ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Visual document retrieval requires rapidly locating relevant pages from large multi-modal corpora in response to user queries. While recent methods powered by Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) show competitive accuracy, they suffer from prohibitive computational costs by applying intensive MLLM encoding to every single page. Meanwhile, we observe that user queries are typically keyword-anchored, containing semantically rich words that are expected to appear directly in the visible text of relevant pages, offering an efficient cue for quickly narrowing down candidate pages. Building on this insight, we propose LightSTAR, an efficient framework that decomposes visual document retrieval into: 1) LLM-free Visual Selection, which utilizes content-grounded query encoding to focus on informative words and employs LLM-free visual embeddings to produce a high-recall candidate set; and 2) Vision-adaptive Semantic Refinement, which further performs fine-grained semantic matching exclusively on these top candidates via adaptive region-wise feature fusion to effectively combine textual and layout cues, optimized through a hardness-aware contrastive objective. Experimental results demonstrate that LightSTAR achieves state-of-the-art retrieval accuracy while reducing end-to-end latency by several-fold, offering a highly practical solution to the accuracy-efficiency trade-off in visual document retrieval. Code is available at https://github.com/bokufa/LightSTAR.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LightSTAR：通过轻量级选择与视觉自适应精炼实现高效的视觉文档检索
视觉文档检索需要在大型多模态语料库中快速定位与用户查询相关的页面。尽管基于多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的最新方法在准确性上表现优异，但它们因对每一页都应用密集的MLLM编码而导致计算成本过高。同时，我们观察到用户查询通常以关键词为中心，包含语义丰富的词汇，这些词汇预期会直接出现在相关页面的可见文本中，从而提供快速缩小候选页面范围的有效线索。基于这一洞察，我们提出LightSTAR框架，将视觉文档检索分解为：1）无LLM视觉选择，利用内容锚定的查询编码聚焦于信息性词汇，并采用无LLM视觉嵌入生成高召回率候选集；2）视觉自适应语义精炼，通过自适应区域特征融合，仅在上述顶级候选页面上进行细粒度语义匹配，有效结合文本与布局线索，并通过感知难度的对比学习目标进行优化。实验结果表明，LightSTAR在保持最先进检索精度的同时，将端到端延迟降低数倍，为视觉文档检索中的精度-效率权衡提供了高度实用的解决方案。代码见https://github.com/bokufa/LightSTAR。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of existing MLLM-based visual document retrieval systems, this work proposes LightSTAR to improve efficiency without sacrificing accuracy. It decomposes retrieval into two stages: first, an LLM-free Visual Selection module uses content-grounded query encoding and lightweight visual embeddings to generate a high-recall candidate set by leveraging keyword anchors in user queries; second, a Vision-adaptive Semantic Refinement module performs fine-grained matching on top candidates using region-wise feature fusion and a hardness-aware contrastive loss to integrate text and layout cues. Experiments show LightSTAR achieves state-of-the-art accuracy while reducing end-to-end latency by several-fold, effectively resolving the accuracy-efficiency trade-off.
为解决现有基于多模态大语言模型的视觉文档检索系统计算成本过高的问题，LightSTAR提出两阶段高效框架：首先，通过内容引导的查询编码与无LLM视觉嵌入生成高召回候选集；其次，对候选集进行视觉自适应语义精炼，通过区域级特征融合与硬度感知对比损失优化图文匹配。实验表明，LightSTAR在保持最先进精度的同时，端到端延迟降低数倍，有效平衡了效率与性能。
LatentCRS: A Variational EM Framework for Bridging Semantics and Behavior in LLM-based Conversational Recommendation
Authors: Guanrong Li, Kuo Tian, Jinnan Qi, Qinghan Fu, Zhen Wu, Rui Xia, Xinyu Dai
First: 2025-03-12T09:01:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T16:15:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Conversational Recommender Systems (CRS) powered by Large Language Models (LLMs) enable users to articulate explicit and dynamic preferences, overcoming the limitations of fixed templates. However, despite their superior semantic proficiency, LLMs have not yet achieved corresponding improvements in recommendation accuracy. This discrepancy arises from a fundamental representation gap: while LLMs operate within a semantic space, they lack the behavioral grounding needed to encode user behavioral patterns, such as item co-occurrences, which are crucial for accurate recommendations. To address this, we propose a model-agnostic Variational EM Framework for Bridging Semantics and Behavior in LLM-based Conversational Recommendation (LatentCRS). Based on the observation that dialogue and interactions reflect the same latent intent, LatentCRS uses a variational expectation-maximization (EM) procedure, where user intent connects semantic representations with behavioral patterns. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that LatentCRS effectively bridges the representation gap and outperforms baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LatentCRS：一种用于弥合基于大语言模型的对话推荐系统中语义与行为差距的变分EM框架
基于大语言模型（LLMs）的对话推荐系统（CRS）使用户能够表达明确且动态的偏好，克服了固定模板的局限性。然而，尽管LLMs在语义能力上表现优异，其推荐准确性尚未相应提升。这一差距源于根本性的表征鸿沟：LLMs虽在语义空间中运作，却缺乏编码用户行为模式（如物品共现）所需的行为基础，而这些模式对准确推荐至关重要。为解决此问题，我们提出了一种模型无关的变分EM框架——LatentCRS，用于弥合基于LLM的对话推荐系统中的语义与行为差距。基于对话与交互反映相同潜在意图的观察，LatentCRS采用变分期望最大化（EM）过程，通过用户意图将语义表征与行为模式连接起来。在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，LatentCRS有效弥合了表征鸿沟，并优于基线方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between LLMs' strong semantic understanding and their poor performance in recommendation accuracy due to lack of behavioral grounding, LatentCRS introduces a model-agnostic variational EM framework that aligns semantic representations from dialogue with latent behavioral patterns such as item co-occurrences. By treating dialogue and interaction as reflections of the same latent intent, the framework uses variational expectation-maximization to jointly optimize semantic and behavioral representations. Experimental results on real-world datasets show that LatentCRS effectively bridges this representation gap and achieves superior recommendation performance compared to existing baselines.
为解决大语言模型虽擅长语义理解但推荐效果不佳的问题，本文提出LatentCRS，一种无模型依赖的变分EM框架，用于弥合语义与行为表征之间的鸿沟。该框架基于对话与交互反映同一潜在意图的观察，通过变分期望最大化过程联合优化语义表示与行为模式（如物品共现）。在真实数据集上的实验表明，LatentCRS有效弥合了语义与行为表征差距，推荐效果显著优于现有基线方法。
